/** Базовый URL картинки (склейка). */
export function imgUrl(file) {
  if (!file) return null;
  return IMAGE_BASE + file;
}
/** Портрет полководца с fallback на общий портрет фракции. */
export function generalPortraitUrl(factionId, index = 0) {
  const list = GENERAL_PRESET_PORTRAITS[factionId];
  if (list && list[index]) {
    return imgUrl(list[index]);
  }
  return imgUrl(GENERAL_PORTRAITS[factionId]);
}
/** Герб фракции. */
export function factionEmblemUrl(factionId) {
  return imgUrl(FACTION_EMBLEMS[factionId]);
}
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  3D-МОДЕЛИ ГЕКСОВ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
export const MODEL_BASE = '/models/';
/**
 * Списки моделей по типу террейна.
 * На каждый гекс выбирается ОДНА случайная модель из массива.
 * Имена файлов — относительно MODEL_BASE (public/models/).
 */
export const TERRAIN_MODELS = {
  mountain: ['mounts.glb', 'mounts_1.glb', 'mounts_2.glb', 'mounts_3.glb', 'mounts_4.glb'],
  forest: ['forest.glb', 'forest_1.glb', 'forest_2.glb', 'forest_3.glb', 'forest_4.glb'],
};
/**
 * Настройки отображения моделей по типу террейна.
 * Общие для всех вариантов одного террейна.
 */
export const TERRAIN_MODEL_SETTINGS = {
  mountain: {
    scale: 1.45, offsetY: -10, offsetX: 0, offsetZ: 0,
    rotationY: 0,
    rotationYRandom: false,
    castShadow: true, receiveShadow: true, fitToHex: true,
  },
  forest: {
    scale: 1.45, offsetY: -7, offsetX: 0, offsetZ: 0,
    rotationY: 0,
    rotationYRandom: false,
    castShadow: true, receiveShadow: true, fitToHex: true,
  },
};
/**
 * URL всех моделей для террейна (для прогрева кэша).
 * Возвращает массив или пустой массив.
 */
export function terrainModelUrls(terrain) {
  const list = TERRAIN_MODELS[terrain];
  if (!list || list.length === 0) return [];
  return list.map((file) => MODEL_BASE + file);
}
/**
 * URL модели для террейна с учётом seed.
 * Один и тот же seed + terrain → одна и та же модель
 * (детерминизм важен: при перестройке сцены модель не «прыгает»).
 *
 * seed обычно = hash(tile.key), чтобы у каждого гекса была своя модель.
 */
export function terrainModelUrl(terrain, seed = 0) {
  const list = TERRAIN_MODELS[terrain];
  if (!list || list.length === 0) return null;
  const idx = Math.abs(seed) % list.length;
  return MODEL_BASE + list[idx];
}
/** Настройки модели для террейна или null. */
export function terrainModelSettings(terrain) {
  return TERRAIN_MODEL_SETTINGS[terrain] || null;
}
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  3D-МОДЕЛИ ГЕНЕРАЛОВ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export const GENERAL_MODEL_COUNT = 5;
export const GENERAL_MODEL_BASE = '/models/';
/**
 * URL модели генерала по presetIndex (0..4).
 * Если модели нет — берётся 1-я как fallback (см. GeneralVisual).
 *
 * presetIndex 0 → player_general_1.glb
 * presetIndex 1 → player_general_2.glb
 * ...
 * presetIndex 4 → player_general_5.glb
 */
export function generalModelUrl(presetIndex = 0) {
  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(GENERAL_MODEL_COUNT - 1, presetIndex | 0)) + 1;
  return `${GENERAL_MODEL_BASE}player_general_${idx}.glb`;
}
/** URL дефолтной (первой) модели — для fallback. */
export function generalModelFallbackUrl() {
  return `${GENERAL_MODEL_BASE}player_general_1.glb`;
}
/** Все URL моделей — для прогрева кэша. */
export function generalModelUrls() {
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 1; i <= GENERAL_MODEL_COUNT; i++) {
    out.push(`${GENERAL_MODEL_BASE}player_general_${i}.glb`);
  }
  return out;
}

import { Game } from './core/Game.js';
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
  const uiRoot = document.getElementById('ui-root');
  const toastRoot = document.getElementById('toast-root');
  try {
    const game = new Game({ canvas, uiRoot, toastRoot });
    game.input.attachCameraController(game.cameraController);
    game.start('scythians');
    window.__game = game; // для отладки
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    document.body.innerHTML = `<pre style="color:#f88;padding:20px">Ошибка: ${err.message}</pre>`;
  }
});

📄 application\BattleController.js

import { BattleAI } from '../domain/battle/BattleAI.js';
export class BattleController {
  constructor({ world, bus }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.bus = bus;
    this.battle = null;
    this._aiScheduled = false;
    this._aiBusy = false;
    this.activeSide = null;
    this.humanSides = { attacker: false, defender: false };
  }
  open({ attackerGen, targetTile, defenderFactionId, playerSide = 'attacker', humanSides = null }) {
    const battle = this.world.startBattle({
      attackerGen, targetTile, defenderFactionId, playerSide,
    });
    this.battle = battle;
    battle._aiInstance = new BattleAI(battle);
    if (humanSides) {
      this.humanSides = humanSides;
    } else {
      const playerFaction = this.world.playerFactionId;
      this.humanSides = {
        attacker: battle.attackerGen?.factionId === playerFaction,
        defender: battle.defenderGen?.factionId === playerFaction,
      };
    }
    battle.humanSides = this.humanSides;
    if (this.humanSides.attacker) {
      this.activeSide = 'attacker';
    } else if (this.humanSides.defender) {
      this.activeSide = 'defender';
    } else {
      this.activeSide = 'attacker';
    }
    battle.currentHumanSide = this.activeSide;
    this.bus.emit('battle:started', { battle });
    this._scheduleIfNeeded();
    return battle;
  }
  _setTurn(side, isHuman) {
    this.activeSide = side;
    if (isHuman) this.battle.currentHumanSide = side;
  }
  _scheduleIfNeeded() {
    if (!this.battle || this.battle.finished) return;
    const turn = this.battle.turn;
    const isHuman = this.humanSides[turn] === true;
    this._setTurn(turn, isHuman);
    this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: this.battle });
    if (!isHuman) this._scheduleAI();
  }
  selectCard(card) {
    if (!this.battle) return;
    if (!this.battle.isActiveTurn) return;
    this.battle.selectCard(card);
    this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: this.battle });
  }
  playCard(card, lane, index) {
    if (!this.battle) return false;
    if (!this.battle.isActiveTurn) return false;
    const ok = this.battle.playCard(card, lane, index);
    if (ok) {
      this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: this.battle });
      if (this.battle.finished) return true;
    }
    return ok;
  }
  endTurn() {
    if (!this.battle) return;
    if (!this.battle.isActiveTurn) return;
    this.battle.endPlayerTurn();
    this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: this.battle });
    if (this.battle.finished) return;
    this._scheduleIfNeeded();
  }
  skipTurn() {
    if (!this.battle) return;
    if (!this.battle.isActiveTurn) return;
    this.battle.skipTurn();
    this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: this.battle });
    if (this.battle.finished) return;
    this._scheduleIfNeeded();
  }
  /**
   * Пошаговый ИИ.
   * Каждый такт — 1 карта или завершение хода.
   * Задержка 800–1200 мс, чтобы игрок видел процесс.
   */
  _scheduleAI() {
    if (!this.battle) return;
    if (this.battle.finished) return;
    if (this._aiScheduled) return;
    this._aiScheduled = true;
    this._aiBusy = false;
    const startDelay = 700 + Math.random() * 300;   // 700–1000 мс перед первым тактом
    setTimeout(() => {
      this._aiScheduled = false;
      this._runAIStep();
    }, startDelay);
  }
  _runAIStep() {
    if (!this.battle || this.battle.finished) return;
    if (this.humanSides[this.battle.turn] === true) {
      this._scheduleIfNeeded();
      return;
    }
    const b = this.battle;
    const aiSide = b.turn;
    b.currentHumanSide = aiSide;
    let played = false;
    try {
      played = b._aiInstance?._stepSingle
        ? b._aiInstance._stepSingle()
        : this._fallbackAIStep(aiSide);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[AI step]', e);
      played = false;
    }
    this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: b });
    if (b.finished) return;
    if (played) {
      // Ещё есть чем ходить — следующий такт через 800–1200 мс
      this._aiScheduled = true;
      const nextDelay = 800 + Math.random() * 400;
      setTimeout(() => {
        this._aiScheduled = false;
        this._runAIStep();
      }, nextDelay);
    } else {
      // Ход закончен — пауза и завершение
      this._aiScheduled = true;
      setTimeout(() => {
        this._aiScheduled = false;
        if (!this.battle || this.battle.finished) return;
        try {
          this.battle.currentHumanSide = this.battle.turn;
          this.battle._endTurn(this.battle.turn);
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('[AI endTurn]', e);
        }
        this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: this.battle });
        if (!this.battle.finished) this._scheduleIfNeeded();
      }, 600);
    }
  }
  _fallbackAIStep(side) {
    const b = this.battle;
    const d = b[side];
    const priorities = [
      ['support', 'support'],
      ['warrior', 'defense'],
      ['archer', 'attack'],
      ['special', 'attack'],
    ];
    for (const [type, lane] of priorities) {
      const card = d.hand.find((c) => c.type === type && b.costFor(c, side) <= d.tacticPoints);
      if (!card) continue;
      const cost = b.costFor(card, side);
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        if (d.board.isEmpty(lane, i)) {
          d.tacticPoints -= cost;
          d.board.place(lane, i, card);
          d.hand = d.hand.filter((c) => c.id !== card.id);
          b._log(`Враг: ${card.name} → ${lane} (-${cost}⚡)`);
          b._emit('card:played', { card, side, lane, index: i });
          b._checkLineBonus();
          b._checkColumnBonus();
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  async autoResolve({ attackerGen, targetTile, defenderFactionId, playerSide = 'attacker' }) {
    const battle = this.world.startBattle({
      attackerGen, targetTile, defenderFactionId, playerSide,
    });
    battle._aiInstance = new BattleAI(battle);
    const oldHuman = this.humanSides;
    this.humanSides = { attacker: false, defender: false };
    battle.humanSides = this.humanSides;
    this.activeSide = null;
    battle.currentHumanSide = playerSide;
    await battle.autoPlay(null, 0);
    const result = {
      winner: battle.winner,
      attackerLives: battle.attacker.lives,
      defenderLives: battle.defender.lives,
      targetTile: battle.targetTile,
      attackerGen: battle.attackerGen,
      defenderGen: battle.defenderGen,
      playerSide: battle.playerSide,
      defenderFactionId: battle.defenderFaction,
      activeSide: playerSide,
      humanSides: { attacker: false, defender: false },
      auto: true,
    };
    this.bus.emit('battle:finished', result);
    this.humanSides = oldHuman;
    return result;
  }
  finish() {
    if (!this.battle) return;
    const b = this.battle;
    const result = {
      winner: b.winner,
      attackerLives: b.attacker.lives,
      defenderLives: b.defender.lives,
      targetTile: b.targetTile,
      attackerGen: b.attackerGen,
      defenderGen: b.defenderGen,
      playerSide: b.playerSide,
      activeSide: this.activeSide,
      humanSides: { ...this.humanSides },
      defenderFactionId: b.defenderFaction,
    };
    this.battle = null;
    this.activeSide = null;
    this.humanSides = { attacker: false, defender: false };
    this._aiScheduled = false;
    this._aiBusy = false;
    this.bus.emit('battle:finished', result);
  }
  retreat() {
    if (!this.battle) return;
    if (!this.battle.isActiveTurn) return;
    this.battle.finished = true;
    this.battle.winner = this.activeSide === 'attacker' ? 'defender' : 'attacker';
    this.battle._log('Отступление');
    this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: this.battle });
  }
}

import { ConfirmDialog } from '../ui/components/ConfirmDialog.js';
import { Theme } from '../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Оркестрация боёв: открытие, завершение, победы/поражения.
 * Знает о BattleController и world, но не о Game.
 */
export class BattleOrchestrator {
    constructor({ world, bus, battleController, sceneBuilder, cameraController, input, ui, sound, profile, humanFactions, pendingBattle, sceneSync }) {
        this.world = world;
        this.bus = bus;
        this.battleController = battleController;
        this.sceneBuilder = sceneBuilder;
        this.cameraController = cameraController;
        this.input = input;
        this.ui = ui;
        this.sound = sound;
        this.profile = profile;
        this.humanFactions = humanFactions;
        this.pendingBattle = pendingBattle;   // { cost: 0, defenderFactionId: null }
        this.sceneSync = sceneSync;
        this.onMatchFinished = null;          // колбэк от Game
        this.onAfterAITurns = null;           // колбэк от Game
    }
    /**
     * Подтверждение атаки на генерала.
     */
    confirmAttack(general, tile, cost, onConfirm) {
        const enemy = this.world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
        const enemyName = enemy?.name || 'Вражеский полководец';
        let defenderFactionId = enemy?.factionId;
        if (!defenderFactionId && tile.cityId) {
            const city = this.world.getCity(tile.cityId);
            defenderFactionId = city?.settlement
                ? (city.neutralFaction || 'tauri')
                : (city?.factionId || null);
        }
        if (!defenderFactionId) defenderFactionId = tile.factionId || null;
        new ConfirmDialog(document.getElementById('ui-root'), {
            title: 'Начать бой?',
            text: `${general.name} атакует ${enemyName}. Потрачено ${cost} очка движения.`,
            confirmText: 'Атаковать', cancelText: 'Отмена',
            onConfirm: () => onConfirm(defenderFactionId),
            onCancel: () => { },
        });
    }
    confirmSettlementAttack(general, tile, city, cost, onConfirm) {
        const defenderName = city.neutralFaction === 'cimmerians' ? 'Киммерийцы' : 'Тавры';
        new ConfirmDialog(document.getElementById('ui-root'), {
            title: 'Напасть на поселение?',
            text: `${general.name} атакует ${city.name} (${defenderName}). Генерал-защитник будет обороняться. Потрачено ${cost} очка движения.`,
            confirmText: 'Напасть', cancelText: 'Отмена',
            onConfirm: () => onConfirm(city.neutralFaction || 'tauri'),
            onCancel: () => { },
        });
    }
    executeBattle(general, tile, cost, defenderFactionOverride, selection) {
        this.pendingBattle.cost = cost ?? 1;
        let defenderFactionId = defenderFactionOverride;
        if (!defenderFactionId) {
            const enemyGen = this.world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
            if (enemyGen) {
                defenderFactionId = enemyGen.factionId;
            } else if (tile.cityId) {
                const city = this.world.getCity(tile.cityId);
                defenderFactionId = city?.settlement
                    ? (city.neutralFaction || 'tauri')
                    : (city?.factionId || null);
            } else {
                defenderFactionId = tile.factionId || null;
            }
        }
        if (!defenderFactionId) {
            this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Некого атаковать' });
            return;
        }
        this.pendingBattle.defenderFactionId = defenderFactionId;
        this.sceneBuilder.setMovementOverlays(null, false);
        this.sceneBuilder.setAttackOverlays(null, false);
        this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralSelected(general.id, false);
        selection.selectedGeneralId = null;
        this.bus.emit('general:selected', { general: null });
        selection.updateHireButton();
        const cfg = this.profile.matchConfig || { mode: 'ai' };
        const humans = this.humanFactions || [];
        const attackerHuman = humans.includes(general.factionId);
        const defenderHuman = cfg.mode === 'hotseat' ? humans.includes(defenderFactionId) : false;
        const playerSide = attackerHuman ? 'attacker' : 'defender';
        this.battleController.open({
            attackerGen: general, targetTile: tile, defenderFactionId, playerSide,
            humanSides: { attacker: attackerHuman, defender: defenderHuman },
        });
    }
    onBattleFinished(result) {
        const { winner, targetTile, attackerGen, defenderGen, playerSide, activeSide, humanSides } = result;
        const cost = this.pendingBattle.cost || 0;
        this.pendingBattle.cost = 0;
        this.pendingBattle.defenderFactionId = null;
        const side = activeSide || playerSide || 'attacker';
        const isHumanFight = humanSides?.attacker || humanSides?.defender;
        let playerWon = false;
        if (isHumanFight) {
            playerWon = (side === 'attacker' && winner === 'attacker') || (side === 'defender' && winner === 'defender');
        } else {
            playerWon = (playerSide === 'attacker' && winner === 'attacker') || (playerSide === 'defender' && winner === 'defender');
        }
        const playerGen = side === 'attacker' ? attackerGen : defenderGen;
        if (playerGen && playerWon) {
            const events = playerGen.addXp(10);
            for (const ev of events) {
                if (ev.kind === 'level') {
                    this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `⭐ ${playerGen.name}: уровень ${ev.level}!` });
                    this.sound.play('victory');
                } else if (ev.kind === 'perk') {
                    this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `🏅 Новый перк: ${ev.perkName}` });
                }
            }
            this.bus.emit('general:selected', { general: playerGen });
        }
        if (playerWon) this.sound.play('victory');
        else if (winner !== 'draw') this.sound.play('defeat');
        if (winner === 'attacker') this.applyAttackerVictory(attackerGen, defenderGen, targetTile, cost);
        else if (winner === 'defender') this.applyDefenderVictory(attackerGen, defenderGen, targetTile, cost);
        if (winner !== 'draw') this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: playerWon ? 'Победа!' : 'Поражение' });
        else this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Ничья' });
        const connEvents = this.world.connectivity.enforceForAll();
        for (const ev of connEvents) {
            if (ev.text) this.bus.emit('notification:show', {
                text: ev.text,
                kind: ev.type === 'city' ? 'danger' : 'info',
            });
        }
        this.world._checkElimination();
        if (this.world.winner) this.onMatchFinished?.();
        this.world.recomputeVisibility();
        this.sceneSync.run(this.world);
        this.onAfterAITurns?.();
    }
    applyAttackerVictory(attackerGen, defenderGen, targetTile, cost) {
        if (!attackerGen || !targetTile) return;
        const enemyGenOnTile = this.world.getGeneralAt(targetTile.hex);
        if (enemyGenOnTile && enemyGenOnTile.id !== attackerGen.id
            && enemyGenOnTile.factionId !== attackerGen.factionId) {
            enemyGenOnTile.lives = 0;
        }
        if (defenderGen && defenderGen.id !== attackerGen.id
            && defenderGen.factionId !== attackerGen.factionId) {
            defenderGen.lives = 0;
        }
        const capturedCity = targetTile.cityId ? this.world.getCity(targetTile.cityId) : null;
        if (capturedCity) {
            if (capturedCity.factionId !== attackerGen.factionId) {
                this.world.captureCity(capturedCity.id, attackerGen.factionId);
                this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `🏛 Захвачен ${capturedCity.name}` });
            }
        } else {
            targetTile.setFaction(attackerGen.factionId);
        }
        attackerGen.moveTo(targetTile.hex);
        if (cost > 0) attackerGen.spendMove(cost);
    }
    applyDefenderVictory(attackerGen, defenderGen, targetTile, cost) {
        if (!attackerGen) return;
        attackerGen.lives = Math.max(0, attackerGen.lives - 1);
        if (cost > 0) attackerGen.spendMove(cost);
        // targetTile не трогаем
    }
}

import { ConfirmDialog } from '../ui/components/ConfirmDialog.js';
/**
 * Клики по городам и покупка поселений.
 */
export class CityInteractionService {
  constructor({ world, bus, battleOrchestrator, selection, ui, sceneBuilder, cameraController, input, sound, sceneSync }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.bus = bus;
    this.battleOrchestrator = battleOrchestrator;
    this.selection = selection;
    this.ui = ui;
    this.sceneBuilder = sceneBuilder;
    this.cameraController = cameraController;
    this.input = input;
    this.sound = sound;
    this.sceneSync = sceneSync;
  }
  handleCityClick(tile, city) {
    const playerFactionId = this.world.playerFactionId;
    const isMine = city.factionId === playerFactionId;
    if (isMine) {
      this.openCity(tile, city);
      this.selection.selectGeneral(null);
      return;
    }
    const general = this.selection.getSelectedGeneral();
    if (general && this.world.movement.canAttack(general, tile)) {
      const cost = this.world.movement.attackCostTo(general, tile);
      const defenderFactionId = city.settlement
        ? (city.neutralFaction || 'tauri')
        : (city.factionId || 'goths');
      const kind = city.settlement ? 'поселение' : city.isCity ? 'город' : 'деревню';
      const canBuy = city.settlement;
      new ConfirmDialog(document.getElementById('ui-root'), {
        title: `Атаковать ${kind}?`,
        text: `${general.name} может атаковать ${city.name}. Потратится ${cost} очка движения.` +
              (canBuy ? ' Или купить за 40 💰 (только для поселения).' : ''),
        confirmText: 'Атаковать',
        cancelText: canBuy ? 'Купить…' : 'Отмена',
        onConfirm: () => this.battleOrchestrator.executeBattle(
          general, tile, cost, defenderFactionId, this.selection
        ),
        onCancel: () => {
          if (canBuy) {
            new ConfirmDialog(document.getElementById('ui-root'), {
              title: `Купить ${city.name}?`,
              text: `Купить поселение за 40 золота? Генерал-защитник исчезнет.`,
              confirmText: 'Купить', cancelText: 'Отмена',
              onConfirm: () => this.buySettlement(city.id),
              onCancel: () => {},
            });
          }
        },
      });
      return;
    }
    this.openCity(tile, city);
    this.selection.selectGeneral(null);
  }
  attackCityById(cityId, defenderFactionId) {
    const city = this.world.getCity(cityId);
    if (!city || !city.tileKey) return;
    const tile = this.world.map.getByKey(city.tileKey);
    if (!tile) return;
    const general = this.selection.getSelectedGeneral();
    if (!general) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Сначала выберите полководца' });
      return;
    }
    if (!this.world.movement.canAttack(general, tile)) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Слишком далеко' });
      return;
    }
    const cost = this.world.movement.attackCostTo(general, tile);
    this.battleOrchestrator.executeBattle(general, tile, cost, defenderFactionId, this.selection);
  }
  openCity(tile, city) {
    if (this.world) this.world.selectedGeneralId = this.selection.selectedGeneralId;
    this.ui.openCityPanel({ tile, city, world: this.world });
  }
  buySettlement(cityId) {
    if (!this.world) return;
    const city = this.world.getCity(cityId);
    if (!city || !city.settlement) return;
    const res = this.world.buySettlement(cityId, this.world.playerFactionId, 40);
    if (!res.ok) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: res.reason });
      return;
    }
    this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `🏛 Куплено: ${city.name} (-40💰)` });
    this.world.recomputeVisibility();
    this.sceneSync.run(this.world);
  }
}

export class MarketController {
    constructor({ world, bus }) {
      this.world = world;
      this.bus = bus;
    }
    tryBuy({ offerId }) {
      const factionId = this.world.playerFactionId;
      const result = this.world.market.buyOffer({
        offerId,
        buyerFactionId: factionId,
      });
      if (!result.ok) {
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: result.reason });
        return false;
      }
      this.bus.emit('resources:changed', { factionId });
      this.bus.emit('market:changed', {});
      const goodsLabel = result.goods === 'weapons' ? 'оружия' : 'провизии';
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', {
        text: `Куплено ${result.amount} ${goodsLabel} (-${result.price}💰)`,
      });
      return true;
    }
  }

📄 application\MovementController.js

import { Theme } from '../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Пошаговое движение генерала: BFS-путь + анимация.
 * Не эмитит события напрямую — их эмитит Game после вызова.
 */
export class MovementController {
  constructor({ world, sceneBuilder, cameraController, input, ui, bus, sceneSync }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.sceneBuilder = sceneBuilder;
    this.cameraController = cameraController;
    this.input = input;
    this.ui = ui;
    this.bus = bus;
    this.sceneSync = sceneSync;
    this._moving = false;
  }
  get isMoving() { return this._moving; }
  /**
   * Выполнить движение. Возвращает Promise<boolean> — дошёл ли до цели.
   * onComplete(general, capturedAny) — колбэк после движения.
   */
  async executeMove(general, targetTile, { onComplete } = {}) {
    if (!this.world || !general || !targetTile) return false;
    if (this._moving) return false;
    this._moving = true;
    const factionId = general.factionId;
    this.sceneBuilder.setMovementOverlays(null, false);
    this.sceneBuilder.setAttackOverlays(null, false);
    this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralSelected(general.id, false);
    const path = this.findPath(general, targetTile);
    if (!path || path.length === 0) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Нет пути' });
      this._moving = false;
      onComplete?.(general, false);
      return false;
    }
    let capturedAny = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < path.length; i++) {
      const step = path[i];
      const tile = step.tile;
      const cost = step.cost;
      if (general.movePoints < cost) {
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Не хватает очков движения' });
        this.bus.emit('general:no-move-points', {
          general, needed: cost, have: general.movePoints,
        });
        break;
      }
      general.spendMove(cost);
      const fromHex = general.hex;
      general.moveTo(tile.hex);
      this.sceneBuilder.updateGeneralBars?.(this.world);   // ← новая строка
      const captured = this.world.captureTile(tile, factionId);
      if (captured) capturedAny = true;
      this.animateGeneralMove(general, fromHex, tile.hex);
      if (captured) {
        const v = this.sceneBuilder._tileVisuals.get(tile.key);
        if (v && typeof v.setFactionColor === 'function') {
          v.setFactionColor(factionId);
          v.setFactionRingVisible(true);
        }
        this.world.recomputeVisibility();
        this.sceneBuilder.updateFog(this.world);
        this.sceneBuilder.refreshBorders();
        this.bus.emit('tile:captured', { tile, factionId, by: general.id });
      }
      await this._sleep(280);
    }
    // ─── Финальная синхронизация: движение не меняет ресурсы и владельцев
    // городов, поэтому resources: false и rebuild: false ───
    this.sceneBuilder.updateGeneralPositions(this.world);
    this.world.recomputeVisibility();
    this.sceneSync.run(this.world, { rebuild: false, resources: false });
    this.bus.emit('general:moved', { general });
    this._moving = false;
    onComplete?.(general, capturedAny);
    return true;
  }
  findPath(general, targetTile) {
    const start = general.hex;
    if (start.key === targetTile.key) return [];
    const queue = [{ hex: start, prev: null }];
    const visited = new Set([start.key]);
    let goalNode = null;
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const cur = queue.shift();
      for (const n of cur.hex.neighbors()) {
        if (visited.has(n.key)) continue;
        const t = this.world.map.get(n.q, n.r);
        if (!t || !t.isLand) continue;
        const occ = this.world.getGeneralAt(n);
        if (occ && occ.id !== general.id) continue;
        if (this.world.movement.isForeignCityTile(t, general.factionId)) continue;
        visited.add(n.key);
        const node = { hex: n, prev: cur, tile: t };
        if (n.key === targetTile.key) {
          goalNode = node;
          break;
        }
        queue.push(node);
      }
      if (goalNode) break;
    }
    if (!goalNode) return null;
    const path = [];
    let node = goalNode;
    while (node && node.hex.key !== start.key) {
      path.unshift({
        tile: node.tile,
        cost: this.world.movement.tileCost(node.tile),
      });
      node = node.prev;
    }
    return path;
  }
  animateGeneralMove(general, fromHex, toHex) {
    const visual = this.sceneBuilder._generalVisuals.get(general.id);
    if (!visual) return;
    const from = fromHex.toWorld(Theme.hexSize);
    const to = toHex.toWorld(Theme.hexSize);
    const dx = to.x - from.x;
    const dz = to.z - from.z;
    const angle = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
    const startX = from.x;
    const startZ = from.z;
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 260;
    const tick = (now) => {
      const t = Math.min(1, (now - startTime) / duration);
      const ease = t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 2) / 2;
      const x = startX + dx * ease;
      const z = startZ + dz * ease;
      visual.group.position.set(x, 0, z);
      visual.group.rotation.y = angle;
      if (t < 1) {
        requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      } else {
        visual.group.position.set(to.x, 0, to.z);
        visual.group.rotation.y = angle;
      }
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  _sleep(ms) {
    return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
  }
}

/**
 * Оркестрация найма. Связывает UI и домен через EventBus.
 */
export class RecruitController {
    constructor({ world, bus }) {
        this.world = world;
        this.bus = bus;
    }
    tryRecruit(cardKey) {
        const factionId = this.world.playerFactionId;
        if (!factionId) return;
        const result = this.world.recruit.recruit(factionId, cardKey);
        if (!result.ok) {
            this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: result.reason });
            return;
        }
        this.bus.emit('resources:changed', { factionId });
        this.bus.emit('deck:changed', { general: this.world.playerGeneral });
        this.bus.emit('card:recruited', { card: result.card });
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', {
            text: `${result.card.name} нанят (-${result.cost.gold}💰)`,
        });
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 application\SceneSync.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Единая точка синхронизации сцены после изменения мира.
 * Заменяет повторяющийся набор rebuild → updateFog → updateCityFaction
 * → refreshBorders → setPickables → resources:changed → refreshMinimap.
 */
 export class SceneSync {
  constructor({ sceneBuilder, input, ui, bus }) {
    this.sceneBuilder = sceneBuilder;
    this.input = input;
    this.ui = ui;
    this.bus = bus;
  }
  run(world, { rebuild = true, resources = true, factionId = null } = {}) {
    if (!world) return;
    if (rebuild) this.sceneBuilder.rebuild(world);
    this.sceneBuilder.updateGeneralBars?.(world);
    this.sceneBuilder.updateFog(world);
    this.sceneBuilder.updateCityFaction(world);
    this.sceneBuilder.refreshBorders();
    this.input.setPickables(this.sceneBuilder.getPickables());
    if (resources) {
      this.bus.emit('resources:changed', {
        factionId: factionId || world.playerFactionId,
      });
    }
    this.ui.quickJump?.refreshMinimap();
  }
}

📄 application\SelectionController.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { Theme } from '../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Отвечает за выбор генерала, гекса и обновление кнопки «Найм».
 * Не трогает сцену напрямую — только через sceneBuilder.
 */
export class SelectionController {
  constructor({ world, bus, sceneBuilder, cameraController, ui }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.bus = bus;
    this.sceneBuilder = sceneBuilder;
    this.cameraController = cameraController;
    this.ui = ui;
    this.selectedGeneralId = null;
    this.selectedTileKey = null;
    this._lastSelectedId = null; // помним, кем играли последним
  }
  selectGeneral(general) {
    if (general && general.factionId !== this.world.playerFactionId) general = null;
    if (this.selectedGeneralId) this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralSelected(this.selectedGeneralId, false);
    this.selectedGeneralId = general ? general.id : null;
    this.selectedTileKey = null;
    this.sceneBuilder.highlight.setSelected(null);
    if (general) {
      this._lastSelectedId = general.id;
      this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralSelected(general.id, true);
      this.cameraController.centerOn(general.hex, Theme.hexSize);
      const moves = this.world.movement.reachableForMove(general);
      const attacks = this.world.movement.reachableForAttack(general);
      this.sceneBuilder.setMovementOverlays(moves, true);
      this.sceneBuilder.setAttackOverlays(attacks, true);
      this.bus.emit('general:selected', { general });
    } else {
      this.sceneBuilder.setMovementOverlays(null, false);
      this.sceneBuilder.setAttackOverlays(null, false);
      this.bus.emit('general:selected', { general: null });
    }
    this.updateHireButton();
  }
  /**
   * После хода ИИ вернуть выделение: кто ходил последним, а если погиб —
   * первый живой генерал игрока.
   */
  autoSelectAfterAITurns() {
    if (!this.world) return;
    const mine = this.world.generals.filter(
      (g) => g.alive && g.factionId === this.world.playerFactionId,
    );
    if (mine.length === 0) return;
    const last = mine.find((g) => g.id === this._lastSelectedId) || null;
    this.selectGeneral(last || mine[0]);
  }
  selectTile(tile) {
    this.selectedTileKey = tile ? tile.key : null;
    if (!tile) { this.sceneBuilder.highlight.setSelected(null); return; }
    this.cameraController.centerOn(tile.hex, Theme.hexSize);
    const neighbors = tile.hex.neighbors()
      .map((n) => this.world.map.get(n.q, n.r))
      .filter((t) => t && t.isLand)
      .map((t) => t.key);
    this.sceneBuilder.highlight.setSelected(tile.key, neighbors);
  }
  getSelectedGeneral() {
    if (!this.selectedGeneralId) return null;
    return this.world.generals.find((g) => g.id === this.selectedGeneralId) || null;
  }
  isGeneralInOwnCity(general) {
    if (!general || !this.world) return null;
    const tile = this.world.map.get(general.hex.q, general.hex.r);
    if (!tile?.cityId) return null;
    const city = this.world.getCity(tile.cityId);
    if (!city || city.factionId !== general.factionId) return null;
    return { tile, city };
  }
  updateHireButton() {
    const general = this.getSelectedGeneral();
    const own = general ? this.isGeneralInOwnCity(general) : null;
    this.ui.setHireEnabled(!!own);
  }
}

📄 application\TileInteractionService.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { Theme } from '../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Клики и наведение на гексы/генералов.
 * Связывает выбор, движение, бой, город.
 * Держит состояние «подтверждённого хода»: первый клик закрепляет цель
 * (маркер), второй клик по гексу с маркером запускает движение.
 */
export class TileInteractionService {
  constructor({
    world, bus, sceneBuilder, cameraController, input,
    selection, movementController, battleOrchestrator,
    cityService, ui,
  }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.bus = bus;
    this.sceneBuilder = sceneBuilder;
    this.cameraController = cameraController;
    this.input = input;
    this.selection = selection;
    this.movement = movementController;
    this.battle = battleOrchestrator;
    this.city = cityService;
    this.ui = ui;
    this._hoveredGeneralId = null;
    this._hoveredCityId = null;
    // ─── Превью пути + подтверждение хода ───
    this._confirmedTargetKey = null; // закреплённая цель (маркер)
    this._lastPathKey = null;        // последняя показанная точка пути (для дедупликации)
    this._hintShown = false;
    this.bus.on('general:selected', () => this._clearPathState());
  }
  _clearPathState() {
    this._confirmedTargetKey = null;
    this._lastPathKey = null;
    this.sceneBuilder.clearPathPreview();
  }
  /** Пересчитать превью пути под наведённый гекс (или убрать точки). */
  _updatePathPreview(tile) {
    const general = this.selection.getSelectedGeneral();
    if (!general || !general.alive || !tile) {
      this.sceneBuilder.setPathPreview(null);
      return;
    }
    if (!this.world.movement.canMove(general, tile)) {
      this.sceneBuilder.setPathPreview(null);
      return;
    }
    const path = this.movement.findPath(general, tile);
    if (!path || path.length === 0) {
      this.sceneBuilder.setPathPreview(null);
      return;
    }
    const keys = path.map((s) => s.tile.key);
    const joined = keys.join('|');
    if (joined === this._lastPathKey) return;
    this._lastPathKey = joined;
    this.sceneBuilder.setPathPreview(keys);
  }
  onTileClick(tile, hitGeneral, hitCity) {
    if (!this.world || this.movement.isMoving) return;
    const playerFactionId = this.world.playerFactionId;
    // ─── Клик по генералу ───
    if (hitGeneral) {
      if (hitGeneral.factionId !== playerFactionId) {
        const mine = this.selection.getSelectedGeneral();
        if (mine && mine.factionId === playerFactionId) {
          const enemyTile = this.world.map.get(hitGeneral.hex.q, hitGeneral.hex.r);
          if (enemyTile && this.world.movement.canAttack(mine, enemyTile)) {
            const dist = this.world.movement.attackCostTo(mine, enemyTile);
            this.battle.confirmAttack(mine, enemyTile, dist, (defenderFactionId) => {
              this.battle.executeBattle(mine, enemyTile, dist, defenderFactionId, this.selection);
            });
            return;
          }
        }
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Это полководец другой фракции' });
        return;
      }
      if (this.selection.selectedGeneralId === hitGeneral.id) {
        this.cameraController.centerOn(hitGeneral.hex, Theme.hexSize);
        return;
      }
      this.selection.selectGeneral(hitGeneral);
      return;
    }
    // ─── Клик по городу ───
    if (hitCity && tile?.cityId) {
      this.city.handleCityClick(tile, hitCity);
      return;
    }
    // ─── Клик по гексу ───
    if (!tile) return;
    const general = this.selection.getSelectedGeneral();
    if (!general || !general.alive) return;
    // Враг на гексе?
    const enemyGen = this.world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
    if (enemyGen && enemyGen.factionId !== general.factionId) {
      this._clearPathState();
      if (this.world.movement.canAttack(general, tile)) {
        const dist = this.world.movement.attackCostTo(general, tile);
        this.battle.confirmAttack(general, tile, dist, (defenderFactionId) => {
          this.battle.executeBattle(general, tile, dist, defenderFactionId, this.selection);
        });
        return;
      }
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Слишком далеко — не дойти до врага' });
      return;
    }
    // Чужой город без генерала?
    if (tile.cityId) {
      const city = this.world.getCity(tile.cityId);
      if (city && city.factionId !== general.factionId && city.factionId !== null) {
        this._clearPathState();
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `Сначала победите защитников ${city.name}` });
        return;
      }
      if (city && city.factionId === null && city.settlement) {
        this._clearPathState();
        if (this.world.movement.canAttack(general, tile)) {
          const dist = this.world.movement.attackCostTo(general, tile);
          this.battle.confirmSettlementAttack(general, tile, city, dist, (defenderFactionId) => {
            this.battle.executeBattle(general, tile, dist, defenderFactionId, this.selection);
          });
        } else {
          this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `Сначала победите или купите ${city.name}` });
        }
        return;
      }
    }
    // ─── Обычный гекс: два клика — закрепить, подтвердить ───
    if (this.world.movement.canMove(general, tile)) {
      if (this._confirmedTargetKey === tile.key) {
        this._clearPathState();
        this.movement.executeMove(general, tile, {
          onComplete: (g) => {
            if (g.movePoints > 0 && g.alive) {
              this.selection.selectGeneral(g);
            } else {
              this.selection.selectGeneral(null);
            }
            this.selection.updateHireButton();
          },
        });
        return;
      }
      // Первый клик: закрепляем цель. Если пути на экране ещё нет
      // (например, тач-экран без hover) — рисуем его сразу.
      this._confirmedTargetKey = tile.key;
      this.sceneBuilder.setPathMarker(tile.key);
      this._updatePathPreview(tile);
      if (!this._hintShown) {
        this._hintShown = true;
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', {
          text: 'Ещё раз кликните по клетке с маркером — генерал пойдёт',
        });
      }
      return;
    }
    // Не хватает очков — сбрасываем закрепление
    this._clearPathState();
    const needed = this.world.movement.costTo(general, tile);
    if (needed != null && needed > general.movePoints) {
      this.bus.emit('general:no-move-points', {
        general, needed, have: general.movePoints,
      });
    }
  }
  onTileHover(tile, screenPos, meta = {}) {
    // ─── Сначала сбрасываем прошлые подсветки (безопасно) ───
    if (this._hoveredGeneralId) {
      if (typeof this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralHovered === 'function') {
        this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralHovered(this._hoveredGeneralId, false);
      }
      this._hoveredGeneralId = null;
    }
    if (this._hoveredCityId) {
      if (typeof this.sceneBuilder.setCityHovered === 'function') {
        this.sceneBuilder.setCityHovered(this._hoveredCityId, false);
      }
      this._hoveredCityId = null;
    }
    // ─── Превью пути: считаем ДО подсветок, чтобы ничего не могло
    // прервать его исполнение ───
    if (this.movement.isMoving || meta.hoverType === 'haloIcon') {
      this.sceneBuilder.setPathPreview(null);
    } else if (!meta.hoverType) {
      this._updatePathPreview(tile);
    } else {
      // hover по генералу/городу — точки убираем, маркер закрепления остаётся
      this.sceneBuilder.setPathPreview(null);
    }
    // ─── Подсветки (безопасные вызовы) ───
    if (meta.hoverType === 'general' && meta.general) {
      this._hoveredGeneralId = meta.general.id;
      if (typeof this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralHovered === 'function') {
        this.sceneBuilder.setGeneralHovered(this._hoveredGeneralId, true);
      }
    } else if (meta.hoverType === 'city' && tile?.cityId) {
      this._hoveredCityId = tile.cityId;
      if (typeof this.sceneBuilder.setCityHovered === 'function') {
        this.sceneBuilder.setCityHovered(this._hoveredCityId, true);
      }
    }
    this.sceneBuilder.highlight.setHovered(tile ? tile.key : null);
    if (meta.hoverType === 'haloIcon' && meta.halo) {
      this.bus.emit('halo:hovered', { halo: meta.halo, screenPos });
    } else {
      this.bus.emit('halo:hovered', { halo: null, screenPos });
    }
  }
}

📄 application\TurnOrchestrator.js

import { Theme } from '../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Ходы: игрок, AI, hot-seat. Самый «толстый» сервис,
 * но всё ещё в 3 раза меньше прежнего Game.
 */
export class TurnOrchestrator {
  constructor({
    world, bus, turnManager, profile, ui, sound, sceneBuilder,
    cameraController, input, selection, movementController, battleController, sceneSync,
  }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.bus = bus;
    this.turnManager = turnManager;
    this.profile = profile;
    this.ui = ui;
    this.sound = sound;
    this.sceneBuilder = sceneBuilder;
    this.cameraController = cameraController;
    this.input = input;
    this.selection = selection;
    this.movement = movementController;
    this.battleController = battleController;
    this.sceneSync = sceneSync;
    this.humanFactions = [];
    this.playerFactionId = null;
    this._aiBusy = false;
    this.onMatchFinished = null;   // колбэк от Game
  }
  setHumanFactions(list, playerId) {
    this.humanFactions = list;
    this.playerFactionId = playerId;
  }
  get isBusy() { return this._aiBusy; }
  endTurn() {
    if (!this.world) return;
    if (this.movement.isMoving) return;
    if (this._aiBusy) {
      this._aiBusy = false;
      if (this.turnManager) this.turnManager.isRunning = false;
    }
    const cfg = this.profile.matchConfig || { mode: 'ai' };
    if (cfg.mode === 'hotseat') { this.endTurnForHotseat(); return; }
    this.world.endTurn();
    this.selection.selectGeneral(null);
    this.sceneBuilder.updateGeneralPositions(this.world);
    this.bus.emit('turn:ended', { turn: this.world.turn });
    this.bus.emit('resources:changed', { factionId: this.world.playerFactionId });
    this.ui.quickJump?.refreshMinimap();
    this.runAITurns();
  }
  endTurnForHotseat() {
    const humans = this.humanFactions || [];
    if (humans.length < 2) { this.world.endTurn(); this.runAITurns(); return; }
    this.selection.selectGeneral(null);
    const nextId = this.world.nextHumanFaction(humans);
    const isFirstHuman = humans[0] === nextId && this.world.playerFactionId === humans[humans.length - 1];
    this.world.switchToFaction(nextId);
    this.playerFactionId = nextId;
    this.profile.lastPlayedFaction = nextId;
    if (isFirstHuman) {
      this.world.turn += 1;
      for (const g of this.world.generals) g.resetTurn();
      this.world.recruitPointsUsedMap.clear();
      for (const f of this.world.factions) {
        if (f.eliminated || f.neutral) continue;
        const pool = this.world.resourcePools.get(f.id);
        const income = this.world.economy.computeIncome(this.world, f.id);
        pool.add(income);
        this.world.recruitPools.get(f.id)?.addTurnIncome(this.world);
        const created = this.world.market.autoCreateOffers(f.id);
        for (const offer of created) {
          const label = offer.goods === 'weapons' ? 'оружия' : 'провизии';
          this.bus.emit('toast:show', {
            text: `Излишки ${label} ушли на рынок: ${offer.amount} за ${offer.price}💰`,
          });
        }
      }
      this.world._aiMarketTurn();
      this.world._checkElimination();
      this.bus.emit('turn:ended', { turn: this.world.turn });
    }
    this.world.recomputeVisibility();
    this.sceneSync.run(this.world, { factionId: nextId });
    this.ui.quickJump?.setWorld(this.world);
    this.selection.updateHireButton();
    const f = this.world.factions.find((x) => x.id === nextId);
    this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `Ход: ${f?.name || nextId}` });
    this.sound.play('turn');
    this.ui.turnIndicator.show(`Ход: ${f?.name || nextId}`);
    setTimeout(() => this.ui.turnIndicator.hide(), 1500);
    if (isFirstHuman) this.runAITurns();
  }
  async runAITurns() {
    if (!this.world) return;
    if (this._aiBusy) {
      this._aiBusy = false;
      if (this.turnManager) this.turnManager.isRunning = false;
    }
    const humans = this.humanFactions || [this.world.playerFactionId];
    const aiFactionIds = this.world.factions
      .filter((f) => !f.neutral && !f.eliminated && !humans.includes(f.id))
      .map((f) => f.id);
    if (aiFactionIds.length === 0) {
      this.afterAITurns();
      return;
    }
    this._aiBusy = true;
    this.ui.turnIndicator.show('Ход противника…');
    this.sound.play('turn');
    try {
      const battleStep = await this.turnManager.runAITurns(aiFactionIds, {
        onAITurnStart: (id) => {
          const f = this.world.factions.find((x) => x.id === id);
          this.ui.turnIndicator.show(`Ход: ${f?.name || id}`);
        },
        onAIStep: (step) => {
          try { this.onAIStep(step); }
          catch (e) { console.error('[TurnOrchestrator.onAIStep]', e, step); }
        },
        onAITurnEnd: () => this.sceneBuilder.refreshBorders(),
      });
      if (battleStep) {
        this.ui.turnIndicator.hide();
        if (!battleStep.general) {
          battleStep.general = this.world.generals.find((g) => g.id === battleStep.generalId);
        }
        if (!battleStep.general) {
          console.error('[TurnOrchestrator] battleStep without general');
          this.afterAITurns();
        } else {
          try {
            const result = await this.battleController.autoResolve({
              attackerGen: battleStep.general,
              targetTile: battleStep.targetTile,
              defenderFactionId: battleStep.defenderFactionId || 'goths',
              playerSide: 'defender',
            });
            const atkName = battleStep.general?.name || 'Противник';
            const winner = result.winner === 'attacker' ? atkName : 'защитник';
            const targetCity = battleStep.targetTile?.cityId
              ? this.world.getCity(battleStep.targetTile.cityId)
              : null;
            const place = targetCity?.name || 'территория';
            this.bus.emit('notification:show', {
              text: `${atkName} атаковал ${place}: победа ${winner}`,
              kind: result.winner === 'attacker' ? 'danger' : 'success',
            });
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('[TurnOrchestrator.runAITurns autoResolve]', e);
            battleStep.general.movePoints = 0;
            this.afterAITurns();
          }
        }
      } else {
        this.ui.turnIndicator.hide();
        this.afterAITurns();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[TurnOrchestrator.runAITurns]', e);
      this.ui.turnIndicator.hide();
      this.afterAITurns();
    } finally {
      this._aiBusy = false;
    }
  }
  onAIStep(step) {
    if (!this.world) return;
    try {
      if (step.action === 'move') {
        const gen = step.generalId
          ? this.world.generals.find((g) => g.id === step.generalId)
          : null;
        if (gen && step.fromHex && step.toHex) {
          this.movement.animateGeneralMove(gen, step.fromHex, step.toHex);
        }
        if (step.captured && step.toHex) {
          const tile = this.world.map.get(step.toHex.q, step.toHex.r);
          if (tile) {
            const v = this.sceneBuilder._tileVisuals.get(tile.key);
            if (v && typeof v.setFactionColor === 'function') {
              if (tile.factionId) v.setFactionColor(tile.factionId);
              v.setFactionRingVisible(true);
            }
          }
        }
        this.sceneBuilder.refreshBorders();
        this.world.recomputeVisibility();
        this.sceneBuilder.updateFog(this.world);
        this.sceneBuilder.updateGeneralBars?.(this.world);   // ← новая строка
        this.bus.emit('ai:step:move', { step });
      } else if (step.action === 'attack-city' || step.action === 'attack') {
        const gen = step.generalId
          ? this.world.generals.find((g) => g.id === step.generalId)
          : null;
        if (gen) this.sceneBuilder.updateGeneralPositions(this.world);
        this.sceneBuilder.refreshBorders();
        this.bus.emit('ai:step:attack', { step });
      } else if (step.action === 'recruit') {
        this.bus.emit('notification:show', {
          text: 'Противник нанял карту',
          kind: 'info',
        });
    } else if (step.action === 'hire-general') {
        this.sceneSync.run(this.world, { resources: false });
        this.bus.emit('notification:show', {
          text: 'Противник нанял полководца',
          kind: 'danger',
        });
      } else if (step.action === 'buy-settlement') {
        this.bus.emit('notification:show', {
          text: `Противник купил поселение «${step.cityName}»`,
          kind: 'danger',
        });
        this.sceneSync.run(this.world, { resources: false });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[TurnOrchestrator.onAIStep]', e, step);
    }
  }
  afterAITurns() {
    if (!this.world) return;
    const humans = this.humanFactions || [];
    if (humans.length > 0) {
      this.world.switchToFaction(humans[0]);
      this.playerFactionId = humans[0];
    }
    this.world.recomputeVisibility();
    this.sceneSync.run(this.world);
    this.selection.updateHireButton();
    this.selection.autoSelectAfterAITurns();
  }
}

  halo_wheat: '🌾', halo_pasture: '🐎', halo_mine: '⛏', halo_hunt: '🏹',
  halo_fishery: '🐟', halo_sawmill: '🌲', halo_vineyard: '🍇', halo_apiary: '🐝',
  halo_salt: '🧂', halo_pottery: '⚱',
};
export function iconUrl(key) {
  if (!key) return null;
  if (typeof key === 'string' && (key.startsWith('/') || key.startsWith('http'))) {
    return key;
  }
  const filename = ICON[key] || key;
  return `${ICON_BASE}${filename}.svg?v=${ICON_VERSION}`;
}
export function iconImg(key, { size = 16, alt = '', cls = '', color = '', title = '' } = {}) {
  const url = iconUrl(key);
  if (!url) return '';
  const filename = url.replace(ICON_BASE, '').replace('.svg', '').split('?')[0];
  const fallback = ICON_FALLBACK[filename] || ICON_FALLBACK[key] || '?';
  const styleAttr = color ? `style="color:${color}"` : '';
  const titleAttr = title ? `title="${title.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}"` : '';
  return `<img src="${url}"
    width="${size}" height="${size}"
    alt="${alt}" class="icon ${cls}"
    data-icon="${key}"
    data-fallback="${fallback}"
    ${titleAttr} ${styleAttr}
    onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:this.dataset.fallback,style:'font-size:${size}px;line-height:1;display:inline-block;vertical-align:middle;'}))">`;
}
export const RES_ICON = {
  gold:    iconImg('res_gold',    { size: 14 }),
  prov:    iconImg('res_prov',    { size: 14 }),
  weapons: iconImg('res_weapons', { size: 14 }),
  coins:   iconImg('res_coins',   { size: 14 }),
  tactic:  iconImg('res_tactic',  { size: 14 }),
};

📄 core\EventBus.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Простая шина событий. Обеспечивает слабую связность между модулями.
 * Любой класс может публиковать и слушать события, не зная о других.
 */
 export class EventBus {
    constructor() {
      this._handlers = new Map();
    }
    on(type, handler) {
      if (!this._handlers.has(type)) this._handlers.set(type, new Set());
      this._handlers.get(type).add(handler);
      return () => this.off(type, handler);
    }
    off(type, handler) {
      this._handlers.get(type)?.delete(handler);
    }
    emit(type, payload) {
      const handlers = this._handlers.get(type);
      if (!handlers) return;
      for (const h of handlers) {
        try { h(payload); } catch (e) {
          console.error(`[EventBus] "${type}"`, e);
        }
      }
    }
    clear() { this._handlers.clear(); }
  }

📄 core\Game.js

import { EventBus } from './EventBus.js';
import { World } from '../domain/world/World.js';
import { Renderer } from '../infrastructure/three/Renderer.js';
import { CameraController } from '../infrastructure/three/CameraController.js';
import { SceneBuilder } from '../infrastructure/three/SceneBuilder.js';
import { InputController } from '../input/InputController.js';
import { UIManager } from '../ui/UIManager.js';
import { Logger } from './Logger.js';
import { Theme } from '../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
import { RecruitController } from '../application/RecruitController.js';
import { BattleController } from '../application/BattleController.js';
import { TurnManager } from '../domain/ai/TurnManager.js';
import { SoundManager } from '../infrastructure/audio/SoundManager.js';
import { MarketController } from '../application/MarketController.js';
import { LobbyScreen } from '../ui/components/lobby/LobbyScreen.js';
import { SummonModal } from '../ui/components/lobby/SummonModal.js';
import { SaveManager } from '../domain/collection/SaveManager.js';
import { createStarterProfile } from '../domain/collection/PlayerProfileFactory.js';
import { DeckRepository } from '../domain/deck/DeckRepository.js';
import { RewardService } from '../domain/match/RewardService.js';
import { NotificationCenter } from '../ui/components/NotificationCenter.js';
// ─── Сервисы ───
import { SelectionController } from '../application/SelectionController.js';
import { MovementController } from '../application/MovementController.js';
import { BattleOrchestrator } from '../application/BattleOrchestrator.js';
import { CityInteractionService } from '../application/CityInteractionService.js';
import { TileInteractionService } from '../application/TileInteractionService.js';
import { TurnOrchestrator } from '../application/TurnOrchestrator.js';
import { SceneSync } from '../application/SceneSync.js';
const FACTION_IDS = ['scythians', 'bosporus', 'chersonesos', 'rome', 'goths'];
export class Game {
  constructor({ canvas, uiRoot, toastRoot }) {
    this.logger = new Logger('Game');
    this.bus = new EventBus();
    this.profile = SaveManager.load() || createStarterProfile('scythians');
    if (!this.profile.matchConfig) {
      this.profile.matchConfig = { mode: 'ai', playerCount: 5, playerFaction: 'scythians' };
    }
    this.deckRepo = DeckRepository.fromJSON(this.profile.unlockedDecks, this.profile.collection);
    for (const fid of FACTION_IDS) {
      const deck = this.deckRepo.getDeck(fid);
      if (deck.totalCount === 0) this.deckRepo.autoFill(fid);
    }
    this.rewardService = new RewardService({ profile: this.profile, bus: this.bus });
    this.world = null;
    this._humanFactions = [];
    this._playerFactionId = null;
    this.renderer = new Renderer(canvas);
    this.cameraController = new CameraController(this.renderer.camera, this.renderer.renderer);
    this.sceneBuilder = new SceneBuilder(this.renderer.scene);
    this.sound = new SoundManager();
    this.ui = new UIManager({
      root: uiRoot, toastRoot, bus: this.bus, world: null,
      onEndTurn: () => this.turnOrchestrator?.endTurn(),
      onRecruit: (key) => this.recruitController?.tryRecruit(key),
      battleController: null, marketController: null, sound: this.sound,
    });
    this.ui.onHire = () => this.openRecruitForSelected();
    this.ui.onMarket = () => this.openMarket();
    this.ui.onBuySettlement = (cityId) => this.cityService?.buySettlement(cityId);
    this.ui.onLensModeChange = (mode) => {
      this.sceneBuilder.setLensMode(mode);
      this.input.setPickables(this.sceneBuilder.getPickables());
    };
    this.input = new InputController({
      canvas, camera: this.renderer.camera,
      onTileClick: (tile, hitGeneral, hitCity) => this.tileService?.onTileClick(tile, hitGeneral, hitCity),
      onTileHover: (tile, screenPos, meta) => this.tileService?.onTileHover(tile, screenPos, meta),
    });
    this.input.attachCameraController(this.cameraController);
    this.notifications = new NotificationCenter(uiRoot, { max: 8 });
    this.bus.on('toast:show', ({ text }) => this.notifications.push({ text, kind: 'info' }));
    this.bus.on('notification:show', ({ text, kind }) => this.notifications.push({ text, kind }));
    this.bus.on('battle:finished', (r) => this.battleOrchestrator?.onBattleFinished(r));
    this.bus.on('battle:started', () => this.sound.play('play'));
    this._running = false;
    this.lobby = null;
    this.summonModal = null;
    // ─── Сервисы уровня мира ───
    this.selection = null;
    this.movement = null;
    this.battleOrchestrator = null;
    this.cityService = null;
    this.tileService = null;
    this.turnOrchestrator = null;
  }
  start(_factionId = 'scythians') {
    this._showLobby();
    this._loop();
  }
  _showLobby() {
    this.lobby = new LobbyScreen(this.ui.root, {
      profile: this.profile,
      decks: this.deckRepo,
      onPlay: (factionId) => this._startGame(factionId),
      onSummon: () => this._openSummon(),
      onSave: (opts) => {
        this.profile.unlockedDecks = this.deckRepo.toJSON();
        SaveManager.save(this.profile);
        if (opts?.error) this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: opts.error });
      },
    });
    this.lobby.show();
  }
  _openSummon() {
    if (!this.summonModal) {
      this.summonModal = new SummonModal(this.ui.root, {
        profile: this.profile,
        onSummoned: () => {
          this.profile.unlockedDecks = this.deckRepo.toJSON();
          SaveManager.save(this.profile);
          this.lobby?._render();
        },
      });
    }
    this.summonModal.open();
  }
  _startGame(factionId) {
    const cfg = this.profile.matchConfig || { mode: 'ai', playerCount: 5 };
    if (cfg.mode === 'hotseat') {
      const list = cfg.hotseatFactions || [];
      if (list.length === 0) {
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Не выбраны фракции для hot-seat' });
        return;
      }
      const ids = list.map((x) => x.factionId);
      if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) {
        this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Фракции игроков должны быть уникальны' });
        return;
      }
      for (const item of list) {
        const v = this.deckRepo.validate(item.factionId);
        if (!v.ok) {
          this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: v.errors.join('; ') });
          return;
        }
      }
      if (this.lobby) this.lobby.hide();
      this._initWorldForFaction(list[0].factionId);
      return;
    }
    if (!factionId) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Фракция не выбрана' });
      return;
    }
    const v = this.deckRepo.validate(factionId);
    if (!v.ok) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: v.errors.join('; ') });
      return;
    }
    if (this.lobby) this.lobby.hide();
    this._initWorldForFaction(factionId);
  }
  _initWorldForFaction(factionId) {
    const cfg = this.profile.matchConfig || { mode: 'ai', playerCount: 5 };
    const playerCount = Math.max(2, Math.min(5, cfg.playerCount || 5));
    let activeFactions;
    if (cfg.mode === 'hotseat') {
      const list = (cfg.hotseatFactions || []).map((x) => x.factionId);
      activeFactions = list.length ? list : FACTION_IDS.slice(0, playerCount);
    } else {
      activeFactions = FACTION_IDS.slice(0, playerCount);
    }
    this._humanFactions = cfg.mode === 'hotseat' ? activeFactions.slice() : [factionId];
    const firstFaction = cfg.mode === 'hotseat' ? activeFactions[0] : factionId;
    this._playerFactionId = firstFaction;
    const playerDeck = this.deckRepo.getDeck(firstFaction);
    this.world = new World({
      seed: 1337, radius: 14, playerDeck,
      playerGeneralPreset: this.profile.starterGeneralByFaction?.[firstFaction] ?? 0,
      activeFactions,
    });
    this.world.onFactionEliminated = (fid) => {
      const f = this.world.factions.find((x) => x.id === fid);
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `⚔ Фракция «${f?.name || fid}» выбыла` });
    };
    this.world.onMarketAutoOffer = ({ text }) => {
      this.bus.emit('notification:show', { text, kind: 'trade' });
    };
    this.world.onConnectivityEvent = (ev) => {
      if (ev.text) {
        this.bus.emit('notification:show', {
          text: ev.text,
          kind: ev.type === 'city' ? 'danger' : 'info',
        });
      }
    };
    // ─── Контроллеры ───
    this.turnManager = new TurnManager({ world: this.world, bus: this.bus });
    this.recruitController = new RecruitController({ world: this.world, bus: this.bus });
    this.marketController = new MarketController({ world: this.world, bus: this.bus });
    this.battleController = new BattleController({ world: this.world, bus: this.bus });
    // ─── Сервисы ───
    this.selection = new SelectionController({
      world: this.world, bus: this.bus,
      sceneBuilder: this.sceneBuilder, cameraController: this.cameraController, ui: this.ui,
    });
    // sceneSync создаём ДО movement — он им пользуется
    this.sceneSync = new SceneSync({
      sceneBuilder: this.sceneBuilder,
      input: this.input,
      ui: this.ui,
      bus: this.bus,
    });
    this.movement = new MovementController({
      world: this.world, sceneBuilder: this.sceneBuilder,
      cameraController: this.cameraController,
      input: this.input, ui: this.ui, bus: this.bus,
      sceneSync: this.sceneSync,
    });
    this.battleOrchestrator = new BattleOrchestrator({
      world: this.world, bus: this.bus, battleController: this.battleController,
      sceneBuilder: this.sceneBuilder, cameraController: this.cameraController,
      input: this.input, ui: this.ui, sound: this.sound,
      profile: this.profile, humanFactions: this._humanFactions,
      pendingBattle: { cost: 0, defenderFactionId: null },
      sceneSync: this.sceneSync,
    });
    this.battleOrchestrator.onMatchFinished = () => this._finishMatchRewards();
    this.battleOrchestrator.onAfterAITurns = () => {
      if (this.turnOrchestrator?.isBusy) this.turnOrchestrator.afterAITurns();
    };
    this.cityService = new CityInteractionService({
      world: this.world, bus: this.bus,
      battleOrchestrator: this.battleOrchestrator,
      selection: this.selection, ui: this.ui,
      sceneBuilder: this.sceneBuilder,
      cameraController: this.cameraController,
      input: this.input, sound: this.sound,
      sceneSync: this.sceneSync,
    });
    this.tileService = new TileInteractionService({
      world: this.world, bus: this.bus,
      sceneBuilder: this.sceneBuilder,
      cameraController: this.cameraController, input: this.input,
      selection: this.selection, movementController: this.movement,
      battleOrchestrator: this.battleOrchestrator,
      cityService: this.cityService, ui: this.ui,
    });
    this.turnOrchestrator = new TurnOrchestrator({
      world: this.world, bus: this.bus, turnManager: this.turnManager,
      profile: this.profile, ui: this.ui, sound: this.sound,
      sceneBuilder: this.sceneBuilder, cameraController: this.cameraController,
      input: this.input, selection: this.selection,
      movementController: this.movement, battleController: this.battleController,
      sceneSync: this.sceneSync,
    });
    this.turnOrchestrator.setHumanFactions(this._humanFactions, firstFaction);
    this.turnOrchestrator.onMatchFinished = () => this._finishMatchRewards();
    // ─── UI-связки ───
    this.ui.setWorld(this.world);
    this.ui.battleController = this.battleController;
    this.ui.marketController = this.marketController;
    this.ui.onBuySettlement = (cityId) => this.cityService.buySettlement(cityId);
    this.ui.onAttackCity = (cityId, defenderFactionId) => this.cityService.attackCityById(cityId, defenderFactionId);
    if (this.ui.battleModal) this.ui.battleModal.controller = this.battleController;
    if (this.ui.marketPanel) {
      this.ui.marketPanel.controller = this.marketController;
      this.ui.marketPanel.world = this.world;
    }
    if (this.ui.factionsPanel) this.ui.factionsPanel.world = this.world;
    if (this.ui.quickJump) {
      this.ui.quickJump.theme = { hexSize: Theme.hexSize };
      this.ui.quickJump.cameraController = this.cameraController;
    }
    // ─── Найм генерала → перестроить сцену ───
    this.bus.on('world:generalsChanged', ({ general }) => {
      if (!this.world) return;
      this.world.recomputeVisibility();
      this.sceneSync.run(this.world, { resources: false });
      if (general) {
        this.cameraController.centerOn(general.hex, Theme.hexSize);
        this.selection.selectGeneral(general);
      }
    });
    this._startWorld(firstFaction);
  }
  _startWorld(factionId) {
    this.world.selectPlayerFaction(factionId);
    const pool = this.world.resourcePools.get(this.world.playerFactionId);
    this.ui.bindResources(pool);
    this.world.recomputeVisibility();
    this.sceneBuilder.rebuild(this.world);
    this.sceneBuilder.updateFog(this.world);
    this.input.setPickables(this.sceneBuilder.getPickables());
    const player = this.world.playerFaction;
    if (player?.startHex) this.cameraController.centerOn(player.startHex, Theme.hexSize);
    this.ui.initMinimap({
      cameraController: this.cameraController,
      onCenter: (tile) => { if (tile) this.cameraController.centerOn(tile.hex, Theme.hexSize); },
    });
    if (!localStorage.getItem('tavrida_tutorial_v1')) {
      this.ui.showTutorial(() => { this.input.setPickables(this.sceneBuilder.getPickables()); });
    }
    this.selection.updateHireButton();
    this.bus.emit('game:started', { faction: factionId });
  }
  // ─── Публичные API ───
  openMarket() { if (this.world) this.ui.marketPanel.open(); }
  openRecruitForSelected() {
    if (!this.world) return;
    const general = this.selection.getSelectedGeneral();
    if (!general) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Сначала выберите полководца' });
      return;
    }
    const own = this.selection.isGeneralInOwnCity(general);
    if (!own) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Полководец должен стоять в вашем городе/деревне' });
      return;
    }
    this.ui.openRecruitModal({ factionId: this.world.playerFactionId, general, world: this.world });
  }
  _finishMatchRewards() {
    const cfg = this.profile.matchConfig || { mode: 'ai' };
    const winner = this.world.winner;
    const defeated = this.world.factions
      .filter((f) => !f.neutral && f.eliminated && f.id !== winner)
      .map((f) => f.id);
    this.rewardService.applyMatchRewards({
      winnerFactionId: winner,
      playerFactionId: this._playerFactionId || this.world.playerFactionId,
      mode: cfg.mode,
      defeatedByPlayerFaction: defeated,
      humanFactionIds: this._humanFactions || [],
    });
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.ui.root.querySelectorAll('.battle-modal-overlay, .battle-result-overlay').forEach((e) => e.remove());
      this.world = null;
      this._humanFactions = [];
      this._showLobby();
    }, 1500);
  }
  _loop() {
    if (this._running) return;
    this._running = true;
    const tick = () => {
      if (!this._running) return;
      this.renderer.tick();
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    tick();
  }
  stop() { this._running = false; }
}

export class Logger {
    constructor(namespace = 'App') { this.ns = namespace; }
    info(...args)  { console.log(`[${this.ns}]`, ...args); }
    warn(...args)  { console.warn(`[${this.ns}]`, ...args); }
    error(...args) { console.error(`[${this.ns}]`, ...args); }
  }

📄 core\Random.js

/**
 * Детерминированный ГПСЧ (mulberry32). Единый интерфейс для всех случайностей,
 * чтобы можно было воспроизвести мир по seed.
 */
 export class Random {
    constructor(seed = 1337) {
      this._seed = seed >>> 0;
      this._state = this._seed;
    }
    next() {
      let t = (this._state += 0x6D2B79F5);
      t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    }
    int(min, max) { return Math.floor(this.next() * (max - min + 1)) + min; }
    pick(arr) { return arr[Math.floor(this.next() * arr.length)]; }
    chance(p) { return this.next() < p; }
    reset() { this._state = this._seed; }
  }

📄 core\VisibilityService.js

/**
 * Туман войны. Радиус обзора = 3 гекса от каждого своего полководца.
 * Все города (isCity=true) видны всегда — это публичная информация.
 */
 export class VisibilityService {
  constructor({ world }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.viewRadius = 3;
  }
  recompute() {
    for (const tile of this.world.map.all()) {
      tile.isVisible = false;
    }
    // ─── Обзор от полководцев ───
    for (const gen of this.world.generals) {
      if (!gen.alive) continue;
      const center = gen.hex;
      for (const tile of this.world.map.all()) {
        if (tile.hex.distance(center) <= this.viewRadius) {
          tile.isVisible = true;
          tile.isExplored = true;
        }
      }
    }
    // ─── Все города и поселения видны всегда ───
    for (const city of this.world.cities) {
      if (!city.tileKey) continue;
      const tile = this.world.map.getByKey(city.tileKey);
      if (!tile) continue;
      tile.isVisible = true;
      tile.isExplored = true;
      // Ореол вокруг города раскрыт в тумане
      for (const n of tile.hex.neighbors()) {
        const nt = this.world.map.get(n.q, n.r);
        if (nt && nt.isLand) nt.isExplored = true;
      }
    }
  }
  isVisible(tile) { return !!tile?.isVisible; }
  isExplored(tile) { return !!tile?.isExplored; }
}

📄 domain\ai\AIController.js
