import { AIStrategy } from './AIStrategy.js';
/**
 * Исполнитель решений ИИ.
 * Доработано: корректный найм через пул, движение, атака городов.
 */
export class AIController {
    constructor({ world, strategy = new AIStrategy() }) {
        this.world = world;
        this.strategy = strategy;
    }
    takeTurn(factionId) {
        const actions = [];
        let attempts = 0;
        while (attempts < 8) {
            attempts++;
            const decision = this.strategy.decide({ world: this.world, factionId });
            if (decision.kind === 'idle') break;
            // ─── Найм ───
            if (decision.kind === 'recruit') {
                const res = this.world.recruit.recruit({
                    factionId,
                    cardKey: decision.cardKey,
                    generalId: decision.general.id,
                });
                if (!res.ok) break;
                actions.push({ kind: 'recruit', card: res.card });
                continue;
            }
            // ─── Атака полководца или города ───
            if (decision.kind === 'attack' || decision.kind === 'attack-city') {
                const { general, tile } = decision;
                const dist = this.world.movement.attackCostTo(general, tile) ?? 1;
                general.spendMove(dist);
                const enemy = this.world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
                let defenderFactionId;
                if (enemy) {
                    defenderFactionId = enemy.factionId;
                } else if (tile.cityId) {
                    const city = this.world.getCity(tile.cityId);
                    defenderFactionId = city?.settlement
                        ? (city.neutralFaction || 'tauri')
                        : (city?.factionId || 'goths');
                } else {
                    defenderFactionId = 'goths';
                }
                actions.push({
                    kind: 'battle',
                    attackerGen: general,
                    targetTile: tile,
                    defenderFactionId,
                });
                break;
            }
            // ─── Движение ───
            if (decision.kind === 'move') {
                const { general, tile } = decision;
                const dist = this.world.movement.costTo(general, tile);
                if (dist == null) break;
                general.spendMove(dist);
                general.moveTo(tile.hex);
                if (!tile.factionId) {
                    const city = tile.cityId ? this.world.getCity(tile.cityId) : null;
                    if (!city || city.factionId === factionId) tile.setFaction(factionId);
                }
                actions.push({ kind: 'move', general, tile });
                continue;
            }
            break;
        }
        return { factionId, actions };
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 domain\ai\AIStrategy.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { HALO_TYPES } from '../../domain/world/haloConfig.js';
/**
 * Стратегия ИИ.
 *
 * Приоритеты:
 *   1. Купить поселение (если есть 40 золота и поселение граничит с нами).
 *   2. Атака — только если цель СОСЕДНЯЯ и карт >= MIN_CARDS_TO_ATTACK.
 *   3. Найм/возврат в город/деревню, если карт < RECRUIT_THRESHOLD.
 *   4. Захват ничейного гекса с приоритетом полезных ореолов.
 *   5. Возврат в город, если карт мало.
 *
 * «Полезность» ореола: сумма доходов gold+prov+weapons. Чем больше — тем ценнее.
 * Приоритет — ореолам, которые МЫ МОЖЕМ монополизировать (граничат с нами).
 */
 export class AIStrategy {
    static MIN_CARDS_TO_ATTACK = 4;
    static RECRUIT_THRESHOLD = 5;
    decide({ world, factionId, opts = {} }) {
        const skipInstant = opts.skipInstant === true;
        const hasRecruited = opts.hasRecruited === true;
        const generals = world.generals.filter(
            (g) => g.factionId === factionId && g.alive
        );
        if (!generals.length) return { kind: 'idle' };
        const strategy = FACTION_STRATEGIES[factionId] || FACTION_STRATEGIES.scythians;
        // ─── Мгновенные действия ───
        if (!skipInstant) {
            const hire = this._tryHireGeneral(world, factionId);
            if (hire) return hire;
            const recruit = this._tryRecruit(world, factionId, generals[0]);
            if (recruit && Math.random() < strategy.recruitChance) return recruit;
        }
        // ─── 1. Покупка поселений ───
        const buySettlement = this._tryBuySettlement(world, factionId, generals);
        if (buySettlement) return buySettlement;
        // ─── 2. Атака (только соседняя цель и с картами) ───
        const attackOptions = [];
        for (const gen of generals) {
            const cardsReady = gen.deck.cards.length >= AIStrategy.MIN_CARDS_TO_ATTACK;
            const attacks = world.movement.reachableForAttack(gen);
            for (const { tile, general: enemy, distance } of attacks.values()) {
                if (distance !== 1) continue;   // только соседний гекс
                if (enemy && enemy.factionId !== factionId) {
                    if (!cardsReady && enemy.lives > 1) continue;
                    if (gen.lives <= 1 && enemy.lives > 1) continue;
                    let score = strategy.attackGeneralWeight * (100 - distance * 2);
                    score += (3 - enemy.lives) * 20;
                    if (enemy.lives === 1) score += 40 * strategy.killWeight;
                    if (gen.lives <= 1) score -= 80;
                    attackOptions.push({ gen, tile, kind: 'attack', score });
                } else if (tile.cityId) {
                    const city = world.getCity(tile.cityId);
                    if (!city || city.factionId === factionId) continue;
                    if (!cardsReady) continue;
                    const enemyGen = world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
                    if (enemyGen && enemyGen.factionId !== factionId) {
                        if (gen.deck.cards.length < AIStrategy.MIN_CARDS_TO_ATTACK + 2) continue;
                    }
                    let score = strategy.attackCityWeight * (city.isCity ? 95 : 65);
                    score -= distance * 3;
                    if (city.settlement) score -= 30;   // покупать дешевле
                    attackOptions.push({ gen, tile, kind: 'attack-city', score });
                }
            }
        }
        if (attackOptions.length > 0) {
            attackOptions.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
            const best = attackOptions[0];
            if (best.score > 0) {
                return { kind: best.kind, general: best.gen, tile: best.tile };
            }
        }
        // ─── 3. Движение ───
        const moveOptions = [];
        for (const gen of generals) {
            const moves = world.movement.reachableForMove(gen);
            const isInOwnCity = this._isInOwnCity(world, gen, factionId);
            const hasRecruitOptions = this._hasRecruitOptions(world, factionId);
            const cardsLow = gen.deck.cards.length < AIStrategy.RECRUIT_THRESHOLD;
            for (const { tile, distance } of moves.values()) {
                if (tile.hex.key === gen.hex.key) continue;
                if (distance > gen.movePoints) continue;
                const city = tile.cityId ? world.getCity(tile.cityId) : null;
                const isOwnTile = tile.factionId === factionId;
                const isNeutralTile = !tile.factionId;
                const isForeignTile = tile.factionId && tile.factionId !== factionId;
                let score = 0;
                if (isNeutralTile) {
                    // ─── Ничейный гекс — считаем «полезность» ───
                    score = this._scoreNeutralTile(world, tile, factionId, distance, strategy);
                } else if (isForeignTile) {
                    score += (25 - distance * 2) * strategy.exploreWeight;
                } else if (isOwnTile) {
                    // Транзит по своим
                    const distToNeutral = this._nearestUnownedDistance(world, tile, factionId);
                    if (distToNeutral === 0) score += 5;
                    else score -= 10;
                }
                // ─── Сильный бонус: вернуться в СВОЙ ГОРОД/ДЕРЕВНЮ для найма ───
                if (
                    !hasRecruited &&
                    hasRecruitOptions &&
                    cardsLow &&
                    city &&
                    city.factionId === factionId &&
                    !isInOwnCity &&
                    distance <= gen.movePoints
                ) {
                    // Насколько карт не хватает
                    const deficit = AIStrategy.RECRUIT_THRESHOLD - gen.deck.cards.length;
                    score += 100 + deficit * 20;
                }
                // ─── Бонус за выход из города на ничейный ───
                if (isInOwnCity && (isNeutralTile || isForeignTile)) {
                    score += 20;
                }
                moveOptions.push({ gen, tile, kind: 'move', score });
            }
        }
        if (moveOptions.length === 0) {
            for (const gen of generals) {
                const any = this._anyAdjacent(gen, world, factionId);
                if (any) return { kind: 'move', general: gen, tile: any, emergency: true };
            }
            return { kind: 'idle' };
        }
        moveOptions.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
        const best = moveOptions[0];
        if (best.score > -50) {
            return { kind: best.kind, general: best.gen, tile: best.tile };
        }
        return { kind: 'idle' };
    }
    /**
     * Score для ничейного гекса.
     * Учитывает ореол: полезность (yield), монополия, связность с нашей территорией.
     */
    _scoreNeutralTile(world, tile, factionId, distance, strategy) {
        let score = (20 - distance * 2) * strategy.exploreWeight;
        // Принадлежность к ореолу?
        const halo = world.haloAt(tile);
        if (halo) {
            const type = HALO_TYPES[halo.type];
            if (type) {
                const yieldSum = (type.yield.gold || 0) + (type.yield.prov || 0) + (type.yield.weapons || 0);
                // Сколько гексов ореола уже наши
                const tiles = world.haloTiles(halo);
                const owned = tiles.filter((t) => t.factionId === factionId).length;
                const total = tiles.length;
                const ownedRatio = owned / Math.max(1, total);
                // Бонус за полезность ореола
                score += yieldSum * 8;
                // Бонус за близость к монополии
                if (owned > 0 && owned < total) {
                    // Мы на пути к монополии — захват гекса даёт +1
                    score += 30 * ownedRatio;
                } else if (owned === 0) {
                    // Ореол полностью чужой/ничейный
                    score += 5;
                } else if (owned === total) {
                    // Уже монополия — не нужно
                    score -= 100;
                }
                // Штраф: ореол занят несколькими фракциями — риск
                const owners = new Set(tiles.map((t) => t.factionId).filter(Boolean));
                if (owners.size >= 2) score -= 20;
            }
        }
        // Связность: соседний гекс — наш?
        let adjacentOwn = 0;
        for (const n of tile.hex.neighbors()) {
            const nt = world.map.get(n.q, n.r);
            if (nt && nt.factionId === factionId) adjacentOwn++;
        }
        score += adjacentOwn * 10;
        return score;
    }
    _tryBuySettlement(world, factionId, generals) {
        const pool = world.resourcePools.get(factionId);
        if (!pool || pool.gold < 40) return null;
        // Ищем поселение, которое граничит с нашей территорией
        for (const city of world.cities) {
            if (!city.settlement) continue;
            if (city.factionId === factionId) continue;
            if (!city.tileKey) continue;
            const tile = world.map.getByKey(city.tileKey);
            if (!tile) continue;
            // Есть ли у нас соседний гекс?
            let adjacent = false;
            for (const n of tile.hex.neighbors()) {
                const nt = world.map.get(n.q, n.r);
                if (nt && nt.factionId === factionId) { adjacent = true; break; }
            }
            if (!adjacent) continue;
            // Есть ли генерал, который может дойти?
            for (const gen of generals) {
                const dist = world.movement.costTo(gen, tile);
                if (dist != null && dist <= gen.movePoints) {
                    // Покупаем!
                    const res = world.buySettlement(city.id, factionId, 40);
                    if (res.ok) {
                        return {
                            kind: 'buy-settlement',
                            cityId: city.id,
                            cityName: city.name,
                            general: gen,
                        };
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    _nearestUnownedDistance(world, tile, factionId, maxRadius = 5) {
        if (!tile.factionId) return 0;
        for (let r = 1; r <= maxRadius; r++) {
            const ring = this._hexRing(tile.hex, r);
            for (const h of ring) {
                const t = world.map.get(h.q, h.r);
                if (t && t.isLand && !t.factionId) return r;
            }
        }
        return maxRadius + 1;
    }
    _hexRing(center, r) {
        const out = [];
        if (r === 0) return [center];
        const dirs = [
            { q: 1, r: 0 }, { q: 1, r: -1 }, { q: 0, r: -1 },
            { q: -1, r: 0 }, { q: -1, r: 1 }, { q: 0, r: 1 },
        ];
        let hex = { q: center.q + dirs[4].q * r, r: center.r + dirs[4].r * r };
        for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            for (let j = 0; j < r; j++) {
                out.push({ q: hex.q, r: hex.r });
                hex = { q: hex.q + dirs[i].q, r: hex.r + dirs[i].r };
            }
        }
        return out;
    }
    _isInOwnCity(world, gen, factionId) {
        const t = world.map.get(gen.hex.q, gen.hex.r);
        if (!t?.cityId) return false;
        const c = world.getCity(t.cityId);
        return !!(c && c.factionId === factionId);
    }
    _hasRecruitOptions(world, factionId) {
        const pool = world.recruitPools.get(factionId);
        return !!(pool && pool.available.length > 0);
    }
    _anyAdjacent(gen, world, factionId) {
        const { movement, map } = world;
        for (const n of gen.hex.neighbors()) {
            const tile = map.get(n.q, n.r);
            if (!tile || !tile.isLand) continue;
            if (tile.hex.key === gen.hex.key) continue;
            const occ = world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
            if (occ && occ.id !== gen.id) continue;
            if (movement.isForeignCityTile(tile, factionId)) continue;
            if (movement.tileCost(tile) > gen.movePoints) continue;
            return tile;
        }
        return null;
    }
    _tryHireGeneral(world, factionId) {
        const myCities = world.cities.filter(
            (c) => c.factionId === factionId && c.isCity
        );
        const myGenerals = world.generals.filter(
            (g) => g.factionId === factionId && g.alive
        );
        const pool = world.resourcePools.get(factionId);
        if (!pool) return null;
        // Максимум = городов + 1, при золоте >= 150 — ещё +1
        let maxGenerals = Math.max(1, myCities.length + 1);
        if (pool.gold >= 150) maxGenerals += 1;
        // Есть ли павший генерал — приоритет на восстановление
        const deadGeneral = world.generals.find(
            (g) => g.factionId === factionId && !g.alive
        );
        if (myGenerals.length >= maxGenerals && !deadGeneral) return null;
        if (pool.gold < 30) return null;
        for (const city of myCities) {
            if (!city.tileKey) continue;
            const tile = world.map.getByKey(city.tileKey);
            if (!tile) continue;
            const genInCity = world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
            if (genInCity && genInCity.factionId === factionId) {
                return { kind: 'hire-general', general: genInCity, tileKey: tile.key };
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    _tryRecruit(world, factionId, general) {
        if (general.deck.cards.length >= 8) return null;
        const pool = world.recruitPools.get(factionId);
        if (!pool || !pool.available.length) return null;
        const resourcePool = world.resourcePools.get(factionId);
        if (!resourcePool || resourcePool.gold < 10) return null;
        const tile = world.map.get(general.hex.q, general.hex.r);
        const city = tile?.cityId ? world.getCity(tile.cityId) : null;
        if (!city || city.factionId !== factionId) return null;
        const priorities = this._recruitPriority(factionId);
        for (const key of priorities) {
            if (!pool.available.find((c) => c.key === key)) continue;
            return { kind: 'recruit', general, cardKey: key };
        }
        return { kind: 'recruit', general, cardKey: pool.available[0].key };
    }
    _recruitPriority(factionId) {
        return {
            scythians: ['scyth_rider', 'scyth_warrior', 'scyth_archer', 'scyth_shaman'],
            bosporus: ['bosp_hoplite', 'bosp_cavalry', 'bosp_archer', 'bosp_merchant'],
            chersonesos: ['cher_hoplite', 'cher_mercenary', 'cher_archer', 'cher_priest'],
            rome: ['rome_legion', 'rome_centurion', 'rome_archer', 'rome_siege'],
            goths: ['goth_berserk', 'goth_warrior', 'goth_archer', 'goth_shaman'],
        }[factionId] || [];
    }
}
const FACTION_STRATEGIES = {
    scythians: { name: 'Набеги', recruitChance: 0.35, attackGeneralWeight: 1.2, attackCityWeight: 0.8, moveToCityWeight: 1.0, exploreWeight: 1.5, killWeight: 1.5 },
    bosporus: { name: 'Торговля', recruitChance: 0.5, attackGeneralWeight: 0.8, attackCityWeight: 1.0, moveToCityWeight: 1.4, exploreWeight: 1.0, killWeight: 0.8 },
    chersonesos: { name: 'Оборона', recruitChance: 0.6, attackGeneralWeight: 0.7, attackCityWeight: 0.9, moveToCityWeight: 1.1, exploreWeight: 0.9, killWeight: 1.0 },
    rome: { name: 'Прямые атаки', recruitChance: 0.4, attackGeneralWeight: 1.0, attackCityWeight: 1.5, moveToCityWeight: 1.2, exploreWeight: 0.8, killWeight: 1.2 },
    goths: { name: 'Агрессия', recruitChance: 0.4, attackGeneralWeight: 1.4, attackCityWeight: 1.0, moveToCityWeight: 0.9, exploreWeight: 1.3, killWeight: 1.3 },
};

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 domain\ai\BotTurnPlanner.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { AIStrategy } from './AIStrategy.js';
export class BotTurnPlanner {
    constructor({ world }) {
        this.world = world;
    }
    planForFaction(factionId) {
        console.log(`[Planner] planForFaction ${factionId}`);
        const faction = this.world.factions.find((f) => f.id === factionId);
        if (!faction) {
            console.warn(`[Planner] no faction ${factionId}`);
            return [];
        }
        if (faction.eliminated) {
            console.log(`[Planner] ${factionId} eliminated`);
            return [];
        }
        const strategy = new AIStrategy();
        const steps = [];
        let factionHasRecruited = false;
        const generalsBefore = this.world.generals.filter(
            (g) => g.factionId === factionId && g.alive
        );
        console.log(`[Planner] ${factionId}: generals=${generalsBefore.length}`,
            generalsBefore.map((g) => ({
                id: g.id, hex: g.hex.key, mp: g.movePoints,
            })));
        if (generalsBefore.length === 0) {
            console.log(`[Planner] ${factionId}: no alive generals`);
            return [];
        }
        // ─── 1. Найм генерала ───
        const hire = this._findHireDecision(strategy, factionId);
        if (hire) {
            const res = this.world.recruit.recruitGeneral({
                factionId,
                tileKey: hire.tileKey,
            });
            if (res.ok) {
                steps.push({ action: 'hire-general', generalId: null, factionId });
                console.log(`[Planner] hired general: ${res.general.name}`);
            } else {
                console.log(`[Planner] hire failed:`, res.reason);
            }
        }
        // ─── 1.5. Покупка поселения ───
        const buyDecision = this._findBuySettlementDecision(strategy, factionId);
        if (buyDecision) {
            const res = this.world.buySettlement(buyDecision.cityId, factionId, 40);
            if (res.ok) {
                steps.push({
                    action: 'buy-settlement',
                    cityId: buyDecision.cityId,
                    cityName: buyDecision.cityName,
                    factionId,
                });
                console.log(`[Planner] bought settlement: ${buyDecision.cityName}`);
            }
        }
        // ─── 2. Найм карты ───
        const gensInCity = this.world.generals.filter((g) =>
            g.factionId === factionId &&
            g.alive &&
            this._isInOwnCity(g, factionId)
        );
        for (const gen of gensInCity) {
            if (factionHasRecruited) break;
            const recruit = this._findRecruitDecision(strategy, factionId);
            if (!recruit) break;
            const ok = this.world.recruit.recruit({
                factionId,
                cardKey: recruit.cardKey,
                generalId: recruit.general.id,
            }).ok;
            if (ok) {
                steps.push({ action: 'recruit', generalId: recruit.general.id, factionId });
                factionHasRecruited = true;
                console.log(`[Planner] recruited: ${recruit.cardKey}`);
            } else {
                console.log(`[Planner] recruit failed for ${recruit.cardKey}`);
            }
        }
        // ─── 3. Движение ───
        const generalsNow = this.world.generals.filter(
            (g) => g.factionId === factionId && g.alive
        );
        for (const gen of generalsNow) {
            console.log(`[Planner] moving ${gen.id} at ${gen.hex.key} mp=${gen.movePoints}`);
            const genSteps = this._moveGeneral(gen, strategy, factionId, factionHasRecruited);
            console.log(`[Planner] ${gen.id}: got ${genSteps.length} steps`);
            steps.push(...genSteps);
            if (genSteps.some((s) => s.action === 'attack' || s.action === 'attack-city')) {
                break;
            }
        }
        // ─── АВАРИЙНЫЙ ВЫХОД ───
        const movedAny = steps.some(
            (s) => s.action === 'move' || s.action === 'attack' || s.action === 'attack-city'
        );
        if (!movedAny) {
            console.warn(`[Planner] EMERGENCY: nobody moved, forcing`);
            for (const gen of generalsNow) {
                const em = this._emergencyMove(gen, factionId);
                if (em.length > 0) {
                    steps.push(...em);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        console.log(`[Planner] ${factionId}: total steps=${steps.length}`);
        return steps;
    }
    _isInOwnCity(gen, factionId) {
        const t = this.world.map.get(gen.hex.q, gen.hex.r);
        if (!t?.cityId) return false;
        const c = this.world.getCity(t.cityId);
        return !!(c && c.factionId === factionId);
    }
    _findHireDecision(strategy, factionId) {
        try {
            const d = strategy.decide({
                world: this.world, factionId, opts: { skipInstant: false },
            });
            return d.kind === 'hire-general' ? d : null;
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('[Planner] _findHireDecision CRASH:', e);
            return null;
        }
    }
    _findRecruitDecision(strategy, factionId) {
        try {
            const d = strategy.decide({
                world: this.world, factionId, opts: { skipInstant: false },
            });
            return d.kind === 'recruit' ? d : null;
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('[Planner] _findRecruitDecision CRASH:', e);
            return null;
        }
    }
    _moveGeneral(gen, strategy, factionId, hasRecruited) {
        const steps = [];
        const MAX_ITER = 15;
        let guard = 0;
        const recentPositions = new Set();
        while (gen.movePoints > 0 && guard++ < MAX_ITER) {
            console.log(`  [Planner._moveGeneral] iter=${guard} gen=${gen.id} hex=${gen.hex.key} mp=${gen.movePoints}`);
            const posKey = `${gen.hex.key}|${gen.movePoints}`;
            if (recentPositions.has(posKey)) {
                console.warn(`  [Planner] STOP: recent position ${posKey}`);
                break;
            }
            recentPositions.add(posKey);
            let decision;
            try {
                decision = strategy.decide({
                    world: this.world, factionId,
                    opts: { skipInstant: true, hasRecruited },
                });
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('  [Planner] decide CRASH:', e);
                break;
            }
            console.log(`  [Planner] decision:`, decision.kind, decision.tile?.key);
            if (decision.kind === 'idle') {
                console.warn(`  [Planner] STOP: idle`);
                break;
            }
            // ─── АТАКА ───
            // ВАЖНО: сохраняем `general` — он нужен BattleFactory!
            // ─── АТАКА ───
            if (decision.kind === 'attack' || decision.kind === 'attack-city') {
                const dist = this.world.movement.attackCostTo(gen, decision.tile);
                // ─── ЗАЩИТА: атака только на соседний гекс ───
                if (dist !== 1) {
                    console.warn(`  [Planner] attack blocked: target not adjacent (dist=${dist})`);
                    // Идём к цели шагом
                    const nextStep = this._chooseNextStep(gen, decision.tile, factionId);
                    if (nextStep && this._doStep(gen, nextStep, factionId, steps)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    // Не можем — выходим
                    break;
                }
                if (dist > gen.movePoints) {
                    console.warn(`  [Planner] attack too far, break`);
                    break;
                }
                const fromHex = gen.hex;
                gen.spendMove(dist);
                let defenderFactionId = 'goths';
                const enemyGen = this.world.getGeneralAt(decision.tile.hex);
                if (enemyGen) {
                    defenderFactionId = enemyGen.factionId;
                } else if (decision.tile.cityId) {
                    const city = this.world.getCity(decision.tile.cityId);
                    if (city?.settlement) {
                        defenderFactionId = city.neutralFaction || 'tauri';
                    } else if (city?.factionId) {
                        defenderFactionId = city.factionId;
                    }
                }
                steps.push({
                    generalId: gen.id,
                    general: gen,
                    fromHex,
                    toHex: decision.tile.hex,
                    action: decision.kind === 'attack' ? 'attack' : 'attack-city',
                    targetTile: decision.tile,
                    defenderFactionId,
                    factionId,
                });
                gen.movePoints = 0;
                break;
            }
            // ─── ДВИЖЕНИЕ ───
            if (decision.kind === 'move') {
                if (decision.tile.hex.key === gen.hex.key) {
                    console.warn(`  [Planner] decision on self, using fallback`);
                    const fallback = this._anyMove(gen, factionId);
                    if (!fallback) {
                        console.warn(`  [Planner] no fallback, break`);
                        break;
                    }
                    if (!this._doStep(gen, fallback, factionId, steps)) break;
                    continue;
                }
                const nextStep = this._chooseNextStep(gen, decision.tile, factionId);
                if (!nextStep) {
                    console.warn(`  [Planner] no nextStep, using fallback`);
                    const fallback = this._anyMove(gen, factionId);
                    if (!fallback) break;
                    if (!this._doStep(gen, fallback, factionId, steps)) break;
                    continue;
                }
                if (!this._doStep(gen, nextStep, factionId, steps)) break;
                continue;
            }
            break;
        }
        return steps;
    }
    _doStep(gen, step, factionId, steps) {
        if (!step) return false;
        if (step.cost > gen.movePoints) {
            console.log(`  [Planner] _doStep: cost ${step.cost} > mp ${gen.movePoints}`);
            return false;
        }
        if (step.tile.hex.key === gen.hex.key) {
            console.log(`  [Planner] _doStep: same hex`);
            return false;
        }
        gen.spendMove(step.cost);
        const fromHex = gen.hex;
        const wasCaptured = this._captureTile(step.tile, factionId);
        gen.moveTo(step.tile.hex);
        console.log(`  [Planner] _doStep: ${fromHex.key} → ${step.tile.hex.key} captured=${wasCaptured}`);
        steps.push({
            generalId: gen.id,
            fromHex,
            toHex: step.tile.hex,
            action: 'move',
            targetTile: step.tile,
            captured: wasCaptured,
            factionId,
        });
        return true;
    }
    _chooseNextStep(gen, targetTile, factionId) {
        const { movement, map } = this.world;
        const candidates = [];
        for (const n of gen.hex.neighbors()) {
            const tile = map.get(n.q, n.r);
            if (!tile || !tile.isLand) continue;
            if (tile.hex.key === gen.hex.key) continue;
            const occ = this.world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
            if (occ && occ.id !== gen.id) continue;
            if (movement.isForeignCityTile(tile, factionId)) continue;
            const cost = movement.tileCost(tile);
            if (cost > gen.movePoints) continue;
            const dist = tile.hex.distance(targetTile.hex);
            candidates.push({ tile, cost, dist });
        }
        if (candidates.length === 0) return null;
        const direct = candidates.find((c) => c.tile.key === targetTile.key);
        if (direct) return direct;
        candidates.sort((a, b) => (a.dist - b.dist) || (a.cost - b.cost));
        return candidates[0];
    }
    _anyMove(gen, factionId) {
        const { movement, map } = this.world;
        const candidates = [];
        for (const n of gen.hex.neighbors()) {
            const tile = map.get(n.q, n.r);
            if (!tile || !tile.isLand) continue;
            if (tile.hex.key === gen.hex.key) continue;
            const occ = this.world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
            if (occ && occ.id !== gen.id) continue;
            if (movement.isForeignCityTile(tile, factionId)) continue;
            const cost = movement.tileCost(tile);
            if (cost > gen.movePoints) continue;
            let prio = 3;
            if (!tile.factionId) prio = 0;
            else if (tile.factionId !== factionId) prio = 1;
            candidates.push({ tile, cost, prio });
        }
        if (candidates.length === 0) return null;
        candidates.sort((a, b) => (a.prio - b.prio) || (a.cost - b.cost));
        return candidates[0];
    }
    _emergencyMove(gen, factionId) {
        const steps = [];
        const candidates = [];
        for (const n of gen.hex.neighbors()) {
            const tile = this.world.map.get(n.q, n.r);
            if (!tile || !tile.isLand) continue;
            if (tile.hex.key === gen.hex.key) continue;
            const occ = this.world.getGeneralAt(tile.hex);
            if (occ && occ.id !== gen.id) continue;
            const cost = this.world.movement.tileCost(tile);
            if (cost > gen.movePoints) continue;
            let prio;
            if (!tile.factionId) prio = 0;
            else if (tile.factionId !== factionId) prio = 1;
            else prio = 2;
            const city = tile.cityId ? this.world.getCity(tile.cityId) : null;
            if (city && city.factionId === factionId) prio = 3;
            candidates.push({ tile, cost, prio });
        }
        if (candidates.length === 0) {
            console.warn(`[Planner] _emergencyMove: no candidates for ${gen.id} at ${gen.hex.key}`);
            return steps;
        }
        candidates.sort((a, b) => a.prio - b.prio || a.cost - b.cost);
        const best = candidates[0];
        console.log(`[Planner] EMERGENCY MOVE: ${gen.id} ${gen.hex.key} → ${best.tile.key}`);
        gen.spendMove(best.cost);
        const fromHex = gen.hex;
        const wasCaptured = this._captureTile(best.tile, factionId);
        gen.moveTo(best.tile.hex);
        steps.push({
            generalId: gen.id,
            fromHex,
            toHex: best.tile.hex,
            action: 'move',
            targetTile: best.tile,
            captured: wasCaptured,
            factionId,
        });
        return steps;
    }
    _findBuySettlementDecision(strategy, factionId) {
        try {
            const d = strategy.decide({
                world: this.world, factionId, opts: { skipInstant: false },
            });
            return d.kind === 'buy-settlement' ? d : null;
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('[Planner] _findBuySettlementDecision CRASH:', e);
            return null;
        }
    }
    _captureTile(tile, factionId) {
        return this.world.captureTile(tile, factionId);
    }
}

📄 domain\ai\TurnManager.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { BotTurnPlanner } from './BotTurnPlanner.js';
/**
 * Менеджер ходов. Боты ходят пошагово.
 */
export class TurnManager {
    constructor({ world, bus }) {
        this.world = world;
        this.bus = bus;
        this.planner = new BotTurnPlanner({ world });
        this.isRunning = false;
    }
    async runAITurns(aiFactionIds, callbacks = {}) {
        console.log('=== TurnManager.runAITurns() ===');
        console.log('  aiFactionIds:', aiFactionIds, 'isRunning:', this.isRunning);
        if (this.isRunning) {
            console.warn('  FORCE RESET isRunning');
            this.isRunning = false;
        }
        this.isRunning = true;
        try {
            for (const factionId of aiFactionIds) {
                const faction = this.world.factions.find((f) => f.id === factionId);
                if (!faction || faction.eliminated) {
                    console.log(`  skip ${factionId}: eliminated=${faction?.eliminated}`);
                    continue;
                }
                console.log(`  ▶ faction ${factionId} start`);
                callbacks.onAITurnStart?.(factionId);
                await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
                const plan = this.planner.planForFaction(factionId);
                console.log(`  faction ${factionId}: plan length=${plan.length}`, plan);
                for (const step of plan) {
                    console.log(`    step:`, step.action, step.fromHex?.key, '→', step.toHex?.key);
                    callbacks.onAIStep?.(step);
                    this.bus.emit('ai:step', step);
                    const delay = (step.action === 'recruit' || step.action === 'hire-general') ? 200 : 280;
                    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delay));
                }
                const battleStep = plan.find(
                    (s) => s.action === 'attack' || s.action === 'attack-city'
                );
                if (battleStep) {
                    console.log(`  faction ${factionId}: battle step found`);
                    callbacks.onAITurnEnd?.({ factionId, battle: battleStep });
                    return battleStep;
                }
                callbacks.onAITurnEnd?.({ factionId });
                this.bus.emit('ai:turnEnded', { factionId });
                console.log(`  ◀ faction ${factionId} end`);
            }
            return null;
        } finally {
            this.isRunning = false;
            console.log('=== TurnManager.runAITurns() END ===');
        }
    }
}

📄 domain\battle\Ability.js

import { BattleEvents } from './BattleEvents.js';
/**
 * Способность карты. Срабатывает на триггер, если condition выполнена.
 *
 * Поля:
 *  - id, name, desc
 *  - trigger: одно из BattleEvents
 *  - condition(ctx): boolean  — проверка в момент срабатывания
 *  - effect(ctx): void        — что делает (может менять карты, полководцев)
 *  - onlyFor: 'self' | 'ally' | 'enemy' | 'any'  — к кому применяется
 *  - once: boolean           — срабатывает один раз за бой
 */
export class Ability {
  constructor({ id, name, desc, trigger, condition, effect, onlyFor = 'self', once = false }) {
    this.id = id;
    this.name = name;
    this.desc = desc;
    this.trigger = trigger;
    this.condition = condition || (() => true);
    this.effect = effect;
    this.onlyFor = onlyFor;
    this.once = once;
    this._used = false;
  }
  shouldFire(ctx) {
    if (this.once && this._used) return false;
    if (ctx.event !== this.trigger) return false;
    return this.condition(ctx);
  }
  fire(ctx) {
    if (!this.shouldFire(ctx)) return false;
    this.effect(ctx);
    if (this.once) this._used = true;
    return true;
  }
  reset() { this._used = false; }
}

    trigger: BattleEvents.CARD_PLAYED,

  lastStand: () => new Ability({
    id: 'lastStand', name: 'Последний рубеж',
    desc: 'Когда эта карта при смерти (1 HP) получает урон, наносит 2 урона атакующему.',
    trigger: BattleEvents.CARD_DAMAGED,
    condition: (ctx) =>
      ctx.payload.card.id === ctx.self.id &&
      ctx.payload.source &&
      ctx.payload.source.side !== ctx.side &&
      ctx.self.str === 1,
    effect: (ctx) => { ctx.battle.damageCard(ctx.payload.source, 2, ctx.self); },
  }),
};
export class AbilityFactory {
  static create(id) {
    const def = DEFS[id];
    if (!def) return null;
    return def();
  }
  static allIds() { return Object.keys(DEFS); }
  static describe(id) {
    const a = DEFS[id] ? DEFS[id]() : null;
    if (!a) return null;
    return { id: a.id, name: a.name, desc: a.desc };
  }
}

📄 domain\battle\Battle.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { BattleBoard } from './BattleBoard.js';
import { CardInstance } from './CardInstance.js';
import { AbilityFactory } from './AbilityFactory.js';
import { BattleEvents } from './BattleEvents.js';
import { LineBonus } from './LineBonus.js';
import { ColumnBonus } from './ColumnBonus.js';
import { GeneralDamageRule } from './GeneralDamageRule.js';
import { TerrainBattleModifier } from './TerrainBattleModifier.js';
export class Battle {
  constructor({
    attackerGen, defenderGen,
    attackerFaction, defenderFaction,
    targetTile, attackerDeck, defenderDeck,
    playerSide = 'attacker',
    maxIdleTurns = 3,
  }) {
    this.attackerGen = attackerGen;
    this.defenderGen = defenderGen;
    this.attackerFaction = attackerFaction;
    this.defenderFaction = defenderFaction;
    this.targetTile = targetTile;
    this.playerSide = playerSide;
    // ─── Кто сейчас «активный» игрок. Для hot-seat переключается ───
    this.currentHumanSide = playerSide;
    // ─── Владение ходом: 'human' | 'ai'. Источник истины «чей сейчас ход».
    // Пересчитывается сам при каждой смене хода (см. _endTurn)
    // и при установке humanSides (см. сеттер ниже) ───
    this._humanSides = null;
    this.turnOwnership = 'human';
    this.maxIdleTurns = maxIdleTurns;
    this._aiInstance = null;
    this.terrain = new TerrainBattleModifier(targetTile?.terrain || 'plain');
    this.attacker = this._makeSide(attackerGen, attackerDeck || []);
    this.defender = this._makeSide(defenderGen, defenderDeck || []);
    this._shuffleDeck(this.attacker);
    this._shuffleDeck(this.defender);
    this.lineBonus = { attacker: new LineBonus(), defender: new LineBonus() };
    this.columnBonus = { attacker: new ColumnBonus(), defender: new ColumnBonus() };
    this.generalDamage = { attacker: new GeneralDamageRule(), defender: new GeneralDamageRule() };
    this.turn = 'attacker';
    this.turnCount = 1;
    this.idleTurns = 0;
    this.log = [];
    this.finished = false;
    this.winner = null;
    this.selectedCard = null;
    this.onEvent = null;
    this.onCardDied = null;
    // ─── Счётчик «без урона» (для правила 3 раунда) ───
    this._noDamageTurns = 0;
    this._drawOpeningHand('attacker', 1);
    this._drawOpeningHand('defender', 1);
    this._refreshTactic('attacker');
    this._refreshTactic('defender');
    this._checkLineBonus();
    this._checkColumnBonus();
    this._emit(BattleEvents.TURN_STARTED, { side: this.turn, turn: this.turnCount });
  }
  // ─── Владение ходом ───
  set humanSides(map) {
    this._humanSides = map || { attacker: false, defender: false };
    this._updateOwnership();
  }
  get humanSides() { return this._humanSides; }
  /** Пересчитать владельца текущего хода из humanSides. */
  _updateOwnership() {
    this.turnOwnership = this._humanSides?.[this.turn] === true ? 'human' : 'ai';
  }
  _makeSide(gen, deck) {
    return {
      gen,
      lives: gen.lives,
      maxLives: gen.maxLives,
      board: new BattleBoard(),
      hand: [],
      deck: deck.map((c) => this._makeCard(c)),
    };
  }
  _makeCard(card) {
    if (card instanceof CardInstance) return card;
    const instance = new CardInstance(card);
    const keys = card.abilityKeys || [];
    instance.abilities = keys.map((k) => AbilityFactory.create(k)).filter(Boolean);
    return instance;
  }
  _shuffleDeck(sideData) {
    const arr = sideData.deck;
    for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
    }
  }
  _drawOpeningHand(side, count) {
    const data = this[side];
    if (!data || !data.deck) return;
    const hand = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count && data.deck.length > 0; i++) {
      hand.push(data.deck.pop());
    }
    data.hand = hand;
  }
  _drawToHand(side) {
    const d = this[side];
    if (!d || d.deck.length === 0) return null;
    const card = d.deck.pop();
    d.hand.push(card);
    return card;
  }
  _refreshTactic(side) {
    const d = this[side];
    const supportCount = d.board.count('support');
    d.tacticPoints = 1 + supportCount + 1;
  }
  costFor(card, side) {
    return this.terrain.effectiveCost(card, side);
  }
  // ─── Геттеры ───
  // Ход «активен для человека» = текущий ход принадлежит человеческой
  // стороне (по turnOwnership, а не по currentHumanSide — это поле
  // теперь про перспективу UI, а не про допуск ИИ).
  get isActiveTurn() { return this.turnOwnership === 'human' && !this.finished; }
  get isPlayerTurn() { return this.isActiveTurn; }
  get playerData() { return this[this.currentHumanSide]; }
  get enemyData() { return this[this._otherSide(this.currentHumanSide)]; }
  get enemySide() { return this._otherSide(this.currentHumanSide); }
  // ─── Действия ───
  selectCard(card) {
    if (!this.isActiveTurn) return false;
    if (this.costFor(card, this.currentHumanSide) > this.playerData.tacticPoints) return false;
    this.selectedCard = this.selectedCard === card ? null : card;
    return true;
  }
  playCard(card, lane, index) {
    if (!this.isActiveTurn) return false;
    const side = this.currentHumanSide;
    const cost = this.costFor(card, side);
    if (cost > this[side].tacticPoints) return false;
    const board = this[side].board;
    if (!board.place(lane, index, card)) return false;
    this[side].tacticPoints -= cost;
    this[side].hand = this[side].hand.filter((c) => c.id !== card.id);
    this.selectedCard = null;
    this._log(`${card.name} → ${laneLabel(lane)} (-${cost}⚡)`);
    this._emit(BattleEvents.CARD_PLAYED, { card, side, lane, index });
    this._checkLineBonus();
    this._checkColumnBonus();
    this._checkVictory();
    return true;
  }
  endPlayerTurn() {
    if (!this.isActiveTurn) return false;
    this.selectedCard = null;
    this._endTurn(this.currentHumanSide);
    return true;
  }
  skipTurn() {
    if (!this.isActiveTurn) return false;
    this.idleTurns++;
    this.selectedCard = null;
    this._log('Игрок пропустил ход');
    this._endTurn(this.currentHumanSide);
    return true;
  }
  _endTurn(side) {
    this._emit(BattleEvents.TURN_ENDED, { side });
    this._applyTurnEndEffects(side);
    if (this.finished) return;
    // ─── Правило «3 раунда без урона → победа защитника» ───
    // Один раунд = 2 хода (attacker + defender), поэтому * 2.
    this._noDamageTurns = (this._noDamageTurns || 0) + 1;
    if (this._noDamageTurns >= this.maxIdleTurns * 2) {
      this.finished = true;
      this.winner = 'defender';
      this._log(`Никто не наносил урон ${this.maxIdleTurns} раунда — победа защитника`);
      this._emit(BattleEvents.BATTLE_FINISHED, { winner: this.winner });
      return;
    }
    this.turn = this._otherSide(side);
    if (this.turn === 'attacker') this.turnCount++;
    // ─── владение ходом переключается вместе с ходом ───
    this._updateOwnership();
    this._refreshTactic(this.turn);
    this.generalDamage[this.turn].resetTurn();
    this.columnBonus[this.turn].resetTurn();
    this._applyTurnStartEffects(this.turn);
    this._checkLineBonus();
    this._checkColumnBonus();
    this._checkVictory();
    if (this.finished) return;
    const drawn = this._drawToHand(this.turn);
    if (drawn) this._emit(BattleEvents.CARD_DRAWN, { side: this.turn, card: drawn });
    this._emit(BattleEvents.TURN_STARTED, { side: this.turn, turn: this.turnCount });
  }
  _otherSide(side) { return side === 'attacker' ? 'defender' : 'attacker'; }
  aiTurn() {
    if (this.finished) return;
    // Ход человека — ИИ не вмешивается. Раньше эта проверка была мёртвым
    // кодом (this._humanTurn нигде не выставлялся), теперь она настоящая.
    if (this.turnOwnership === 'human') return;
    if (this._aiInstance) {
      try { this._aiInstance.run(); } catch (e) { console.error('[aiTurn]', e); }
      return;
    }
    // fallback
    const side = this.turn;
    this._refreshTactic(side);
    this._aiPlayType(side, 'support', 'support');
    this._aiPlayType(side, 'warrior', 'defense');
    this._aiPlayType(side, ['archer', 'special'], 'attack');
    this._endTurn(side);
  }
  _aiPlayType(side, types, lane) {
    const d = this[side];
    const typeArr = Array.isArray(types) ? types : [types];
    const card = d.hand.find((c) => typeArr.includes(c.type) && this.costFor(c, side) <= d.tacticPoints);
    if (!card) return;
    const cost = this.costFor(card, side);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      if (d.board.isEmpty(lane, i)) {
        d.tacticPoints -= cost;
        d.board.place(lane, i, card);
        d.hand = d.hand.filter((c) => c.id !== card.id);
        this._log(`Враг: ${card.name} → ${laneLabel(lane)} (-${cost}⚡)`);
        this._emit(BattleEvents.CARD_PLAYED, { card, side, lane, index: i });
        this._checkLineBonus();
        this._checkColumnBonus();
        return;
      }
    }
  }
  autoPlay(onStep, stepDelayMs = 220) {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const step = () => {
        if (this.finished) { resolve(this.winner); return; }
        this._aiTurnForSide(this.turn);
        if (this.finished) { onStep?.(); resolve(this.winner); return; }
        onStep?.();
        setTimeout(step, stepDelayMs);
      };
      setTimeout(step, stepDelayMs);
    });
  }
  _aiTurnForSide(side) {
    try {
      this._refreshTactic(side);
      this._aiPlayType(side, 'support', 'support');
      this._aiPlayType(side, 'warrior', 'defense');
      this._aiPlayType(side, ['archer', 'special'], 'attack');
      this._endTurn(side);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[autoPlay]', e);
      try { this._endTurn(side); } catch (_) { }
    }
  }
  _emit(event, payload) {
    if (this.onEvent) {
      try { this.onEvent(event, payload); } catch (e) { console.error('[Battle.onEvent]', e); }
    }
    const ctxBase = { battle: this, event, payload };
    for (const side of ['attacker', 'defender']) {
      const board = this[side].board;
      for (const card of board.allCards()) {
        for (const ability of card.abilities) {
          const ctx = { ...ctxBase, self: card, side };
          try { ability.fire(ctx); } catch (e) { }
        }
      }
    }
    if (event === BattleEvents.LANE_FULL) this._activateLineBonus(payload);
    if (event === BattleEvents.CARD_DIED) this._activateColumnBonus(payload);
    // ─── Любой урон сбрасывает счётчик застоя ───
    if (event === BattleEvents.CARD_DAMAGED || event === BattleEvents.GENERAL_DAMAGED) {
      const amount = payload?.amount || 0;
      if (amount > 0) this._noDamageTurns = 0;
    }
  }
  _activateLineBonus({ side, lane }) {
    const lb = this.lineBonus[side];
    if (!lb.active[lane]) return;
    if (lane === 'support') {
      const board = this[side].board;
      for (const card of board.allCards()) {
        this.healCard(card, 1, null);
      }
    }
  }
  _activateColumnBonus({ side, card }) {
    const cb = this.columnBonus[side];
    if (!cb.active[card.index]) return;
    if (side === this.currentHumanSide) {
      this._log(`Колонка ${card.index + 1} активирована — нужно заменить карту`);
    } else {
      this._aiForceReplace(side, card.index);
    }
  }
  _aiForceReplace(side, columnIndex) {
    const d = this[side];
    const card = d.hand[0];
    if (!card) return;
    const cost = this.costFor(card, side);
    for (const lane of ['defense', 'attack', 'support']) {
      if (d.board.isEmpty(lane, columnIndex)) {
        if (cost > d.tacticPoints && !this.columnBonus[side].canUseCredit()) return;
        if (cost > d.tacticPoints) {
          this.columnBonus[side].markCreditUsed();
          this._log(`Враг в кредит ставит ${card.name}`);
        } else {
          d.tacticPoints -= cost;
        }
        d.board.place(lane, columnIndex, card);
        d.hand = d.hand.filter((c) => c.id !== card.id);
        this._emit(BattleEvents.CARD_PLAYED, { card, side, lane, index: columnIndex });
        return;
      }
    }
  }
  _checkLineBonus() {
    for (const side of ['attacker', 'defender']) {
      const prev = { ...this.lineBonus[side].active };
      const curr = this.lineBonus[side].check(this[side].board);
      for (const lane of ['attack', 'defense', 'support']) {
        if (!prev[lane] && curr[lane]) {
          this._log(`${sideLabel(side)}: ${laneLabel(lane)} заполнена`);
          this._emit(BattleEvents.LANE_FULL, { side, lane });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  _checkColumnBonus() {
    for (const side of ['attacker', 'defender']) {
      const prev = [...this.columnBonus[side].active];
      this.columnBonus[side].check(this[side].board);
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        if (!prev[i] && this.columnBonus[side].active[i]) {
          this._log(`${sideLabel(side)}: колонка ${i + 1} заполнена`);
          this._emit(BattleEvents.COLUMN_FULL, { side, index: i });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  _applyTurnStartEffects(side) {
    const regen = this.lineBonus[side].getRegenPerTurn();
    if (regen > 0) {
      for (const card of this[side].board.allCards()) {
        this.healCard(card, regen, null);
      }
    }
  }
  _applyTurnEndEffects(side) {
    const dmg = this.lineBonus[side].getGeneralDamagePerTurn();
    if (dmg > 0) {
      this.damageGeneral(this._otherSide(side), dmg);
    }
    const opp = this[this._otherSide(side)];
    for (const card of opp.board.allCards()) {
      if (card.statuses?.poison) {
        this.damageCard(card, 1, null);
        card.statuses.poison--;
        if (card.statuses.poison <= 0) delete card.statuses.poison;
      }
      if (card.statuses?.burn) {
        this.damageCard(card, 1, null);
        card.statuses.burn--;
        if (card.statuses.burn <= 0) delete card.statuses.burn;
      }
    }
  }
  randomEnemyCard(side, lane) {
    const opp = this[this._otherSide(side)];
    const cards = opp.board.lines[lane].filter((c) => c);
    if (cards.length === 0) return null;
    return cards[Math.floor(Math.random() * cards.length)];
  }
  damageCard(card, amount, source = null) {
    if (!card || !card.alive) return;
    const before = card.str;
    card.takeDamage(amount);
    const actual = before - card.str;
    this._emit(BattleEvents.CARD_DAMAGED, { card, amount: actual, source });
    if (!card.alive) {
      const side = this._cardSide(card);
      if (side) {
        const board = this[side].board;
        board.remove(card);
        this._log(`${card.name} пал`);
        this._emit(BattleEvents.CARD_DIED, { card, side });
        if (side === this.currentHumanSide && this.onCardDied) {
          try { this.onCardDied(card); } catch (e) { console.error('[Battle.onCardDied]', e); }
        }
        this._checkColumnBonus();
      }
    }
    this._checkVictory();
  }
  healCard(card, amount, source = null) {
    if (!card || !card.alive) return;
    const before = card.str;
    card.heal(amount);
    const actual = card.str - before;
    if (actual > 0) {
      this._emit(BattleEvents.CARD_HEALED, { card, amount: actual, source });
    }
  }
  addStatus(card, kind, turns) {
    if (!card) return;
    card.statuses = card.statuses || {};
    card.statuses[kind] = (card.statuses[kind] || 0) + turns;
    this._log(`${card.name}: ${statusLabel(kind)}`);
    this._emit(BattleEvents.STATUS_ADDED, { card, kind, turns });
  }
  damageGeneral(side, amount) {
    const data = this[side];
    const rule = this.generalDamage[side];
    const lb = this.lineBonus[side];
    const board = data.board;
    if (!rule.canDamage(board, lb)) return false;
    data.lives = Math.max(0, data.lives - amount);
    rule.markDamaged();
    this._log(`${sideLabel(side)}: -${amount} жизнь полководца`);
    this._emit(BattleEvents.GENERAL_DAMAGED, { side, amount });
    this._checkVictory();
    return true;
  }
  _cardSide(card) {
    if (this.attacker.board.allCards().includes(card)) return 'attacker';
    if (this.defender.board.allCards().includes(card)) return 'defender';
    return null;
  }
  _checkVictory() {
    if (!this.attacker?.gen?.alive && !this.defender?.gen?.alive) return;
    if (this.attacker.lives <= 0) { this.finished = true; this.winner = 'defender'; }
    else if (this.defender.lives <= 0) { this.finished = true; this.winner = 'attacker'; }
    if (this.finished) {
      this._regenSurvivors();
      this._emit(BattleEvents.BATTLE_FINISHED, { winner: this.winner });
    }
  }
  _regenSurvivors() {
    for (const side of ['attacker', 'defender']) {
      for (const card of this[side].board.allCards()) {
        card.str = card.baseStr;
      }
    }
  }
  _log(text) {
    this.log.push({ msg: text, type: 'info' });
    if (this.log.length > 40) this.log.shift();
  }
  getLineBonusInfoFor(side) {
    const lb = this.lineBonus[side];
    return {
      attack: { active: lb.active.attack, desc: 'Полный ряд атаки: 1 урон полководцу врага каждый ход' },
      defense: { active: lb.active.defense, desc: 'Полный ряд обороны: блокирует обилки врага' },
      support: { active: lb.active.support, desc: 'Полный ряд поддержки: реген +1 каждый ход' },
    };
  }
  getLineBonusInfo() { return this.getLineBonusInfoFor(this.currentHumanSide); }
  getColumnBonusInfoFor(side) {
    return this.columnBonus[side].active.map((active, i) => ({
      index: i,
      active,
      desc: active
        ? `Колонка ${i + 1} полная: при уничтожении карты — обязательная замена`
        : `Колонка ${i + 1}: заполните все 3 ряда, чтобы активировать`,
    }));
  }
  getColumnBonusInfo() { return this.getColumnBonusInfoFor(this.currentHumanSide); }
}
function laneLabel(lane) {
  return { defense: 'Оборону', attack: 'Атаку', support: 'Поддержку' }[lane] || lane;
}
function sideLabel(side) {
  return side === 'attacker' ? 'Атакующий' : 'Защитник';
}
function statusLabel(kind) {
  return { poison: 'Яд', burn: 'Горение', freeze: 'Заморозка' }[kind] || kind;
}

📄 domain\battle\BattleAI.js

export class BattleAI {
    constructor(battle) {
      this.battle = battle;
    }
    /** Мгновенный ход — используется в autoPlay/autoResolve. */
    run() {
      const b = this.battle;
      if (!b || b.finished) return;
      const side = b.turn;
      b._refreshTactic(side);
      try {
        let placedAny = true;
        let safety = 12;
        while (placedAny && safety-- > 0) {
          placedAny = false;
          if (this._tryKillLowHp(side)) { placedAny = true; continue; }
          if (this._tryFillLane(side, 'attack')) { placedAny = true; continue; }
          if (this._tryFillLane(side, 'defense')) { placedAny = true; continue; }
          if (this._tryFillLane(side, 'support')) { placedAny = true; continue; }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('[BattleAI.run]', e);
      } finally {
        b._endTurn(side);
      }
    }
    /**
     * Пошаговый ИИ: ровно одна карта за такт.
     * Возвращает true, если сыграл карту; false — если ходить больше нечем.
     */
    _stepSingle() {
      const b = this.battle;
      if (!b || b.finished) return false;
      const side = b.turn;
      b._refreshTactic(side);
      if (this._tryKillLowHp(side)) return true;
      if (this._tryFillLane(side, 'attack')) return true;
      if (this._tryFillLane(side, 'defense')) return true;
      if (this._tryFillLane(side, 'support')) return true;
      return false;
    }
    _tryKillLowHp(side) {
      const b = this.battle;
      const enemySide = b._otherSide(side);
      const enemyBoard = b[enemySide].board;
      let hasLowHp = false;
      for (const lane of ['defense', 'attack', 'support']) {
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          const c = enemyBoard.get(lane, i);
          if (c && c.str === 1) { hasLowHp = true; break; }
        }
        if (hasLowHp) break;
      }
      if (!hasLowHp) return false;
      const hand = b[side].hand;
      const dmgCard = hand.find(
        (c) => c.abilityKeys?.includes('volley') || c.abilityKeys?.includes('burst')
      );
      if (!dmgCard) return false;
      if (b.costFor(dmgCard, side) > b[side].tacticPoints) return false;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        if (b[side].board.isEmpty('attack', i)) {
          const cost = b.costFor(dmgCard, side);
          b[side].tacticPoints -= cost;
          b[side].board.place('attack', i, dmgCard);
          b[side].hand = b[side].hand.filter((c) => c.id !== dmgCard.id);
          b._log(`ИИ: ${dmgCard.name} → Атака (добить)`);
          b._emit('card:played', { card: dmgCard, side, lane: 'attack', index: i });
          b._checkLineBonus();
          b._checkColumnBonus();
          return true;
        }
      }
      return false;
    }
    _tryFillLane(side, lane) {
      const b = this.battle;
      const d = b[side];
      if (d.board.isLineFull(lane)) return false;
      const priorities = {
        attack: ['archer', 'special', 'warrior'],
        defense: ['warrior', 'special', 'support'],
        support: ['support', 'special', 'archer'],
      }[lane] || [];
      for (const type of priorities) {
        const card = d.hand.find(
          (c) => c.type === type && b.costFor(c, side) <= d.tacticPoints
        );
        if (!card) continue;
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          if (d.board.isEmpty(lane, i)) {
            const cost = b.costFor(card, side);
            d.tacticPoints -= cost;
            d.board.place(lane, i, card);
            d.hand = d.hand.filter((c) => c.id !== card.id);
            b._log(`ИИ: ${card.name} → ${laneLabel(lane)} (-${cost}⚡)`);
            b._emit('card:played', { card, side, lane, index: i });
            b._checkLineBonus();
            b._checkColumnBonus();
            return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    }
  }
  function laneLabel(lane) {
    return { defense: 'Оборону', attack: 'Атаку', support: 'Поддержку' }[lane] || lane;
  }

📄 domain\battle\BattleBoard.js

import { CardInstance } from './CardInstance.js';
export const LANES = ['defense', 'attack', 'support'];
/**
 * Борт 3×3 (линии × 3 позиции). Знает только свою структуру.
 */
export class BattleBoard {
    constructor() {
        this.lines = {
            defense: [null, null, null],
            attack: [null, null, null],
            support: [null, null, null],
        };
        this._lineBonus = {};
    }
    get(lane, index) { return this.lines[lane]?.[index] ?? null; }
    allCards() {
        const out = [];
        for (const lane of LANES) {
            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                if (this.lines[lane][i]) out.push(this.lines[lane][i]);
            }
        }
        return out;
    }
    has(lane, index) { return !!this.lines[lane]?.[index]; }
    isEmpty(lane, index) { return this.lines[lane]?.[index] === null; }
    place(lane, index, card) {
        if (!LANES.includes(lane)) return false;
        if (index < 0 || index > 2) return false;
        if (this.lines[lane][index] !== null) return false;
        this.lines[lane][index] = card;
        card.lane = lane;
        card.index = index;
        return true;
    }
    remove(card) {
        if (!card || card.lane == null) return false;
        const { lane, index } = card;
        if (this.lines[lane]?.[index] === card) {
            this.lines[lane][index] = null;
            card.lane = null;
            card.index = -1;
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    isLineFull(lane) {
        return this.lines[lane].every((c) => c !== null);
    }
    isLineBonusApplied(lane) {
        return !!this._lineBonus[lane];
    }
    markLineBonus(lane) {
        this._lineBonus[lane] = true;
    }
    count(lane) {
        return this.lines[lane].filter((c) => c).length;
    }
}

/**
 * Все события боя. Единый реестр — чтобы не было опечаток.
 */
 export const BattleEvents = {
  CARD_PLAYED: 'card:played',           // { card, side, lane, index }
  CARD_DRAWN: 'card:drawn',             // { side, card }
  CARD_DAMAGED: 'card:damaged',         // { card, amount, source }
  CARD_HEALED: 'card:healed',           // { card, amount, source }
  CARD_DIED: 'card:died',               // { card, side }
  STATUS_ADDED: 'status:added',         // { card, kind, turns }
  LANE_FULL: 'lane:full',               // { side, lane }
  COLUMN_FULL: 'column:full',           // { side, index }
  GENERAL_DAMAGED: 'general:damaged',   // { side, amount }
  TURN_STARTED: 'turn:started',         // { side, turn }
  TURN_ENDED: 'turn:ended',             // { side }
  BATTLE_FINISHED: 'battle:finished',   // { winner }
};

📄 domain\battle\BattleFactory.js

import { Battle } from './Battle.js';
import { CardFactory } from '../card/CardFactory.js';
import { GeneralFactory } from '../general/GeneralFactory.js';
export class BattleFactory {
  static create({ attackerGen, targetTile, defenderFactionId, world, playerSide = 'attacker' }) {
    const targetCity = targetTile?.cityId ? world.getCity(targetTile.cityId) : null;
    let defenderGen = null;
    if (targetCity?.settlement) {
      defenderGen = world.getSettlementDefender(targetCity);
    } else {
      // ─── Ищем ЖИВОГО генерала фракции-защитника ───
      defenderGen = world.generals.find(
        (g) => g.factionId === defenderFactionId && g.alive
      );
      // ─── Если генерала нет — генерируем временного ───
      if (!defenderGen) {
        defenderGen = {
          id: 'def_' + Date.now(),
          name: GeneralFactory.nameForFaction(defenderFactionId) || 'Вождь',
          factionId: defenderFactionId,
          lives: 3,
          maxLives: 3,
          deck: { cards: [] },
        };
      }
    }
    if (!defenderGen) {
      defenderGen = {
        id: 'def_' + Date.now(),
        name: GeneralFactory.nameForFaction(defenderFactionId) || 'Вождь',
        factionId: defenderFactionId,
        lives: 3,
        maxLives: 3,
        presetIndex: 0,
        deck: { cards: [] },
      };
    }
    const attackerCards = attackerGen.deck?.cards || [];
    const attackerDeck = attackerCards.length > 0
      ? attackerCards.slice()
      : BattleFactory._fallbackDeck(attackerGen.factionId);
    const defenderCards = defenderGen.deck?.cards || [];
    const defenderDeck = defenderCards.length > 0
      ? defenderCards.slice()
      : BattleFactory._aiDeck(defenderFactionId);
    return new Battle({
      attackerGen, defenderGen,
      attackerFaction: attackerGen.factionId,
      defenderFaction: defenderFactionId,
      targetTile, attackerDeck, defenderDeck,
      playerSide,
    });
  }
  static _fallbackDeck(factionId) {
    const keys = CardFactory.keysForFaction(factionId);
    if (keys.length === 0) return [CardFactory.create('goth_warrior')];
    const cheap = keys.filter((k) => CardFactory.defForKey(k).str === 1);
    const pool = cheap.length > 0 ? cheap : keys;
    return [
      CardFactory.create(pool[0]),
      CardFactory.create(pool[0]),
      CardFactory.create(pool[0]),
    ];
  }
  static _aiDeck(factionId) {
    const keys = CardFactory.keysForFaction(factionId);
    if (keys.length === 0) return [CardFactory.create('goth_warrior')];
    const cheap = keys.filter((k) => CardFactory.defForKey(k).str === 1);
    const pool = cheap.length > 0 ? cheap : keys;
    const deck = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) deck.push(CardFactory.create(pool[i % pool.length]));
    return deck;
  }
}

📄 domain\battle\BattleRuleEngine.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { LANES } from './BattleBoard.js';
/**
 * Чистые правила боя. Никакого UI.
 */
export class BattleRuleEngine {
    canPlace(side, cardInstance, tacticPoints) {
        return cardInstance.str <= tacticPoints;
    }
    canTarget(card, lane) {
        if (card.type === 'archer' || card.type === 'special') return true;
        return lane === 'defense';
    }
    attackDamage(attacker, defender, lane) {
        let dmg = attacker.type === 'archer' ? 1
            : attacker.ability.includes('2 урона') ? 2
                : Math.max(1, attacker.str - 1);
        if (defender.ability.includes('-1 получаемый урон') && dmg > 1) dmg -= 1;
        return Math.max(1, dmg);
    }
    /**
     * Атака карты по цели. Возвращает { targetDead, counter, attackerDead }.
     */
    applyAttack(attacker, target, lane, board) {
        const dmg = this.attackDamage(attacker, target, lane);
        target.takeDamage(dmg);
        const targetDead = !target.alive;
        if (targetDead) board.remove(target);
        let counter = 0;
        let attackerDead = false;
        if (!targetDead && lane === 'defense') {
            counter = Math.max(1, target.str - 1);
            attacker.takeDamage(counter);
            attackerDead = !attacker.alive;
            if (attackerDead) board.remove(attacker);
        }
        return { dmg, targetDead, counter, attackerDead };
    }
    /**
     * Проверка бонуса линии. Возвращает { lane, kind } или null.
     */
    checkLineBonus(board, lane) {
        if (board.isLineBonusApplied(lane)) return null;
        if (!board.isLineFull(lane)) return null;
        board.markLineBonus(lane);
        if (lane === 'defense') return { lane, kind: 'life' };
        if (lane === 'attack') return { lane, kind: 'tactic' };
        return { lane, kind: 'tactic2' };
    }
    /** Случайная чужая карта (для способности «1 урон случайной»). */
    randomEnemyCard(board) {
        const all = board.allCards();
        if (all.length === 0) return null;
        return all[Math.floor(Math.random() * all.length)];
    }
    /** Урон случайной карте врага — с удалением, если погибла. */
    damageRandom(board, dmg) {
        const card = this.randomEnemyCard(board);
        if (!card) return null;
        card.takeDamage(dmg);
        if (!card.alive) board.remove(card);
        return card;
    }
}
export { LANES };

📄 domain\battle\CardInstance.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Карта в бою. Отличается от Card тем, что str может меняться
 * (баффы, урон). Вне боя — чистая Card.
 */
 export class CardInstance {
  constructor(card) {
    this.card = card;
    this.id = card.id + '_b' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
    this.key = card.key;
    this.name = card.name;
    this.type = card.type;
    this.prov = card.prov;
    this.ability = card.ability;
    this.abilityKeys = card.abilityKeys || [];
    this.imageKey = card.imageKey;
    this.baseStr = card.str;
    this.str = card.str;
    this.maxStr = card.str;
    this.bonus = 0;
    this.lane = null;
    this.index = -1;
    this.statuses = {};
    this.abilities = [];
  }
  get alive() { return this.str > 0; }
  addBonus(n) { this.bonus += n; this.str += n; this.maxStr += n; }
  takeDamage(n) { this.str = Math.max(0, this.str - n); return this.str; }
  heal(n) { this.str = Math.min(this.maxStr, this.str + n); return this.str; }
  clone() {
    const c = new CardInstance(this.card);
    c.str = this.str;
    c.maxStr = this.maxStr;
    c.bonus = this.bonus;
    return c;
  }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 domain\battle\ColumnBonus.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Бонус за полную колонку (3 карты в одной колонке — по одной в каждом ряду).
 * При уничтожении карты в колонке — обязательная замена (даже в кредит тактики, раз в ход).
 */
export class ColumnBonus {
    constructor() {
        this.active = [false, false, false];  // по индексу колонки
        this._usedCreditThisTurn = false;
    }
    check(board) {
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            this.active[i] =
                board.has('defense', i) &&
                board.has('attack', i) &&
                board.has('support', i);
        }
    }
    /** Актива бонуса по индексу колонки. */
    isActive(index) { return this.active[index]; }
    /** Может ли сейчас быть «кредит». */
    canUseCredit() { return !this._usedCreditThisTurn; }
    markCreditUsed() { this._usedCreditThisTurn = true; }
    resetTurn() { this._usedCreditThisTurn = false; }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 domain\battle\GeneralDamageRule.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Правило урона полководцу.
 * Урон возможен только:
 *  - раз в ход (не более 1)
 *  - если у стороны есть пустая колонка (нет карты хотя бы в 1 из 3 рядов)
 *  Исключения:
 *  - триггер обилки (damageLeader) — игнорирует
 *  - активна линия атаки — игнорирует пустоту
 */
export class GeneralDamageRule {
    constructor() {
        this._damagedThisTurn = false;
    }
    canDamage(board, lineBonus) {
        if (this._damagedThisTurn) return false;
        if (lineBonus?.active?.attack) return true;   // исключение: линия атаки
        return this.hasEmptyColumn(board);
    }
    hasEmptyColumn(board) {
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            const occupied =
                board.has('defense', i) ||
                board.has('attack', i) ||
                board.has('support', i);
            if (!occupied) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    markDamaged() { this._damagedThisTurn = true; }
    resetTurn() { this._damagedThisTurn = false; }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 domain\battle\LineBonus.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Бонус за полный ряд (3 карты в одном ряду).
 *  - атака:   1 урон полководцу врага каждый ход
 *  - оборона: блокирует обилки врага
 *  - поддержка: реген количества союзников каждый ход
 */
export class LineBonus {
    constructor() {
        this.active = { attack: false, defense: false, support: false };
    }
    check(board) {
        this.active.attack = board.isLineFull('attack');
        this.active.defense = board.isLineFull('defense');
        this.active.support = board.isLineFull('support');
        return this.active;
    }
    /** Урон полководцу в начале хода (если активна атака). */
    getGeneralDamagePerTurn() {
        return this.active.attack ? 1 : 0;
    }
    /** Реген всех карт в начале хода (если активна поддержка). */
    getRegenPerTurn() {
        return this.active.support ? 1 : 0;
    }
    /** Заблокированы ли обилки врага (если активна оборона). */
    isAbilityBlocked() {
        return this.active.defense;
    }
    /** Для UI: активен ли ряд и что даёт. */
    info(lane) {
        return {
            active: this.active[lane],
            desc: {
                attack: '1 урон полководцу каждый ход',
                defense: 'Блокирует обилки врага',
                support: 'Реген +1 количества каждый ход',
            }[lane],
        };
    }
}

📄 domain\battle\TerrainBattleModifier.js

/**
 * Террейн-модификаторы боя. Определяют:
 *  - доп. стоимость карты по типу в зависимости от террейна (штраф/скидка)
 *  - штрафы для нападающего в городе
 *  - визуальные подсказки для UI
 *
 * Террейны: forest, plain, city, mountain, steppe, ruins.
 * Типы карт: warrior, archer, support, special.
 */
const TERRAIN_RULES = {
    forest: {
        name: 'Лес',
        desc: 'Лучники скрытны, конные воины теряют мобильность',
        cost: {
            archer: -1,   // скидка
            warrior: 0,
            support: 0,
            special: +1,
        },
        note: 'Лес: лучники дешевле, спецы дороже',
    },
    plain: {
        name: 'Равнина',
        desc: 'Открытое поле — все сражаются на равных',
        cost: { archer: 0, warrior: 0, support: 0, special: 0 },
        note: 'Равнина: без модификаторов',
    },
    mountain: {
        name: 'Горы',
        desc: 'Конные и тяжёлые воины страдают, лучники на высоте',
        cost: {
            archer: 0,
            warrior: +1,   // тяжёлым тяжело
            support: 0,
            special: +1,
        },
        note: 'Горы: воины и спецы дороже',
    },
    steppe: {
        name: 'Степь',
        desc: 'Конные воины — короли степи, поддержка далеко',
        cost: {
            archer: +1,
            warrior: 0,
            support: +1,
            special: 0,
        },
        note: 'Степь: лучники и поддержка дороже',
    },
    city: {
        name: 'Город',
        desc: 'Улицы и стены: нападающий платит больше',
        cost: { archer: 0, warrior: 0, support: 0, special: 0 },
        note: 'Город: штраф нападающему',
        attackerPenalty: 1,   // +1 к стоимости каждой карты у атакующего
    },
    ruins: {
        name: 'Руины',
        desc: 'Обломки — все карты на 1 дороже',
        cost: { archer: +1, warrior: +1, support: +1, special: +1 },
        note: 'Руины: все карты дороже',
    },
};
export class TerrainBattleModifier {
    constructor(terrain = 'plain') {
        this.terrain = TERRAIN_RULES[terrain] ? terrain : 'plain';
        this.rules = TERRAIN_RULES[this.terrain];
    }
    /** Доп. стоимость карты для стороны (0 — без изменений). */
    costBonus(cardType, side) {
        let bonus = this.rules.cost?.[cardType] ?? 0;
        // В городе нападающий платит +1 за любую карту
        if (this.terrain === 'city' && side === 'attacker') {
            bonus += this.rules.attackerPenalty ?? 0;
        }
        return bonus;
    }
    /** Итоговая стоимость карты для стороны. */
    effectiveCost(card, side) {
        const base = card.str || 1;
        const b = this.costBonus(card.type, side);
        return Math.max(1, base + b);
    }
    /** Текст-подсказка для тултипа. */
    getNote(side = null) {
        let note = this.rules.note || '';
        if (this.terrain === 'city' && side === 'attacker') {
            note += ' (вы нападающий: +1 к каждой карте)';
        }
        if (this.terrain === 'city' && side === 'defender') {
            note += ' (вы защитник: штраф не действует)';
        }
        return note;
    }
    get name() { return this.rules.name; }
    get desc() { return this.rules.desc; }
}

📄 domain\card\Card.js

/**
 * Карта. Чистая сущность.
 * imageKey — ключ для загрузки картинки (см. config.js).
 */
 export class Card {
  constructor({ id, key, name, str, prov, type, ability, imageKey, abilityKeys = [], rarity = 'common' }) {
    this.id = id;
    this.key = key;
    this.name = name;
    this.str = str;
    this.prov = prov;
    this.type = type;
    this.ability = ability;
    this.abilityKeys = abilityKeys;
    this.imageKey = imageKey || key;
    this.rarity = rarity;
  }
  clone() { return new Card({ ...this }); }
}

let _nextId = 1;
export class CardFactory {
  static create(key) {
    const def = DEFS[key];
    if (!def) throw new Error(`Unknown card: ${key}`);
    return new Card({
      id: `card_${_nextId++}`,
      key,
      name: def.name,
      str: def.str,
      prov: def.prov,
      type: def.type,
      ability: def.ability,
      abilityKeys: def.abilityKeys,
      imageKey: key,
      rarity: def.rarity,
    });
  }
  static allKeys() { return Object.keys(DEFS); }
  static keysForFaction(factionId) {
    const prefix = {
      scythians: 'scyth_',
      bosporus: 'bosp_',
      chersonesos: 'cher_',
      rome: 'rome_',
      goths: 'goth_',
      tauri: 'tauri_',
      cimmerians: 'cimm_',
    }[factionId];
    if (!prefix) return [];
    return Object.keys(DEFS).filter((k) => k.startsWith(prefix));
  }
  static defForKey(key) { return DEFS[key] || null; }
}

📄 domain\card\Deck.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Колода полководца. Содержит карты.
 * Считает суммарную силу, лимит, умеет перемешивать.
 */
 export class Deck {
    constructor(capacity = 20) {
      this.capacity = capacity;
      this.cards = [];
    }
    get size() { return this.cards.length; }
    get totalStrength() { return this.cards.reduce((s, c) => s + c.str, 0); }
    get isFull() { return this.totalStrength >= this.capacity; }
    canAdd(card) { return this.totalStrength + card.str <= this.capacity; }
    add(card) {
      if (!this.canAdd(card)) return false;
      this.cards.push(card);
      return true;
    }
    remove(cardId) {
      const i = this.cards.findIndex((c) => c.id === cardId);
      if (i === -1) return null;
      return this.cards.splice(i, 1)[0];
    }
    clear() { this.cards = []; }
    shuffle() {
      for (let i = this.cards.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
        [this.cards[i], this.cards[j]] = [this.cards[j], this.cards[i]];
      }
      return this;
    }
    draw() { return this.cards.pop() || null; }
    clone() {
      const d = new Deck(this.capacity);
      d.cards = this.cards.map((c) => c.clone());
      return d;
    }
  }

📄 domain\city\City.js
