import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Один плоский диск тумана. Накладывается поверх гекса, если !isVisible.
 */
export class FogVisual {
  constructor(size) {
    const geo = new THREE.CircleGeometry(size * 0.96, 6);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: Theme.fog.color,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: Theme.fog.opacity,
      depthWrite: false,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide,
    });
    this.mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
    this.mesh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    this.mesh.rotation.z = Math.PI / 6;
    this.mesh.position.y = 1.0;
    this.mesh.renderOrder = 5;
    this.mesh.visible = false;
  }
  addTo(group) { group.add(this.mesh); }
  setVisible(v) { this.mesh.visible = v; }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\GeneralVisual.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
import {
  generalPortraitUrl,
  generalModelUrl,
  generalModelFallbackUrl,
} from '../../../config.js';
import { ModelLoader } from '../ModelLoader.js';
export class GeneralVisual {
  constructor(general, size) {
    this.general = general;
    this.size = size;
    this.group = new THREE.Group();
    this._portraitTexture = null;
    this._modelObj = null;
    this._disposed = false;
    this._build();
  }
  _build() {
    const { size, general } = this;
    const cfg = Theme.general;
    const color = Theme.faction[general.factionId] || 0xd4a94c;
    const shiftX = size * 0.35;
    const shiftZ = size * 0.15;
    // ─── Пьедестал ───
    const ped = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(size * 0.22, size * 0.26, size * 0.1, 12),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: cfg.pedestalColor, roughness: 0.6, metalness: 0.5,
        emissive: cfg.pedestalEmissive, emissiveIntensity: 0.3,
      }),
    );
    ped.position.set(shiftX, size * 0.05, shiftZ);
    ped.castShadow = true;
    ped.receiveShadow = true;
    this.group.add(ped);
    // ─── Цветной диск ───
    const disc = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(
        size * cfg.discRadius * 0.55,
        size * cfg.discRadius * 0.65,
        size * cfg.discHeight, 8,
      ),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color, emissive: color, emissiveIntensity: 0.5,
        roughness: 0.3, metalness: 0.7,
      }),
    );
    disc.position.set(shiftX, size * 0.18, shiftZ);
    disc.castShadow = true;
    this.group.add(disc);
    this._loadGeneralModel(general, size, shiftX, shiftZ);
    // ─── Портрет с дугами (круглый, сверху жизни, снизу движение) ───
    this._portrait = this._createPortraitSprite(general, size, shiftX, shiftZ);
    // ─── Кольцо выбора ───
    this._selRing = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.RingGeometry(size * 0.28, size * 0.34, 32),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: cfg.selectedGlow, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, depthWrite: false,
      }),
    );
    this._selRing.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    this._selRing.position.set(shiftX, size * 0.28, shiftZ);
    this._selRing.visible = false;
    this._selRing.renderOrder = 3;
    this.group.add(this._selRing);
    this._light = new THREE.PointLight(0xf5d57a, 0, size * 4);
    this._light.castShadow = false;
    this._light.decay = 0;
    this.group.add(this._light);
    this._hoverLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x58a6ff, 0, size * 3);
    this._hoverLight.position.set(shiftX, size * 0.4, shiftZ);
    this.group.add(this._hoverLight);
    this.group.traverse((c) => {
      if (c.isMesh || c.isSprite) {
        c.userData = c.userData || {};
        c.userData.general = general;
        c.userData.hoverable = 'general';
      }
    });
  }
  _loadGeneralModel(general, size, shiftX, shiftZ) {
    const presetIndex = this._presetIndexFor(general);
    const primaryUrl = generalModelUrl(presetIndex);
    const fallbackUrl = generalModelFallbackUrl();
    const tryLoad = (url, isFallback) => {
      return ModelLoader.load(url)
        .then((scene) => {
          if (this._disposed || !this.group) return;
          const inst = scene.clone(true);
          this._placeGeneralModel(inst, size, shiftX, shiftZ);
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          if (this._disposed || !this.group) return;
          if (!isFallback) {
            console.warn(`[GeneralVisual] ${url} not found, using fallback ${fallbackUrl}`);
            return tryLoad(fallbackUrl, true);
          }
          console.warn(`[GeneralVisual] fallback ${url} failed, using procedural`);
          const color = Theme.faction[general.factionId] || 0xd4a94c;
          this._addProceduralRider(general, size, shiftX, shiftZ, color);
        });
    };
    tryLoad(primaryUrl, false);
  }
  _placeGeneralModel(obj, size, shiftX, shiftZ) {
    obj.position.set(0, 0, 0);
    obj.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
    obj.scale.set(1, 1, 1);
    const box0 = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj);
    const sizeV = new THREE.Vector3();
    box0.getSize(sizeV);
    const targetHeight = size * 0.9;
    const scale = sizeV.y > 0.001 ? targetHeight / sizeV.y : 1;
    obj.scale.setScalar(scale);
    const box1 = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj);
    const center = new THREE.Vector3();
    box1.getCenter(center);
    obj.position.x += -center.x + shiftX;
    obj.position.z += -center.z + shiftZ;
    obj.position.y += (size * 0.22 - box1.min.y);
    obj.traverse((c) => {
      if (!c.isMesh) return;
      c.castShadow = true;
      c.receiveShadow = false;
      c.userData = c.userData || {};
      c.userData.general = this.general;
      c.userData.hoverable = 'general';
      const mats = Array.isArray(c.material) ? c.material : [c.material];
      for (const m of mats) {
        if (!m) continue;
        if ('metalness' in m && m.metalness > 0.4) m.metalness = 0.3;
        if ('roughness' in m && m.roughness < 0.4) m.roughness = 0.7;
        if ('emissiveIntensity' in m && m.emissiveIntensity > 0.1) {
          m.emissiveIntensity = 0.1;
        }
        m.needsUpdate = true;
      }
    });
    this.group.add(obj);
    this._modelObj = obj;
  }
  _addProceduralRider(general, size, shiftX, shiftZ, color) {
    const horseColor = 0x3a2010;
    const horseBody = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(size * 0.55, size * 0.22, size * 0.22),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: horseColor, roughness: 0.85 }),
    );
    horseBody.position.set(shiftX, size * 0.38, shiftZ);
    horseBody.castShadow = true;
    this.group.add(horseBody);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const leg = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(size * 0.03, size * 0.025, size * 0.22, 5),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: horseColor, roughness: 0.9 }),
      );
      const dx = (i < 2 ? -1 : 1) * size * 0.18;
      const dz = (i % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1) * size * 0.06;
      leg.position.set(shiftX + dx, size * 0.15, shiftZ + dz);
      leg.castShadow = true;
      this.group.add(leg);
    }
    const neck = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(size * 0.12, size * 0.24, size * 0.12),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: horseColor, roughness: 0.85 }),
    );
    neck.position.set(shiftX + size * 0.32, size * 0.5, shiftZ);
    neck.rotation.z = -0.4;
    neck.castShadow = true;
    this.group.add(neck);
    const head = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(size * 0.16, size * 0.1, size * 0.1),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: horseColor, roughness: 0.85 }),
    );
    head.position.set(shiftX + size * 0.42, size * 0.58, shiftZ);
    head.castShadow = true;
    this.group.add(head);
    const rider = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(size * 0.16, size * 0.2, size * 0.14),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness: 0.6, metalness: 0.3 }),
    );
    rider.position.set(shiftX, size * 0.62, shiftZ);
    rider.castShadow = true;
    this.group.add(rider);
    const swordPivot = new THREE.Group();
    swordPivot.position.set(shiftX + size * 0.12, size * 0.66, shiftZ);
    swordPivot.rotation.z = 0.4;
    const blade = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(size * 0.02, size * 0.28, size * 0.015),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xd0d0d0, roughness: 0.2, metalness: 0.9,
        emissive: 0x303030, emissiveIntensity: 0.2,
      }),
    );
    blade.position.y = size * 0.2;
    blade.castShadow = true;
    swordPivot.add(blade);
    this.group.add(swordPivot);
  }
  // ═══════════════ ПОРТРЕТ С ДУГАМИ ═══════════════
  _createPortraitSprite(general, size, shiftX, shiftZ) {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = 320;
    canvas.height = 320;
    this._portraitCanvas = canvas;
    this._portraitCtx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    this._portraitImg = null;
    const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
    texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    this._portraitTexture = texture;
    const url = generalPortraitUrl(general.factionId, this._presetIndexFor(general));
    if (url) {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        if (this._disposed) return;
        this._portraitImg = img;
        this._drawPortrait();
      };
      img.src = url;
    }
    this._drawPortrait();
    const sprite = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
      map: texture, transparent: true, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false,
    }));
    sprite.position.set(shiftX, size * 1.75, shiftZ);
    sprite.scale.set(size * 1.24, size * 1.24, 1);
    sprite.renderOrder = 10;
    sprite.userData = { general, hoverable: 'general' };
    this.group.add(sprite);
    return sprite;
  }
  /**
   * Перерисовать портрет и дуги. Вызывается извне при изменении
   * жизней/очков движения (SceneBuilder.updateGeneralBars).
   */
  updateBars() {
    if (this._disposed || !this._portraitCanvas) return;
    this._drawPortrait();
  }
  _drawPortrait() {
    const ctx = this._portraitCtx;
    if (!ctx) return;
    const g = this.general;   // ← ЭТА СТРОКА ПОТЕРЯЛАСЬ — теперь на месте
    const C = 160;            // центр
    const R_IMG = 72;         // радиус портрета
    const R_ARC = 110;        // радиус дуг
    const W_ARC = 18;         // толщина дуги
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 320, 320);
    // ─── Портрет: круг с обводкой ───
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(C, C, R_IMG, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.clip();
    if (this._portraitImg) {
      const img = this._portraitImg;
      const scale = Math.max((R_IMG * 2) / img.width, (R_IMG * 2) / img.height);
      const w = img.width * scale;
      const h = img.height * scale;
      ctx.drawImage(img, C - w / 2, C - h * 0.62, w, h);
    } else {
      const color = Theme.faction[g.factionId] || 0xd4a94c;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#' + color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 320, 320);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.font = 'bold 64px sans-serif';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.fillText((g.name || '?')[0] || '?', C, C);
    }
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(C, C, R_IMG, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.lineWidth = 4;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
    ctx.stroke();
    // ─── Верхняя дуга: жизни (ячейки) ───
    this._drawArcSegments(ctx, C, C, R_ARC, W_ARC, -Math.PI, 0,
      g.maxLives, g.lives, '#e74c3c');
    // ─── Нижняя дуга: очки движения (ячейки) ───
    this._drawArcSegments(ctx, C, C, R_ARC, W_ARC, 0, Math.PI,
      g.maxMovePoints, g.movePoints, '#58a6ff');
    if (this._portraitTexture) this._portraitTexture.needsUpdate = true;
  }
  /**
   * Дуга из ячеек. from..to — углы в радианах (0 = вправо, y вниз):
   * жизни: -PI..0 (верх), движение: 0..PI (низ).
   */
  _drawArcSegments(ctx, cx, cy, r, w, from, to, max, value, fill) {
    if (max <= 0) return;
    const span = to - from;
    const gap = Math.min(0.06, (span / max) * 0.25);
    for (let i = 0; i < max; i++) {
      const a0 = from + (span / max) * i + gap / 2;
      const a1 = from + (span / max) * (i + 1) - gap / 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1);
      ctx.lineWidth = w;
      ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
      ctx.strokeStyle = i < value ? fill : 'rgba(20,20,25,0.65)';
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    // Тонкая подложка-ободок под дугой
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, from, to);
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  _presetIndexFor(general) {
    if (typeof general.presetIndex === 'number') return general.presetIndex;
    return 0;
  }
  setPosition(x, z) {
    this.group.position.set(x, 0, z);
  }
  setSelected(isSelected) {
    this._selRing.visible = isSelected;
    this._light.intensity = isSelected ? 1.0 : 0;
  }
  setHovered(isHovered) {
    this._hoverLight.intensity = isHovered ? 1000.2 : 0;
  }
  addTo(scene) { scene.add(this.group); return this; }
  removeFrom(scene) {
    this._disposed = true;
    scene.remove(this.group);
  }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\HaloVisual.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { HALO_TYPES } from '../../../domain/world/haloConfig.js';
import { iconUrl } from '../../../config/icons.js';
/**
 * Визуал ореолов с линзой.
 *  - Границы по периметру ореола (толщина/стиль — как у BorderVisual)
 *  - Иконка типа ореола на якорном (центральном) гексе (SVG из public/icons/)
 *  - Цвет — halo.color (из HaloGenerator)
 */
export class HaloVisual {
  constructor(world, size) {
    this.world = world;
    this.size = size;
    this.group = new THREE.Group();
    this._edgesGroup = new THREE.Group();
    this._iconsGroup = new THREE.Group();
    this.group.add(this._edgesGroup);
    this.group.add(this._iconsGroup);
    this._disposed = false;
    this._svgImageCache = new Map();   // url → HTMLImageElement
    this._build();
  }
  _build() {
    if (!this.world) return;
    this.clear();
    const playerId = this.world.playerFactionId;
    for (const halo of this.world.halos) {
      const type = HALO_TYPES[halo.type];
      if (!type) continue;
      const tiles = this.world.haloTiles(halo);
      if (tiles.length === 0) continue;
      const color = new THREE.Color(halo.color ?? type.color);
      this._buildEdges(halo, tiles, color);
      this._buildIcon(halo, type, color, playerId);
    }
  }
  _buildEdges(halo, tiles, color) {
    const cellSet = new Set(halo.cells);
    const drawn = new Set();
    const y = 0.78;
    const thickness = this.size * 0.06;
    for (const tile of tiles) {
      if (!tile.isLand) continue;
      const corners = this._corners(tile);
      const neighbors = tile.hex.neighbors();
      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const n = neighbors[i];
        const nKey = `${n.q},${n.r}`;
        if (cellSet.has(nKey)) continue;
        const edge = this._edgeForNeighbor(tile, i, corners);
        if (!edge) continue;
        const key = this._edgeKey(edge);
        if (drawn.has(key)) continue;
        drawn.add(key);
        const dx = edge.b.x - edge.a.x;
        const dz = edge.b.z - edge.a.z;
        const len = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        if (len < 0.001) continue;
        const ang = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(len, thickness, thickness);
        const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
          color,
          transparent: true,
          opacity: 0.9,
        });
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        mesh.position.set(
          (edge.a.x + edge.b.x) / 2,
          y,
          (edge.a.z + edge.b.z) / 2,
        );
        mesh.rotation.y = -ang;
        this._edgesGroup.add(mesh);
      }
    }
  }
  _buildIcon(halo, type, color, playerId) {
    let anchorTile = null;
    if (halo.anchorCityId) {
      const city = this.world.getCity(halo.anchorCityId);
      if (city?.tileKey) anchorTile = this.world.map.getByKey(city.tileKey);
    }
    if (!anchorTile) {
      anchorTile = this.world.map.getByKey(halo.cells[0]);
    }
    if (!anchorTile) return;
    const { x, z } = anchorTile.hex.toWorld(this.size);
    const iconKey = `halo_${halo.type}`;
    const svgUrl = iconUrl(iconKey);
    // Создаём спрайт сразу с заглушкой (пустой canvas)
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = 128;
    canvas.height = 128;
    const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
    tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    const mat = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
      map: tex,
      transparent: true,
      depthTest: false,
      depthWrite: false,
    });
    const sprite = new THREE.Sprite(mat);
    sprite.position.set(x, this.size * 0.9, z);
    const scale = this.size * 0.45;
    sprite.scale.set(scale, scale, 1);
    sprite.renderOrder = 12;
    // ─── ВАЖНО: помечаем спрайт для InputController ───
    sprite.userData = {
      haloIcon: true,
      haloId: halo.id,
      haloType: halo.type,
      halo,               // ссылка на объект ореола
    };
    this._iconsGroup.add(sprite);
    // Нарисовать сразу (фон + обводка)
    this._drawIconBackground(canvas, color);
    tex.needsUpdate = true;
    // Асинхронно подгрузить SVG
    this._loadSvgImage(svgUrl).then((img) => {
      if (this._disposed) return;
      if (!img) return;
      this._drawIconWithSvg(canvas, color, img);
      tex.needsUpdate = true;
    }).catch(() => {});
  }
  /** Загрузка SVG как Image с кэшем. */
  _loadSvgImage(url) {
    if (!url) return Promise.resolve(null);
    if (this._svgImageCache.has(url)) {
      return Promise.resolve(this._svgImageCache.get(url));
    }
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
      img.onload = () => {
        this._svgImageCache.set(url, img);
        resolve(img);
      };
      img.onerror = () => {
        console.warn('[HaloVisual] SVG failed:', url);
        resolve(null);
      };
      img.src = url;
    });
  }
  /** Нарисовать только фон (круг + обводка) без иконки. */
  _drawIconBackground(canvas, color) {
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const SIZE = canvas.width;
    const cx = SIZE / 2;
    const cy = SIZE / 2;
    const r = SIZE * 0.42;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, SIZE, SIZE);
    // Тёмная подложка
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r + 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10, 6, 3, 0.85)';
    ctx.fill();
    // Цветной круг
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#' + color.getHexString();
    ctx.fill();
    // Золотая обводка
    ctx.lineWidth = 6;
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#d4a94c';
    ctx.stroke();
    // Тонкая внутренняя
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r - 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 243, 208, 0.6)';
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  /** Нарисовать фон + SVG-иконку внутри. */
  _drawIconWithSvg(canvas, color, img) {
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const SIZE = canvas.width;
    const cx = SIZE / 2;
    const cy = SIZE / 2;
    const r = SIZE * 0.42;
    // 1) Фон
    this._drawIconBackground(canvas, color);
    // 2) SVG по центру, во внутреннем радиусе
    const innerR = r * 0.72;
    const drawSize = innerR * 2;
    const dx = cx - drawSize / 2;
    const dy = cy - drawSize / 2;
    try {
      ctx.drawImage(img, dx, dy, drawSize, drawSize);
    } catch (e) {
      // Иногда SVG с внешними ссылками не рендерится — тихо игнорируем
      console.warn('[HaloVisual] drawImage failed:', e);
    }
  }
  _edgeForNeighbor(tile, neighborIdx, corners) {
    const center = tile.hex.toWorld(this.size);
    const n = tile.hex.neighbors()[neighborIdx];
    const nWorld = n.toWorld(this.size);
    const nx = nWorld.x - center.x;
    const nz = nWorld.z - center.z;
    const neighborAng = Math.atan2(nz, nx);
    let bestK = 0;
    let bestDiff = Infinity;
    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
      const a = corners[k];
      const b = corners[(k + 1) % 6];
      const mx = (a.x + b.x) / 2 - center.x;
      const mz = (a.z + b.z) / 2 - center.z;
      const edgeAng = Math.atan2(mz, mx);
      let diff = Math.abs(this._angleDiff(neighborAng, edgeAng));
      if (diff < bestDiff) {
        bestDiff = diff;
        bestK = k;
      }
    }
    return {
      a: corners[bestK],
      b: corners[(bestK + 1) % 6],
    };
  }
  _angleDiff(a, b) {
    let d = a - b;
    while (d > Math.PI) d -= Math.PI * 2;
    while (d < -Math.PI) d += Math.PI * 2;
    return d;
  }
  _edgeKey(edge) {
    const ax = edge.a.x.toFixed(2), az = edge.a.z.toFixed(2);
    const bx = edge.b.x.toFixed(2), bz = edge.b.z.toFixed(2);
    return [ax, az, bx, bz].sort().join('|');
  }
  _corners(tile) {
    const { x, z } = tile.hex.toWorld(this.size);
    const R = this.size * 0.94;
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const ang = Math.PI / 180 * (60 * i - 30);
      out.push({ x: x + R * Math.cos(ang), z: z + R * Math.sin(ang) });
    }
    return out;
  }
  clear() {
    for (const child of [...this._edgesGroup.children]) {
      this._edgesGroup.remove(child);
      child.geometry?.dispose();
      child.material?.dispose();
    }
    for (const child of [...this._iconsGroup.children]) {
      this._iconsGroup.remove(child);
      child.material?.map?.dispose();
      child.material?.dispose();
    }
  }
  refresh() { this._build(); }
  addTo(scene) { scene.add(this.group); return this; }
  removeFrom(scene) { scene.remove(this.group); }
  setVisible(v) { this.group.visible = v; }
  dispose() {
    this._disposed = true;
    this.clear();
  }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\HexVisual.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { BaseVisual } from './BaseVisual.js';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
import { ModelLoader } from '../ModelLoader.js';
import {
  terrainModelUrl,
  terrainModelSettings,
} from '../../../config.js';
/**
 * Гекс с процедурным ландшафтом.
 * Для гор и леса — пытается подставить 3D-модель из public/models/.
 * Если модель не загрузилась или ещё грузится, используется процедурный декор.
 */
export class HexVisual extends BaseVisual {
  constructor(tile, size) {
    super({ tile, size });
    this._overlays = new Map();
    this._modelObj = null;
    this._disposed = false;
  }
  _build() {
    const { tile, size } = this.data;
    if (!this._decorations) this._decorations = [];
    const t = Theme.terrain[tile.terrain] || Theme.terrain.plain;
    const height = 0.7;
    // ─── Плита гекса ───
    const geo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(size * 0.94, size * 0.94, height, 6);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: t.color, roughness: t.roughness, metalness: 0.05,
    });
    const plate = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
    plate.rotation.y = Math.PI;
    plate.position.y = height / 2;
    plate.receiveShadow = true;
    plate.castShadow = true;
    plate.userData = { tile, hoverable: 'tile' };
    this.group.add(plate);
    // ─── Модель гор/леса ───
    const seed = hashString(tile.key);
    const modelUrl = terrainModelUrl(tile.terrain, seed);
    if (modelUrl) {
      this._loadTerrainModel(modelUrl, tile, size, height);
    } else {
      this._addTerrainDecor(tile, size, height);
    }
    // ─── Фракционное кольцо ───
    this._factionRing = null;
    if (tile.factionId && Theme.faction[tile.factionId]) {
      const color = Theme.faction[tile.factionId];
      const ringGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(size * 0.72, size * 0.82, 6);
      const ringMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color, emissive: color, emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
        roughness: 0.4, metalness: 0.5,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9,
        depthWrite: false,
      });
      const ring = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeo, ringMat);
      ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      ring.rotation.z = Math.PI / 6;
      ring.position.y = height + 0.01;
      this.group.add(ring);
      this._factionRing = ring;
    }
  }
  _loadTerrainModel(url, tile, size, plateHeight) {
    const cached = ModelLoader.instance(url);
    if (cached) {
      this._placeTerrainModel(cached, tile, size, plateHeight);
      return;
    }
    ModelLoader.load(url)
      .then((scene) => {
        if (this._disposed || !this.group) return;
        const inst = scene.clone(true);
        this._placeTerrainModel(inst, tile, size, plateHeight);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (this._disposed || !this.group) return;
        this._addTerrainDecor(tile, size, plateHeight);
      });
  }
  _placeTerrainModel(obj, tile, size, plateHeight) {
    const cfg = terrainModelSettings(tile.terrain) || {};
    const fitToHex = cfg.fitToHex !== false;
    // Сброс трансформаций
    obj.position.set(0, 0, 0);
    obj.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
    obj.scale.set(1, 1, 1);
    // 1) Первый box3 — до масштабирования
    const box0 = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj);
    const sizeV = new THREE.Vector3();
    box0.getSize(sizeV);
    // 2) Автоскейл по горизонтали (XZ)
    let baseScale = 1;
    if (fitToHex) {
      const maxXZ = Math.max(sizeV.x, sizeV.z) || 1;
      const target = size * 1.4;
      baseScale = target / maxXZ;
    }
    const userScale = cfg.scale ?? 1;
    const totalScale = baseScale * userScale;
    obj.scale.setScalar(totalScale);
    // 3) Центрируем по XZ и ставим низ на верх плиты
    const box1 = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj);
    const center = new THREE.Vector3();
    box1.getCenter(center);
    obj.position.x += -center.x + (cfg.offsetX ?? 0);
    obj.position.z += -center.z + (cfg.offsetZ ?? 0);
    obj.position.y += (plateHeight - box1.min.y) + (cfg.offsetY ?? 0);
    // 4) Поворот — фиксированный или случайный по seed (анти-клон)
    let rotY = cfg.rotationY || 0;
    if (cfg.rotationYRandom) {
      const rng = mulberry32(hashString(tile.key + ':rot'));
      rotY += rng() * Math.PI * 2;
    }
    obj.rotation.y = rotY;
    // 5) ─── НОРМАЛИЗАЦИЯ PBR-МАТЕРИАЛОВ ───
    // Tinkercad экспортирует материалы с завышенной металличностью и
    // низкой шероховатостью → «пластик». Принудительно приводим к
    // природным значениям для леса/гор.
    // В _placeTerrainModel, внутри obj.traverse
    const isNatural = tile.terrain === 'forest' || tile.terrain === 'mountain';
    const DARKEN = 0.55;
    obj.traverse((c) => {
      if (!c.isMesh) return;
      c.castShadow = cfg.castShadow !== false;
      c.receiveShadow = cfg.receiveShadow !== false;
      c.userData = c.userData || {};
      c.userData.tile = tile;
      c.userData.hoverable = 'tile';
      const mats = Array.isArray(c.material) ? c.material : [c.material];
      for (const m of mats) {
        if (!m) continue;
        if (isNatural) {
          if ('metalness' in m) m.metalness = 0.0;
          if ('roughness' in m) m.roughness = 0.85;
        } else {
          if ('metalness' in m && m.metalness > 0.6) m.metalness = 0.6;
          if ('roughness' in m && m.roughness < 0.2) m.roughness = 0.3;
        }
        // Затемнение (если ещё не сделано в ModelLoader)
        // Проверяем флаг, чтобы не затемнять дважды
        if (m.color && !m.userData?._darkened) {
          m.color.multiplyScalar(DARKEN);
          if (m.userData) m.userData._darkened = true;
          else m.userData = { _darkened: true };
        }
        if ('emissiveIntensity' in m && m.emissiveIntensity > 0.05) {
          m.emissiveIntensity = 0.05;
        }
        m.needsUpdate = true;
      }
    });
    this.group.add(obj);
    this._modelObj = obj;
  }
  setFactionRingVisible(v) {
    if (this._factionRing) this._factionRing.visible = !!v;
  }
  setFactionColor(factionId) {
    if (!this._factionRing) return;
    if (!factionId || !Theme.faction[factionId]) {
      this._factionRing.visible = false;
      return;
    }
    const color = Theme.faction[factionId];
    this._factionRing.material.color.setHex(color);
    this._factionRing.material.emissive.setHex(color);
    this._factionRing.visible = true;
  }
  // ─── Процедурный декор (fallback) ───
  _addTerrainDecor(tile, size, plateHeight) {
    const rng = mulberry32(hashString(tile.key));
    const baseY = plateHeight;
    switch (tile.terrain) {
      case 'forest':
        this._addTrees(rng, size, baseY, 3 + Math.floor(rng() * 3));
        break;
      case 'mountain':
        this._addRocks(rng, size, baseY, 2 + Math.floor(rng() * 2));
        break;
      case 'plain':
        this._addGrass(rng, size, baseY, 2 + Math.floor(rng() * 3));
        break;
      case 'steppe':
        this._addGrass(rng, size, baseY, 2 + Math.floor(rng() * 2), 0xc0a040);
        break;
      case 'ruins':
        this._addRuins(rng, size, baseY);
        break;
    }
  }
  _addTrees(rng, size, baseY, count) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const angle = rng() * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = rng() * size * 0.45;
      const x = Math.cos(angle) * r;
      const z = Math.sin(angle) * r;
      const h = size * (0.15 + rng() * 0.1);
      const trunk = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(size * 0.015, size * 0.025, h, 4),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a2010, roughness: 0.95, metalness: 0 }),
      );
      trunk.position.set(x, baseY + h / 2, z);
      this.group.add(trunk);
      this._decorations.push(trunk);
      const crownColor = new THREE.Color(0x2a5a20).offsetHSL(0, 0, (rng() - 0.5) * 0.1);
      const crown = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.ConeGeometry(size * 0.12, h * 1.2, 5),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: crownColor, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0 }),
      );
      crown.position.set(x, baseY + h + h * 0.4, z);
      this.group.add(crown);
      this._decorations.push(crown);
    }
  }
  _addRocks(rng, size, baseY, count) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const angle = rng() * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = rng() * size * 0.4;
      const x = Math.cos(angle) * r;
      const z = Math.sin(angle) * r;
      const h = size * (0.15 + rng() * 0.25);
      const rock = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.ConeGeometry(size * (0.08 + rng() * 0.06), h, 5),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
          color: 0x7a705a, roughness: 0.95, metalness: 0,
          flatShading: true,
        }),
      );
      rock.position.set(x, baseY + h / 2, z);
      rock.rotation.y = rng() * Math.PI;
      this.group.add(rock);
      this._decorations.push(rock);
    }
  }
  _addGrass(rng, size, baseY, count, color = 0x8aaa40) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const angle = rng() * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = rng() * size * 0.55;
      const x = Math.cos(angle) * r;
      const z = Math.sin(angle) * r;
      const h = size * (0.04 + rng() * 0.05);
      const blade = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.ConeGeometry(size * 0.02, h, 3),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness: 1, metalness: 0 }),
      );
      blade.position.set(x, baseY + h / 2, z);
      this.group.add(blade);
      this._decorations.push(blade);
    }
  }
  _addRuins(rng, size, baseY) {
    const count = 2 + Math.floor(rng() * 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const angle = rng() * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = rng() * size * 0.4;
      const x = Math.cos(angle) * r;
      const z = Math.sin(angle) * r;
      const h = size * (0.15 + rng() * 0.2);
      const col = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(size * 0.05, size * 0.06, h, 6),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x9a8a70, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0 }),
      );
      col.position.set(x, baseY + h / 2, z);
      col.rotation.z = (rng() - 0.5) * 0.5;
      this.group.add(col);
      this._decorations.push(col);
    }
  }
  attachOverlay(kind, overlay) {
    if (!this._overlays) this._overlays = new Map();
    this._overlays.set(kind, overlay);
    overlay.addTo(this.group);
  }
  setOverlayVisible(kind, visible) {
    const o = this._overlays?.get(kind);
    if (o) o.setVisible(visible);
  }
  removeFrom(scene) {
    this._disposed = true;
    super.removeFrom(scene);
  }
}
function hashString(str) {
  let h = 5381;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) h = ((h << 5) + h + str.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  return h >>> 0;
}
function mulberry32(seed) {
  return function () {
    seed |= 0; seed = (seed + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\HighlightOverlay.js

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Визуальный оверлей подсветки. Один инстанс на состояние:
 * hover, selected, neighbor. Накладывается поверх гекса.
 *
 * Использует RingGeometry (плоское кольцо) — не мешает видеть плиту.
 */
export class HighlightOverlay {
    constructor(size, kind) {
        this.kind = kind;
        const cfg = Theme.highlight[kind];
        const geo = new THREE.RingGeometry(size * 0.78, size * 0.94, 6);
        const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
          color: cfg.color,
          transparent: true,
          opacity: cfg.opacity,
          side: THREE.DoubleSide,
          depthWrite: false,
        });
        this.mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        this.mesh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        this.mesh.rotation.z = Math.PI / 6;   // ← было rotation.y, стало rotation.z
        this.mesh.position.y = 0.21;
        this.mesh.visible = false;
        this.mesh.renderOrder = 2;
        if (kind === 'selected') {
          const glowGeo = new THREE.CircleGeometry(size * 0.9, 6);
          const glowMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
            color: cfg.color,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.12,
            side: THREE.DoubleSide,
            depthWrite: false,
          });
          this.glow = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeo, glowMat);
          this.glow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
          this.glow.rotation.z = Math.PI / 6;
          this.glow.position.y = 0.205;
          this.glow.visible = false;
          this.glow.renderOrder = 1;
        }
      }
    setVisible(v) {
        this.mesh.visible = v;
        if (this.glow) this.glow.visible = v;
    }
    addTo(group) {
        group.add(this.mesh);
        if (this.glow) group.add(this.glow);
    }
}

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Плоское кольцо-подсветка доступного гекса.
 * Используется, когда выбран полководец и видны его варианты хода/атаки.
 *
 * kind: 'move'   — синее кольцо (Theme.movement)
 *       'attack' — красное кольцо (жёстко задано)
 */
export class MovementOverlay {
  constructor(size, kind = 'move') {
    const cfg = kind === 'attack'
      ? { color: 0xf85149, opacity: 0.5, ringWidth: { inner: 0.55, outer: 0.72 } }
      : Theme.movement;
    const geo = new THREE.RingGeometry(
      size * cfg.ringWidth.inner,
      size * cfg.ringWidth.outer,
      6,
    );
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: cfg.color,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: cfg.opacity,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide,
      depthWrite: false,
    });
    this.mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
    this.mesh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    this.mesh.rotation.z = Math.PI / 6;
    this.mesh.position.y = 0.21;
    this.mesh.renderOrder = 2;
    this.mesh.visible = false;
  }
  addTo(group) { group.add(this.mesh); }
  setVisible(v) { this.mesh.visible = v; }
}

📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\PathVisual.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
/**
 * Превью маршрута: точки на гексах пути + маркер цели (подтверждение хода).
 * Живёт в сцене напрямую, не в группе гекса.
 *
 * ВАЖНО: меши пути НЕ входят в getPickables() — рейкаст проходит сквозь них.
 * Глубина отключена (depthTest: false): превью всегда поверх террейна и тумана.
 */
export class PathVisual {
  constructor(size) {
    this.size = size;
    this.group = new THREE.Group();
    this._dots = [];
    this._marker = null;
  }
  /** Принимает и тайл (obj.hex.toWorld), и гекс (obj.toWorld) — нормализуем. */
  static _worldPos(obj, size) {
    const hex = typeof obj?.toWorld === 'function' ? obj : obj?.hex;
    return hex?.toWorld(size) || null;
  }
  /** Точки пути. null/[] — убрать все. */
  setPath(pathKeys, getObj) {
    for (const d of this._dots) {
      this.group.remove(d);
      d.geometry.dispose();
      d.material.dispose();
    }
    this._dots = [];
    if (!pathKeys || pathKeys.length === 0) return;
    for (const key of pathKeys) {
      const pos = PathVisual._worldPos(getObj?.(key), this.size);
      if (!pos) continue;
      const dot = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CircleGeometry(this.size * 0.12, 12),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
          color: 0xf5d57a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.85,
          depthWrite: false, depthTest: false,
        }),
      );
      dot.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      dot.position.set(pos.x, 0.3, pos.z);
      dot.renderOrder = 998;
      this.group.add(dot);
      this._dots.push(dot);
    }
  }
  /** Маркер цели: кольцо + точка-центр. null — убрать. */
  setMarker(targetKey, getObj) {
    if (this._marker) {
      for (const m of this._marker) {
        this.group.remove(m);
        m.geometry.dispose();
        m.material.dispose();
      }
      this._marker = null;
    }
    if (!targetKey) return;
    const pos = PathVisual._worldPos(getObj?.(targetKey), this.size);
    if (!pos) return;
    const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.RingGeometry(this.size * 0.35, this.size * 0.5, 32),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: 0xffd24a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false, depthTest: false,
      }),
    );
    ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    ring.position.set(pos.x, 0.34, pos.z);
    const center = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.CircleGeometry(this.size * 0.16, 16),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: 0xffd24a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false, depthTest: false,
      }),
    );
    center.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    center.position.set(pos.x, 0.34, pos.z);
    ring.renderOrder = 999;
    center.renderOrder = 999;
    this.group.add(ring);
    this.group.add(center);
    this._marker = [ring, center];
  }
  addTo(scene) { scene.add(this.group); }
  removeFrom(scene) {
    this.setPath(null, null);
    this.setMarker(null, null);
    scene.remove(this.group);
  }
}

📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\RoadVisual.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Дорога между двумя гексами. Тонкий бокс.
 */
export class RoadVisual {
  constructor(fromTile, toTile, size) {
    this.from = fromTile;
    this.to = toTile;
    this.size = size;
    this.group = new THREE.Group();
    this._build();
  }
  _build() {
    const { from, to, size } = this;
    const p1 = from.hex.toWorld(size);
    const p2 = to.hex.toWorld(size);
    const dx = p2.x - p1.x;
    const dz = p2.z - p1.z;
    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(Theme.city.roadWidth * size, 0.05, len);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: Theme.city.roadColor, roughness: 0.95,
    });
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
    mesh.position.set((p1.x + p2.x) / 2, 0.42 + 0.03, (p1.z + p2.z) / 2);
    mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
    mesh.receiveShadow = true;
    this.group.add(mesh);
  }
  addTo(scene) { scene.add(this.group); return this; }
  removeFrom(scene) { scene.remove(this.group); }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\SeaVisual.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Море. Одна большая плоскость с кастомным шейдером.
 * Волны — сумма синусоид по X/Z, пена на гребнях, блик от солнца.
 *
 * Не знает о домене — только о Theme. Заменяется целиком при желании.
 */
export class SeaVisual {
    constructor() {
        const s = Theme.sea;
        const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(s.size, s.size, 96, 96);
        this.material = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
            uniforms: {
                uTime: { value: 0 },
                uColorDeep: { value: new THREE.Color(s.colorDeep) },
                uColorShallow: { value: new THREE.Color(s.colorShallow) },
                uColorFoam: { value: new THREE.Color(s.colorFoam) },
                uSunDir: {
                    value: new THREE.Vector3(
                        s.sunDirection.x, s.sunDirection.y, s.sunDirection.z,
                    ).normalize()
                },
                uWaveScale: { value: s.waveScale },
                uWaveSpeed: { value: s.waveSpeed },
                uWaveHeight: { value: s.waveHeight },
                uSpecularity: { value: s.specularity },
            },
            vertexShader: /* glsl */`
        uniform float uTime;
        uniform float uWaveScale;
        uniform float uWaveSpeed;
        uniform float uWaveHeight;
        varying vec3 vNormal;
        varying float vWave;
        void main() {
          vec3 pos = position;
          // Три волны с разными частотами
          float w1 = sin(pos.x * uWaveScale + uTime * uWaveSpeed * 1.0) * uWaveHeight;
          float w2 = sin(pos.y * uWaveScale * 1.7 + uTime * uWaveSpeed * 1.4) * uWaveHeight * 0.6;
          float w3 = sin((pos.x + pos.y) * uWaveScale * 0.8 + uTime * uWaveSpeed * 0.6) * uWaveHeight * 0.4;
          pos.z = w1 + w2 + w3;
          vWave = pos.z;
          // Нормаль (приближённо)
          float dx = cos(pos.x * uWaveScale + uTime * uWaveSpeed * 1.0) * uWaveHeight * uWaveScale
                   + cos((pos.x + pos.y) * uWaveScale * 0.8 + uTime * uWaveSpeed * 0.6) * uWaveHeight * 0.4 * uWaveScale * 0.8;
          float dy = cos(pos.y * uWaveScale * 1.7 + uTime * uWaveSpeed * 1.4) * uWaveHeight * 0.6 * uWaveScale * 1.7
                   + cos((pos.x + pos.y) * uWaveScale * 0.8 + uTime * uWaveSpeed * 0.6) * uWaveHeight * 0.4 * uWaveScale * 0.8;
          vNormal = normalize(vec3(-dx, -dy, 1.0));
          gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(pos, 1.0);
        }
      `,
            fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
        uniform vec3 uColorDeep;
        uniform vec3 uColorShallow;
        uniform vec3 uColorFoam;
        uniform vec3 uSunDir;
        uniform float uSpecularity;
        varying vec3 vNormal;
        varying float vWave;
        void main() {
          float h = clamp(vWave * 0.6 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);
          vec3 col = mix(uColorDeep, uColorShallow, h);
          // Блик солнца
          float spec = pow(max(dot(normalize(vNormal), uSunDir), 0.0), uSpecularity);
          col += vec3(1.0, 0.92, 0.72) * spec * 0.85;
          // Пена на гребнях
          float foam = smoothstep(0.55, 0.78, vWave);
          col = mix(col, uColorFoam, foam * 0.35);
          // Легкая виньетка
          gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
        }
      `,
        });
        this.mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, this.material);
        this.mesh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        this.mesh.position.y = s.y;
        this.mesh.receiveShadow = false;
        this.mesh.renderOrder = -1;
    }
    update(time) {
        this.material.uniforms.uTime.value = time;
    }
    addTo(scene) { scene.add(this.mesh); return this; }
    removeFrom(scene) { scene.remove(this.mesh); }
}

📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\StarField.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
/**
 * Звёздное небо. Points на сфере вокруг сцены.
 * Медленно вращается — создаёт «жизнь» в статичной сцене.
 */
export class StarField {
    constructor() {
        const cfg = Theme.sky;
        const positions = [];
        const count = cfg.starsCount;
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const r = cfg.starsRadiusMin + Math.random() * (cfg.starsRadiusMax - cfg.starsRadiusMin);
            const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1); // равномерно по сфере
            positions.push(
                r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta),
                r * Math.cos(phi) * 0.45 + 30,   // чуть сплюснуть по Y
                r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta),
            );
        }
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
        this.material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
            color: cfg.starsColor,
            size: cfg.starsSize,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: cfg.starsOpacity,
            sizeAttenuation: true,
            depthWrite: false,
        });
        this.points = new THREE.Points(geo, this.material);
        this.points.renderOrder = -2;
    }
    update(time) {
        this.points.rotation.y = time * 0.005;
    }
    addTo(scene) { scene.add(this.points); return this; }
    removeFrom(scene) { scene.remove(this.points); }
}

📄 infrastructure\three\visuals\VillageVisual.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Theme } from '../../config/ThemeConfig.js';
const PLATE_H = 0.7;
export class VillageVisual {
  constructor(city, size) {
    this.city = city;
    this.size = size;
    this.group = new THREE.Group();
    this._build();
  }
  _build() {
    const { size, city } = this;
    const rng = mulberry32(hashString(city.id + city.name));
    // ─── Площадка ───
    const base = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(size * 0.3, size * 0.32, size * 0.03, 6),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x7a6a48, roughness: 0.95 }),
    );
    base.position.y = PLATE_H + size * 0.015;
    base.receiveShadow = true;
    this.group.add(base);
    // ─── 3–4 домика ───
    const housesCount = 3 + Math.floor(rng() * 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < housesCount; i++) {
      const angle = (i / housesCount) * Math.PI * 2 + rng() * 0.5;
      const dist = size * (0.08 + rng() * 0.1);
      const hx = Math.cos(angle) * dist;
      const hz = Math.sin(angle) * dist;
      const w = size * (0.06 + rng() * 0.03);
      const d = size * (0.06 + rng() * 0.03);
      const h = size * (0.05 + rng() * 0.03);
      const house = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb89860, roughness: 0.85 }),
      );
      house.position.set(hx, PLATE_H + size * 0.015 + h / 2, hz);
      house.castShadow = true;
      this.group.add(house);
      const roof = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.ConeGeometry(w * 0.85, h * 0.7, 4),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
          color: 0x6a3a1a, roughness: 0.9, flatShading: true,
        }),
      );
      roof.position.set(hx, PLATE_H + size * 0.015 + h + h * 0.35, hz);
      roof.rotation.y = Math.PI / 4;
      roof.castShadow = true;
      this.group.add(roof);
    }
    // ─── Флаг: у своего поселения — фракция; у нейтрального — цвет нейтрала ───
    const flagFaction = city.factionId || city.neutralFaction;
    if (flagFaction) {
      const color = Theme.faction[flagFaction] || 0xd4a94c;
      // Полюшко
      const pole = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.02, 0.02, size * 0.18, 4),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a2a15 }),
      );
      pole.position.set(0, PLATE_H + size * 0.09, 0);
      this.group.add(pole);
      // Полотно
      const flag = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(size * 0.1, size * 0.06),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
          color, emissive: color, emissiveIntensity: 0.4, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        }),
      );
      flag.position.set(size * 0.06, PLATE_H + size * 0.16, 0);
      this.group.add(flag);
    }
    // ─── userData: city + hoverable ───
    this.group.traverse((c) => {
      if (c.isMesh || c.isSprite) {
        c.userData = c.userData || {};
        c.userData.city = city;
        c.userData.hoverable = 'city';
      }
    });
  }
  addTo(scene) { scene.add(this.group); return this; }
  removeFrom(scene) { scene.remove(this.group); }
}
function hashString(str) {
  let h = 5381;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) h = ((h << 5) + h + str.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  return h >>> 0;
}
function mulberry32(seed) {
  return function () {
    seed |= 0; seed = (seed + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

📄 input\InputController.js

import * as THREE from 'three';
export class InputController {
  constructor({ canvas, camera, onTileClick, onTileHover }) {
    this.canvas = canvas;
    this.camera = camera;
    this.onTileClick = onTileClick;
    this.onTileHover = onTileHover;
    this.raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    this.mouse = new THREE.Vector2();
    this._pickables = [];
    this._isDragging = false;
    this._dragMoved = false;
    this._prev = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    this._dragButton = -1;
    this._cameraController = null;
    this._lastHoverKey = null;
    this._keys = new Set();
    this._bind();
    this._startUpdateLoop();
  }
  setPickables(objs) { this._pickables = objs; }
  attachCameraController(controller) { this._cameraController = controller; }
  _bind() {
    const c = this.canvas;
    c.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      if (e.button !== 0 && e.button !== 2) return;
      this._isDragging = true;
      this._dragMoved = false;
      this._prev = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
      this._dragButton = e.button;
      if (e.button === 2) c.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
    });
    c.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      this._isDragging = false;
      this._dragButton = -1;
      c.style.cursor = 'default';
    });
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      if (this._isDragging && this._cameraController) {
        const dx = e.clientX - this._prev.x;
        const dy = e.clientY - this._prev.y;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 4 || Math.abs(dy) > 4) this._dragMoved = true;
        this._prev = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
        this._cameraController.pan(dx * 0.6, dy * 0.6);
        return;
      }
      this._handleHover(e);
    });
    c.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.button !== 0) return;
      if (this._dragMoved) { this._dragMoved = false; return; }
      this._handleClick(e);
    });
    c.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      this._cameraController?.zoom(e.deltaY);
    }, { passive: false });
    let touchStart = null;
    c.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
      if (e.touches.length === 1) {
        const t = e.touches[0];
        touchStart = { x: t.clientX, y: t.clientY, time: Date.now() };
        this._isDragging = true;
        this._dragMoved = false;
        this._prev = { x: t.clientX, y: t.clientY };
      }
    }, { passive: true });
    c.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
      if (e.touches.length === 1 && this._isDragging && this._cameraController) {
        const t = e.touches[0];
        const dx = t.clientX - this._prev.x;
        const dy = t.clientY - this._prev.y;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 4 || Math.abs(dy) > 4) this._dragMoved = true;
        this._prev = { x: t.clientX, y: t.clientY };
        this._cameraController.pan(dx, dy);
      }
    }, { passive: true });
    c.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
      this._isDragging = false;
      if (!touchStart) return;
      if (!this._dragMoved && Date.now() - touchStart.time < 400) {
        const last = e.changedTouches[0];
        this._handleClick({ clientX: last.clientX, clientY: last.clientY });
      }
      touchStart = null;
    }, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      const tag = document.activeElement?.tagName;
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') return;
      const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
      if (['w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright',
        '+', '-', '=', '_'].includes(key)) {
        this._keys.add(key);
        e.preventDefault();
      }
    });
    window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
      this._keys.delete(e.key.toLowerCase());
    });
  }
  _startUpdateLoop() {
    const tick = () => {
      if (this._cameraController) {
        const k = this._keys;
        let dx = 0, dy = 0;
        const speed = 3;
        if (k.has('w') || k.has('arrowup')) dy -= speed;
        if (k.has('s') || k.has('arrowdown')) dy += speed;
        if (k.has('a') || k.has('arrowleft')) dx -= speed;
        if (k.has('d') || k.has('arrowright')) dx += speed;
        if (dx !== 0 || dy !== 0) this._cameraController.pan(dx * 2, dy * 2);
        if (k.has('+') || k.has('=')) this._cameraController.zoom(-60);
        if (k.has('-') || k.has('_')) this._cameraController.zoom(60);
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  _pick(clientX, clientY) {
    const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.mouse.x = ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
    this.mouse.y = -((clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
    this.raycaster.setFromCamera(this.mouse, this.camera);
    if (this._pickables.length === 0) {
      return { tile: null, general: null, city: null, halo: null, hoverType: null };
    }
    const hits = this.raycaster.intersectObjects(this._pickables, true);
    let tile = null, general = null, city = null, halo = null, hoverType = null;
    for (const h of hits) {
      let cur = h.object;
      while (cur) {
        const ud = cur.userData;
        // ─── Иконка ореола ───
        if (ud?.haloIcon && !halo) {
          halo = ud.halo;
          // Приоритет haloIcon — если попали в спрайт, это главное
          hoverType = 'haloIcon';
        }
        if (ud?.hoverable === 'general' && !general) {
          general = ud.general;
          // general имеет приоритет над city, но НЕ над haloIcon
          if (hoverType !== 'haloIcon') hoverType = 'general';
        }
        if (ud?.hoverable === 'city' && !city) {
          city = ud.city;
          if (!general && hoverType !== 'haloIcon') hoverType = 'city';
        }
        if (ud?.tile && !tile) tile = ud.tile;
        cur = cur.parent;
      }
      if (general && tile && city && halo) break;
    }
    // Fallback: если нашли город, но не нашли его tile
    if (city && !tile && city.tileKey) {
      for (const obj of this._pickables) {
        const ud = obj.userData;
        if (ud?.tile && ud.tile.cityId === city.id) {
          tile = ud.tile;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    return { tile, general, city, halo, hoverType };
  }
  _handleClick(e) {
    const { tile, general, city } = this._pick(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    this.onTileClick?.(tile, general, city);
  }
  _handleHover(e) {
    const { tile, hoverType, general, halo } = this._pick(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    const key = `${hoverType || 'none'}:${general?.id || ''}:${halo?.id || ''}:${tile?.key || ''}`;
    if (key === this._lastHoverKey) return;
    this._lastHoverKey = key;
    let cursor = 'default';
    if (hoverType === 'general' || hoverType === 'city' || hoverType === 'haloIcon') cursor = 'pointer';
    else if (this._isDragging) cursor = 'grabbing';
    this.canvas.style.cursor = cursor;
    this.onTileHover?.(tile, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }, { hoverType, general, halo });
  }
}

import { Toast } from './components/Toast.js';
import { Tooltip } from './components/Tooltip.js';
import { GeneralPanel } from './components/GeneralPanel.js';
import { EndTurnButton } from './components/EndTurnButton.js';
import { ResourceBar } from './components/ResourceBar.js';
import { DeckPanel } from './components/DeckPanel.js';

import { CityPanel } from './components/CityPanel.js';
import { BattleModal } from './components/BattleModal.js';
import { TutorialModal } from './components/TutorialModal.js';
import { TurnIndicator } from './components/TurnIndicator.js';
import { MarketPanel } from './components/MarketPanel.js';
import { FactionsPanel } from './components/FactionsPanel.js';
import { GlossaryModal } from './components/GlossaryModal.js';

import { iconImg } from '../config/icons.js';
import { FooterPanel } from './components/FooterPanel.js';
export class UIManager {
  constructor({
    root, toastRoot, bus, world,
    onEndTurn, onRecruit,
    battleController, marketController, sound,
  }) {
    this.root = root;
    this.bus = bus;
    this.world = world;
    this.sound = sound;
    this.onHire = null;
    this.onMarket = null;
    this.onRecruit = onRecruit;
    this.onBuySettlement = null;
    this.onAttackCity = null;
    this.onLensModeChange = null;   // (mode) => void
    this.battleController = battleController;
    this.marketController = marketController;
    this._lensMode = 'none';
    this.toast = new Toast(toastRoot);
    this.tooltip = new Tooltip(root);
    this.generalPanel = new GeneralPanel(root);
    this.deckPanel = new DeckPanel(root);
    this.resourceBar = new ResourceBar(root);
    this.turnIndicator = new TurnIndicator(root);

      onEndTurn?.();
    });

    this.marketPanel = new MarketPanel(root, {
      bus, controller: marketController, world,
    });

      onSkipTurn: () => onEndTurn?.(),
    });
    this.factionsPanel = new FactionsPanel(root, { world });
    this.glossary = new GlossaryModal(root);

    this._subscribe();
  }
  setWorld(world) {
    this.world = world;
    this.tooltip.setWorld(world);
    if (this.marketPanel) this.marketPanel.world = world;
    if (this.factionsPanel) this.factionsPanel.world = world;
    if (this.quickJump) this.quickJump.setWorld(world);
  }
  _buildHud() {

    hud.className = 'hud-top';

        </button>
        <button class="hud-focus-btn" id="btn-focus-city" title="К городу">
          ${iconImg('terrain_city', { size: 16 })}
        </button>

      </div>
    `;
    this.root.appendChild(hud);
    this._hudEl = hud;
    this.resourceBar.el = hud.querySelector('#resource-bar');
    this.resourceBar.refresh();
    // ─── Линзы ───
    const lensPanel = hud.querySelector('#lens-panel');
    lensPanel.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const btn = e.target.closest('[data-lens]');
      if (!btn) return;
      this.setLensMode(btn.getAttribute('data-lens'));
      this.sound?.play('click');
    });
    // ─── Найм ───
    hud.querySelector('#btn-recruit').addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      if (this.onHire) this.onHire();
    });
    // ─── Рынок ───
    hud.querySelector('#btn-market').addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      if (this.onMarket) this.onMarket();
    });
    // ─── Фракции ───
    hud.querySelector('#btn-factions').addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      this.factionsPanel.open();
