    // ─── Глоссарий ───
    hud.querySelector('#btn-glossary').addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      this.glossary.open();
    });
    // ─── Mute ───
    const muteBtn = hud.querySelector('#btn-mute');
    if (this.sound?.isMuted()) {
      muteBtn.querySelector('.icon')?.remove();
      muteBtn.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', iconImg('ui_mute', { size: 16 }));
      muteBtn.classList.add('muted');
    }
    muteBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const muted = this.sound?.toggleMute();
      muteBtn.querySelector('.icon')?.remove();
      muteBtn.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', iconImg(muted ? 'ui_mute' : 'ui_unmute', { size: 16 }));
      muteBtn.classList.toggle('muted', !!muted);
    });
    // ─── Фокус и навигация ───
    this._bindFocusNav(hud);
    this._lensPanel = lensPanel;
    this._updateLensUI();
  }
  _bindFocusNav(hud) {
    // Фокус на генерале
    hud.querySelector('#btn-focus-general')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      if (this.quickJump) {
        this.quickJump._generalIdx = 0;
        this.quickJump._handle('general');
      }
    });
    hud.querySelector('#btn-prev-general')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      this.quickJump?._handle('prev-general');
    });
    hud.querySelector('#btn-next-general')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      this.quickJump?._handle('next-general');
    });
    // Фокус на городе
    hud.querySelector('#btn-focus-city')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      if (this.quickJump) {
        this.quickJump._cityIdx = 0;
        this.quickJump._handle('city');
      }
    });
    hud.querySelector('#btn-prev-city')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      this.quickJump?._handle('prev-city');
    });
    hud.querySelector('#btn-next-city')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.sound?.play('click');
      this.quickJump?._handle('next-city');
    });
  }
  /** Установить режим линз. */
  setLensMode(mode) {
    if (!['none', 'borders', 'halos'].includes(mode)) mode = 'none';
    this._lensMode = mode;
    this._updateLensUI();
    this.onLensModeChange?.(mode);   // ← вызывает sceneBuilder.setLensMode
  }
  _updateLensUI() {
    if (!this._lensPanel) return;
    this._lensPanel.querySelectorAll('[data-lens]').forEach((btn) => {
      const isActive = btn.getAttribute('data-lens') === this._lensMode;
      btn.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
      btn.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(isActive));
    });
  }
  setHireEnabled(enabled) {
    const btn = this._hudEl.querySelector('#btn-recruit');
    if (!btn) return;
    btn.disabled = !enabled;
    btn.style.opacity = enabled ? '1' : '0.4';
  }
  initMinimap({ cameraController, onCenter }) {
    if (!this.quickJump) return;
    this.quickJump.initMinimap({
      world: this.world,
      cameraController,
      onCenter,
    });
    this.bus.on('general:moved', () => this.quickJump?.refreshMinimap());
    this.bus.on('turn:ended', () => this.quickJump?.refreshMinimap());
    this.bus.on('battle:finished', () => this.quickJump?.refreshMinimap());
    this.minimap = null;
  }
  openRecruitModal({ factionId, general, world }) {
    this.recruitModal.open({ factionId, general, world });
  }
  openCityPanel({ tile, city, world }) {
    this.cityPanel.onBuySettlement = (cityId) => {
      this.onBuySettlement?.(cityId);
    };
    this.cityPanel.onAttackCity = (cityId, defenderFactionId) => {
      this.onAttackCity?.(cityId, defenderFactionId);
    };
    this.cityPanel.open({ tile, city, world });
  }
  bindResources(pool) {
    this.resourceBar.bind(pool);
  }
  showTutorial(onDone) {
    this.tutorial.onDone = () => {
      localStorage.setItem('tavrida_tutorial_v1', '1');
      onDone?.();
    };
    this.tutorial.open();
  }
  _subscribe() {
    this.bus.on('general:selected', ({ general }) => {
      if (general) {
        this.generalPanel.show(general);
        this.deckPanel.show(general);
      } else {
        this.generalPanel.hide();
        this.deckPanel.hide();
      }
    });
    this.bus.on('general:moved', () => this.generalPanel.refresh());
    this.bus.on('general:no-move-points', ({ general, needed, have }) => {
      if (!general || !needed) return;
      // Проксируем в DOM-событие для GeneralPanel
      const ev = new CustomEvent('general:no-move-points-ui', {
        detail: { needed, have },
      });
      document.dispatchEvent(ev);
    });
    this.bus.on('resources:changed', () => {
      const factionId = this.world?.playerFactionId;
      if (!factionId) return;
      const pool = this.world.resourcePools.get(factionId);
      if (pool) this.bindResources(pool);
    });
    this.bus.on('deck:changed', ({ general }) => {
      if (general) this.deckPanel.refresh();
    });
    this.bus.on('turn:ended', () => {
      this.generalPanel.refresh();
      this.deckPanel.refresh();
    });
    this.bus.on('battle:finished', () => this.turnIndicator.hide());
    this.bus.on('ai:turnEnded', () => this.turnIndicator.hide());
    // ─── Тултип гекса убран, оставлен только для ореола ───
    this.bus.on('tile:hovered', ({ tile, screenPos }) => {
      if (!tile) { /* ничего */ }
    });
    // ─── Тултип ореола ───
    this.bus.on('halo:hovered', ({ halo, screenPos }) => {
      if (!halo) { this.tooltip.hide(); return; }
      this.tooltip.showHalo(halo, screenPos);
    });
  }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 ui\components\BattleCardEl.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { CardImageLoader } from '../../infrastructure/three/visuals/CardImageLoader.js';
import { iconImg, iconUrl } from '../../config/icons.js';
/**
 * Карта на доске боя.
 * Количество показывается ИКОНКОЙ (stat_str) + число.
 * При уменьшении — иконка меняет оттенок (см. CSS .bc-count.low / .critical).
 */
export class BattleCardEl {
  constructor() { this._imgLoader = new CardImageLoader(); }
  create(card, { selected = false, side = 'attacker', effectiveCost = null } = {}) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = `battle-card ${side === 'attacker' ? 'ally' : 'enemy'} type-${card.type}`;
    if (selected) el.classList.add('selected');
    const abilitiesHtml = (card.abilities || []).map((a) => {
      return `<div class="bc-tt-ability">
        ${iconImg(`ability_${a.id}`, { size: 12, cls: 'icon-inline' })}
        <b>${a.name}:</b> ${a.desc}
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    const hasAbility = (card.abilities || []).length > 0;
    const abilityBadge = hasAbility ? `<div class="bc-ability-badge" title="Есть способность">✦</div>` : '';
    const costBadge = effectiveCost != null
      ? `<div class="bc-cost-badge" title="Стоимость в этом бою">${iconImg('res_tactic', { size: 10 })}${effectiveCost}</div>`
      : '';
    // Иконка количества + цветовой статус
    const strRatio = card.str / (card.maxStr || card.baseStr || card.str);
    const countCls = strRatio <= 0.33 ? 'critical' : strRatio <= 0.66 ? 'low' : '';
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="bc-name-top">${card.name}</div>
      <div class="bc-art"></div>
      <div class="bc-count ${countCls}">
        ${iconImg('stat_str', { size: 11, cls: 'icon-inline' })}${card.str}
      </div>
      ${abilityBadge}
      ${costBadge}
      <div class="bc-type-stripe"></div>
      <div class="bc-tooltip">
        <div class="bc-tt-name">${card.name}</div>
        <div class="bc-tt-type">
          ${typeLabel(card.type)} · ${iconImg('stat_str', { size: 11 })}${card.str}
          · ${iconImg('res_prov', { size: 11 })}${card.prov}
        </div>
        ${effectiveCost != null ? `<div class="bc-tt-cost">${iconImg('res_tactic', { size: 11 })} Стоимость: ${effectiveCost}</div>` : ''}
        ${abilitiesHtml || '<div class="bc-tt-ability">Нет способностей</div>'}
      </div>
    `;
    const art = el.querySelector('.bc-art');
    const url = this._imgLoader.getUrl(card.imageKey);
    if (url && art) art.style.backgroundImage = `url("${url}")`;
    return el;
  }
}
function typeLabel(type) {
  return { warrior: 'Воин', archer: 'Стрелок', support: 'Поддержка', special: 'Особый' }[type] || type;
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 ui\components\BattleHand.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { CardImageLoader } from '../../infrastructure/three/visuals/CardImageLoader.js';
import { iconImg, iconUrl } from '../../config/icons.js';
export class BattleHand {
  constructor(root) {
    this.root = root;
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'battle-hand';
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this._imgLoader = new CardImageLoader();
    this._cards = [];
    this._tacticPoints = 0;
    this._onClick = null;
    this._costFn = null;
  }
  setData(cards, tacticPoints, selectedCard, onClick, costFn) {
    this._cards = cards || [];
    this._tacticPoints = tacticPoints;
    this._selected = selectedCard;
    this._onClick = onClick;
    this._costFn = costFn;   // (card) => effectiveCost
    this._render();
  }
  _render() {
    this.el.innerHTML = '';
    if (this._cards.length === 0) {
      this.el.innerHTML = '<div class="bh-empty">Рука пуста</div>';
      return;
    }
    for (const card of this._cards) {
      const cost = this._costFn ? this._costFn(card) : card.str;
      const disabled = cost > this._tacticPoints;
      const selected = this._selected && this._selected.id === card.id;
      const el = this._createCardEl(card, { disabled, selected, cost });
      if (!disabled && this._onClick) {
        el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          this._onClick(card);
        });
      }
      this.el.appendChild(el);
    }
  }
  _createCardEl(card, { disabled, selected, cost }) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = `battle-hand-card type-${card.type}`;
    el.setAttribute('data-card-id', card.id);
    if (disabled) el.classList.add('disabled');
    if (selected) el.classList.add('selected');
    const abilitiesHtml = (card.abilities || []).map((a) =>
      `<div class="bc-tt-ability">${iconImg(`ability_${a.id}`, { size: 12, cls: 'icon-inline' })}<b>${a.name}:</b> ${a.desc}</div>`
    ).join('');
    const hasAbility = (card.abilities || []).length > 0;
    const abilityBadge = hasAbility ? `<div class="bhc-ability-badge">✦</div>` : '';
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="bhc-name-top">${card.name}</div>
      <div class="bhc-art"></div>
      <div class="bhc-count">${card.str}</div>
      ${abilityBadge}
      <div class="bhc-cost">⚡${cost}</div>
      <div class="bhc-type-stripe"></div>
      <div class="bhc-tooltip">
        <div class="bc-tt-name">${card.name}</div>
        <div class="bc-tt-type">${typeLabel(card.type)} · сила ${card.str} · ${card.prov}🌾</div>
        <div class="bc-tt-cost">Стоимость в этом бою: ${cost} ⚡</div>
        ${abilitiesHtml || '<div class="bc-tt-ability">Нет способностей</div>'}
      </div>
    `;
    const art = el.querySelector('.bhc-art');
    const url = this._imgLoader.getUrl(card.imageKey);
    if (url && art) art.style.backgroundImage = `url("${url}")`;
    return el;
  }
}
function typeLabel(type) {
  return { warrior: 'Воин', archer: 'Стрелок', support: 'Поддержка', special: 'Особый' }[type] || type;
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📄 ui\components\BattleModal.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { BattleCardEl } from './BattleCardEl.js';
import { BattleHand } from './BattleHand.js';
import { BattleVisualLayer } from '../../infrastructure/battle-visuals/BattleVisualLayer.js';
import { BattleBackground } from '../../infrastructure/battle-visuals/BattleBackground.js';
import { BattleResultScreen } from './BattleResultScreen.js';
import { BattlePreviewPanel } from './BattlePreviewPanel.js';
import { CardImageLoader } from '../../infrastructure/three/visuals/CardImageLoader.js';
import { generalPortraitUrl, imgUrl } from '../../config.js';
const LANES = [
  { key: 'defense', icon: '🛡', label: 'Оборона' },
  { key: 'attack',  icon: '⚔', label: 'Атака' },
  { key: 'support', icon: '✨', label: 'Поддержка' },
];
export class BattleModal {
  constructor(root, { controller, bus }) {
    this.controller = controller;
    this.bus = bus;
    this._cardEl = new BattleCardEl();
    this._battle = null;
    this._hand = null;
    this._visualLayer = null;
    this._background = null;
    this._autoPlayActive = false;
    this._resultScreen = null;
    this._previewPanel = null;
    this._imgLoader = new CardImageLoader();
    this._logOpen = false;
    this._lastTactic = {};   // { attacker: 3, defender: 2 }
    this.overlay = document.createElement('div');
    this.overlay.className = 'battle-modal-overlay';
    root.appendChild(this.overlay);
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'battle-modal';
    this.overlay.appendChild(this.el);
    this.el.addEventListener('click', (e) => this._onClick(e));
    this.el.addEventListener('mouseover', (e) => this._onHover(e));
    this.el.addEventListener('mouseout', (e) => this._onHoverOut(e));
    this.bus.on('battle:updated', ({ battle }) => {
      this._battle = battle;
      this._render();
      if (battle.finished && !this._resultScreen) this._showResult(battle);
    });
    this.bus.on('card:played', ({ card, side, lane, index }) => {
      // Анимируем только карты ВРАГА (свои игрок и так видит)
      if (side === this._currentSide()) return;
      if (!this._battle || this._battle.finished) return;
      this._showCardCastAnimation(card, side, lane, index);
    });
    this.bus.on('battle:started', ({ battle }) => {
      this._battle = battle;
      this._autoPlayActive = false;
      this._logOpen = false;
      this._lastTactic = {};
      this._closeResult();
      this.overlay.querySelectorAll('.battle-result-overlay').forEach((e) => e.remove());
      this._background?.destroy();
      this._background = new BattleBackground(this.overlay, battle.targetTile?.terrain || 'plain');
      this.overlay.classList.add('visible');
      this._render();
      const arena = this.el.querySelector('.battle-arena');
      if (arena) {
        this._visualLayer?.dispose();
        this._visualLayer = new BattleVisualLayer(battle, arena, {
          sideToPlayer: this.controller.activeSide || battle.currentHumanSide || 'attacker',
        });
      }
    });
    this.bus.on('battle:finished', () => {
      this._closeResult();
      this.overlay.classList.remove('visible');
      this._visualLayer?.dispose();
      this._visualLayer = null;
      this._background?.destroy();
      this._background = null;
      this._battle = null;
      this._hand = null;
      this._autoPlayActive = false;
      this._resultScreen = null;
      this._previewPanel = null;
      this._logOpen = false;
      this._lastTactic = {};
    });
  }
  _currentSide() {
    const c = this.controller;
    if (c.activeSide) return c.activeSide;
    if (this._battle?.currentHumanSide) return this._battle.currentHumanSide;
    return this._battle?.turn || 'attacker';
  }
  _isHumanSide(side) {
    const hs = this.controller.humanSides;
    return !!(hs && hs[side]);
  }
  _onHover(e) {
    if (!this._previewPanel || !this._battle) return;
    const side = this._currentSide();
    const handCard = e.target.closest?.('.battle-hand-card');
    if (handCard) {
      const cardId = handCard.getAttribute('data-card-id');
      const card = this._battle[side].hand.find((c) => c.id === cardId);
      if (card) {
        const cost = this._battle.costFor(card, side);
        this._previewPanel.showCard(card, { effectiveCost: cost });
      }
      return;
    }
    const boardCard = e.target.closest?.('.battle-card');
    if (boardCard) {
      const cell = boardCard.closest('.cell');
      if (!cell) return;
      const s = cell.getAttribute('data-side');
      const lane = cell.getAttribute('data-lane');
      const idx = parseInt(cell.getAttribute('data-idx'), 10);
      const card = this._battle[s].board.get(lane, idx);
      if (card) {
        const cost = this._battle.costFor(card, s);
        this._previewPanel.showCard(card, { effectiveCost: cost });
      }
    }
  }
  _onHoverOut(e) {
    if (!this._previewPanel || !this._battle) return;
    const leftHand = e.target.closest?.('.battle-hand-card');
    const leftBoard = e.target.closest?.('.battle-card');
    if (!leftHand && !leftBoard) return;
    const side = this._currentSide();
    if (this._battle.selectedCard && this._isHumanSide(side)) {
      const card = this._battle.selectedCard;
      const cost = this._battle.costFor(card, side);
      this._previewPanel.showCard(card, { effectiveCost: cost });
    } else {
      this._previewPanel.hideCard();
    }
  }
  _onClick(e) {
    if (e.target.closest?.('.battle-result-overlay')) return;
    const side = this._currentSide();
    const isHuman = this._isHumanSide(side);
    const action = e.target.closest?.('[data-action]')?.getAttribute('data-action');
    if (action === 'end-turn')  { if (isHuman) this.controller.endTurn(); return; }
    if (action === 'skip-turn') { if (isHuman) this.controller.skipTurn(); return; }
    if (action === 'retreat')   { if (isHuman) this.controller.retreat(); return; }
    if (action === 'auto-play') { this._startAutoPlay(); return; }
    if (action === 'toggle-log') {
      this._logOpen = !this._logOpen;
      this._render();
      return;
    }
    const cell = e.target.closest?.('.cell');
    if (cell) {
      const s = cell.getAttribute('data-side');
      const lane = cell.getAttribute('data-lane');
      const idx = cell.getAttribute('data-idx');
      if (lane && idx !== null && s) this._onCellClick(s, lane, parseInt(idx, 10));
    }
  }
  _startAutoPlay() {
    const b = this._battle;
    if (!b || b.finished || this._autoPlayActive) return;
    this._autoPlayActive = true;
    this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Авто-бой запущен' });
    b.autoPlay(() => this.bus.emit('battle:updated', { battle: b }))
      .then(() => {
        this._autoPlayActive = false;
        if (b.finished && !this._resultScreen) this._showResult(b);
      });
  }
  _showResult(b) {
    const side = this._currentSide();
    const playerWon =
      (side === 'attacker' && b.winner === 'attacker') ||
      (side === 'defender' && b.winner === 'defender');
    const title = b.winner === 'draw' ? 'Ничья' : playerWon ? 'Победа!' : 'Поражение';
    const cls = b.winner === 'draw' ? 'draw' : playerWon ? 'win' : 'lose';
    this._resultScreen = new BattleResultScreen(this.overlay, {
      title, kind: cls, playerSide: side,
      attacker: b.attacker, defender: b.defender, winner: b.winner,
      onContinue: () => {
        this._resultScreen = null;
        this.controller.finish();
      },
    });
  }
  _closeResult() { this._resultScreen?.destroy(); this._resultScreen = null; }
  _onCellClick(side, lane, index) {
    const b = this._battle;
    if (!b || b.finished) return;
    if (side !== this._currentSide()) return;
    if (!this._isHumanSide(side)) return;
    if (!b.selectedCard) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Сначала выберите карту в руке' });
      return;
    }
    const cost = b.costFor(b.selectedCard, side);
    if (cost > b[side].tacticPoints) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: 'Недостаточно тактики' });
      return;
    }
    this.controller.playCard(b.selectedCard, lane, index);
  }
  _render() {
    const b = this._battle;
    if (!b) return;
    const side = this._currentSide();
    const isHuman = this._isHumanSide(side);
    const enemySide = side === 'attacker' ? 'defender' : 'attacker';
    const data = b[side];
    const enemyData = b[enemySide];
    const lineInfo = b.getLineBonusInfoFor(side);
    const enemyLineInfo = b.getLineBonusInfoFor(enemySide);
    const colInfo = b.getColumnBonusInfoFor(side);
    const enemyColInfo = b.getColumnBonusInfoFor(enemySide);
    // ─── Максимум тактики: 1 + 3 (support) + 1 ───
    const maxTactic = 5;
    const playerPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((data.tacticPoints / maxTactic) * 100));
    const enemyPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((enemyData.tacticPoints / maxTactic) * 100));
    // ─── Анимация «потратил тактику» ───
    const prevPlayer = this._lastTactic[side] ?? data.tacticPoints;
    const prevEnemy = this._lastTactic[enemySide] ?? enemyData.tacticPoints;
    const playerSpent = prevPlayer > data.tacticPoints;
    const enemySpent = prevEnemy > enemyData.tacticPoints;
    this._lastTactic[side] = data.tacticPoints;
    this._lastTactic[enemySide] = enemyData.tacticPoints;
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="battle-top">
        <div class="battle-hero enemy-hero">
          ${this._portraitHtml(enemyData.gen, 'enemy')}
          <div class="hero-info">
            <div class="hero-name">${enemyData.gen.name}</div>
            <div class="bh-lives">${this._livesHtml(enemyData.lives, enemyData.maxLives)}</div>
            <div class="hero-tactic-mini ${enemyData.tacticPoints <= 1 ? 'low' : ''}">
              <div class="hero-tactic-fill" style="width:${enemyPct}%"></div>
              <span>⚡ ${enemyData.tacticPoints}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="battle-title">
          ⚔ Бой · ход ${b.turnCount}
          <div class="battle-terrain">${b.terrain.name} · ${b.terrain.getNote(side)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="battle-hero player-hero">
          ${this._portraitHtml(data.gen, 'player')}
          <div class="hero-info">
            <div class="hero-name">${data.gen.name}</div>
            <div class="bh-lives">${this._livesHtml(data.lives, data.maxLives)}</div>
            <div class="hero-tactic-mini ${data.tacticPoints <= 1 ? 'low' : ''}">
              <div class="hero-tactic-fill" style="width:${playerPct}%"></div>
              <span>⚡ ${data.tacticPoints}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="battle-main">
        <div class="battle-left-column">
          <div class="battle-preview-panel-root" id="battle-preview-panel-root"></div>
          ${this._logOpen ? this._logPanelHtml(b) : ''}
        </div>
        <div class="battle-arena">
          <div class="battle-field battle-field-enemy">
            ${this._boardHtml(enemySide, enemyData, enemyLineInfo, enemyColInfo)}
          </div>
          <div class="battle-field battle-field-player">
            ${this._boardHtml(side, data, lineInfo, colInfo)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="battle-side-right">
          <div class="deck-counter">Колода: ${data.deck.length}</div>
          <div class="battle-hand-root" id="battle-hand-root"></div>
          <div class="battle-controls">
            <button class="btn-end-turn" data-action="end-turn" ${isHuman ? '' : 'disabled'}>Конец хода</button>
            <button class="btn-skip"     data-action="skip-turn" ${isHuman ? '' : 'disabled'}>Пропустить</button>
            <button class="btn-auto"     data-action="auto-play" ${this._autoPlayActive ? 'disabled' : ''}>⚡ Авто-бой</button>
            <button class="btn-log"      data-action="toggle-log">📜 ${this._logOpen ? 'Скрыть лог' : 'Лог'}</button>
            <button class="btn-retreat"  data-action="retreat" ${isHuman ? '' : 'disabled'}>Отступить</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    // ─── Анимация потраченной тактики ───
    if (playerSpent) this._flashTactic('player');
    if (enemySpent) this._flashTactic('enemy');
    // ─── Левая колонка: превью ───
    const previewRoot = this.el.querySelector('#battle-preview-panel-root');
    if (previewRoot) {
      this._previewPanel = new BattlePreviewPanel(previewRoot);
      this._previewPanel.setImageLoader(this._imgLoader);
      this._previewPanel.setPseudoHand(enemyData.hand.length);
      if (b.selectedCard && isHuman) {
        const cost = b.costFor(b.selectedCard, side);
        this._previewPanel.showCard(b.selectedCard, { effectiveCost: cost });
      }
    }
    // ─── Правая колонка: своя рука ───
    const handRoot = this.el.querySelector('#battle-hand-root');
    this._hand = new BattleHand(handRoot);
    this._hand.setData(
      isHuman ? data.hand : [],
      data.tacticPoints,
      b.selectedCard,
      (card) => this.controller.selectCard(card),
      (card) => b.costFor(card, side),
    );
    if (!isHuman) {
      handRoot.innerHTML = `<div class="bh-empty">Ход противника…</div>`;
    }
    const arena = this.el.querySelector('.battle-arena');
    if (arena) {
      if (this._visualLayer) {
        this._visualLayer.setContainer(arena);
        this._visualLayer.sideToPlayer = side;
      } else if (b) {
        this._visualLayer = new BattleVisualLayer(b, arena, { sideToPlayer: side });
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(() => this._visualLayer?.flush());
    }
  }
  _flashTactic(who) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const selector = who === 'player' ? '.player-hero .hero-tactic-mini' : '.enemy-hero .hero-tactic-mini';
      const el = this.el.querySelector(selector);
      if (!el) return;
      el.classList.add('tactic-spent');
      setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('tactic-spent'), 500);
    }, 30);
  }
  _logPanelHtml(b) {
    const entries = b.log.slice().reverse()
      .map((l) => `<div class="blf-entry ${l.type}">${l.msg}</div>`)
      .join('');
    return `
      <div class="battle-log-full">
        <div class="blf-header">
          <span>📜 Лог боя</span>
          <button class="blf-close" data-action="toggle-log" title="Скрыть">✕</button>
        </div>
        <div class="blf-list">${entries || '<div class="blf-entry info">Пока пусто</div>'}</div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  _portraitHtml(gen, cls) {
    const url = gen.portraitUrl
      ? (gen.portraitUrl.startsWith('/') ? gen.portraitUrl : imgUrl(gen.portraitUrl))
      : generalPortraitUrl(gen.factionId, gen.presetIndex ?? 0);
    const style = url ? `style="background-image:url('${url}')"` : '';
    return `<div class="hero-portrait ${cls}" data-faction="${gen.factionId}" ${style}></div>`;
  }
  _boardHtml(side, data, lineInfoForSide, colInfoForSide) {
    const b = this._battle;
    const isPlayerSide = side === this._currentSide();
    const humanSide = this._isHumanSide(side);
    const rows = LANES.map((lane) => {
      const lineInfo = lineInfoForSide[lane.key];
      const lineActive = lineInfo.active;
      const lineIcon = `
        <div class="lane-icon ${lineActive ? 'active' : ''}" data-lane-icon="1">
          <span>${lane.icon}</span>
          <div class="lane-icon-tip">
            <b>${lane.label}${lineActive ? ' ✔' : ''}</b><br>${lineInfo.desc}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      const cells = [0, 1, 2].map((i) => {
        const card = data.board.get(lane.key, i);
        let cls = 'cell';
        let inner = '';
        if (card) {
          const cost = b.costFor(card, side);
          inner = this._cardEl.create(card, { side, effectiveCost: cost }).outerHTML;
        }
        if (isPlayerSide && humanSide && b.selectedCard && !card) cls += ' valid-place';
        if (colInfoForSide[i].active) cls += ' col-active';
        return `<div class="${cls}" data-side="${side}" data-lane="${lane.key}" data-idx="${i}">${inner}</div>`;
      }).join('');
      return `<div class="battle-row">${lineIcon}${cells}</div>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<div class="battle-side ${isPlayerSide ? 'side-player' : 'side-enemy'}">${rows}</div>`;
  }
  _livesHtml(lives, max) {
    return Array.from({ length: max }, (_, i) =>
      `<span class="life-dot ${i >= lives ? 'lost' : ''}"></span>`
    ).join('');
  }
  _showCardCastAnimation(card, side, lane, index) {
    if (!this._imgLoader) return;
    const url = this._imgLoader.getUrl(card.imageKey);
    const cast = document.createElement('div');
    cast.className = 'card-cast-anim';
    if (url) cast.style.backgroundImage = `url("${url}")`;
    cast.innerHTML = `<div class="cc-name">${card.name}</div>`;
    this.el.appendChild(cast);
    // Через 600 мс — «приземление» в ячейку
    setTimeout(() => {
      cast.classList.add('landing');
      const targetCell = this.el.querySelector(
        `.cell[data-side="${side}"][data-lane="${lane}"][data-idx="${index}"]`
      );
      if (targetCell) {
        targetCell.classList.add('card-landing');
        setTimeout(() => targetCell.classList.remove('card-landing'), 500);
      }
      setTimeout(() => cast.remove(), 500);
    }, 600);
  }
}

import { CardDetailView } from './CardDetailView.js';
export class BattlePreviewPanel {
  constructor(root) {
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'battle-preview-panel';
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="bpp-pseudo-hand">
        <div class="bpp-pseudo-label">Рука врага</div>
        <div class="bpp-pseudo-cards" data-pseudo-cards></div>
      </div>
      <div class="bpp-card-view" data-card-view style="display:none"></div>
    `;
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this._pseudoCardsEl = this.el.querySelector('[data-pseudo-cards]');
    this._cardViewEl = this.el.querySelector('[data-card-view]');
    this._pseudoBoxEl = this.el.querySelector('.bpp-pseudo-hand');
    this._imgLoader = null;
  }
  setImageLoader(loader) {
    this._imgLoader = loader;
  }
  /** Обновить «руку врага» — компактные рубашки. */
  setPseudoHand(count) {
    this._pseudoCardsEl.innerHTML = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'bpp-pseudo-card';
      this._pseudoCardsEl.appendChild(el);
    }
  }
  /** Показать карту (полное описание). */
  showCard(card, { effectiveCost = null } = {}) {
    if (!card) { this.hideCard(); return; }
    this._cardViewEl.innerHTML = CardDetailView.html(card, {
      size: 'medium',
      showCost: effectiveCost,
    });
    CardDetailView.applyImages(this._cardViewEl, this._imgLoader);
    this._cardViewEl.style.display = '';
    // Псевдо-руку НЕ скрываем — она компактная и живёт сверху
  }
  hideCard() {
    this._cardViewEl.style.display = 'none';
    this._cardViewEl.innerHTML = '';
  }
}

import { CardImageLoader } from '../../infrastructure/three/visuals/CardImageLoader.js';
export class BattleResultScreen {
    constructor(parentOverlay, { title, kind, playerSide, attacker, defender, winner, onContinue }) {
        this._parent = parentOverlay;
        this._onContinue = onContinue;
        this._el = document.createElement('div');
        this._el.className = `battle-result-overlay result-${kind}`;
        const playerData = playerSide === 'attacker' ? attacker : defender;
        const enemyData = playerSide === 'attacker' ? defender : attacker;
        const survivors = (data) => {
            const cards = [];
            for (const lane of ['defense', 'attack', 'support']) {
                for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                    const c = data.board.get(lane, i);
                    if (c) cards.push({ card: c, lane, index: i, alive: c.str > 0 });
                }
            }
            return cards;
        };
        const playerSurvivors = survivors(playerData);
        const enemySurvivors = survivors(enemyData);
        this._el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="battle-result-panel">
        <h2 class="br-title">${title}</h2>
        <div class="br-subtitle">
          ${playerData.gen.name} vs ${enemyData.gen.name}
        </div>
        <div class="br-section">
          <div class="br-section-title">Ваши выжившие (${playerSurvivors.filter(x => x.alive).length})</div>
          <div class="br-grid">${this._cardsHtml(playerSurvivors)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="br-section">
          <div class="br-section-title">Выжившие врага (${enemySurvivors.filter(x => x.alive).length})</div>
          <div class="br-grid">${this._cardsHtml(enemySurvivors)}</div>
        </div>
        <button class="br-continue" data-result-continue="1">Продолжить</button>
      </div>
    `;
        this._el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target.closest?.('[data-result-continue]')) {
                this._onContinue?.();
            }
        });
        parentOverlay.appendChild(this._el);
    }
    _cardsHtml(list) {
        if (list.length === 0) return '<div class="br-empty">Пусто</div>';
        const loader = new CardImageLoader();
        return list.map(({ card, alive }) => {
            const url = loader.getUrl(card.imageKey);
            const bg = url ? `background-image:url("${url}")` : '';
            return `
        <div class="br-card ${alive ? 'alive' : 'dead'}" title="${card.name}">
          <div class="br-card-art" style="${bg}"></div>
          <div class="br-card-count">${card.str}</div>
          <div class="br-card-name">${card.name}</div>
        </div>
      `;
        }).join('');
    }
    destroy() {
        this._el.remove();
        this._el = null;
    }
}

📄 ui\components\CardDetailView.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
import { AbilityFactory } from '../../domain/battle/AbilityFactory.js';
import { factionOfCard } from '../../domain/collection/Collection.js';
/** Обёртка для цифр (зелёным). */
const green = (s) => `<span class="txt-green">${s}</span>`;
/** Обёртка для типов/параметров (синим). */
const blue = (s) => `<span class="txt-blue">${s}</span>`;
/** Раскраска текста способности: цифры — зелёным, типы — синим. */
function colorizeAbilityText(desc) {
  if (!desc) return '';
  // Сначала экранируем HTML
  let safe = String(desc)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  // 1) Цифры + знаки — зелёным (например, -1, +2, 3, ×1.5)
  safe = safe.replace(/(-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?(?:\s*[⚡❤💰🃏🌾]|\s*урон[а-я]*|\s*жизн[а-я]*)?)/g, (m) => green(m));
  // 2) Типы карт и параметры — синим
  //    типы: ОБОРОНУ, АТАКУ, ПОДДЕРЖКУ
  //    параметры: карте, карту, полководцу, полководца, ряда, колонки
  const BLUE_WORDS = [
    'ОБОРОНУ', 'АТАКУ', 'ПОДДЕРЖКУ',
    'оборону', 'атаку', 'поддержку',
    'карте', 'карту', 'картой', 'карт',
    'полководцу', 'полководца', 'полководцем',
    'ряд', 'ряда', 'ряду', 'ряды',
    'колонк', 'колонки', 'колонку',
    'в поддержке', 'в обороне', 'в атаке',
  ];
  for (const w of BLUE_WORDS) {
    const re = new RegExp(`(?<![а-яё])${w}(?![а-яё])`, 'gi');
    safe = safe.replace(re, (m) => blue(m));
  }
  return safe;
}
export class CardDetailView {
  static html(card, opts = {}) {
    const { size = 'large', showCost = null } = opts;
    const factionId = factionOfCard(card.key) || 'neutral';
    const rarity = card.rarity || 'common';
    const rarityLabel = {
      common: 'Обычная', rare: 'Редкая',
      epic: 'Эпическая', legendary: 'Легендарная',
    }[rarity] || rarity;
    const abilities = (card.abilityKeys || [])
      .map((id) => AbilityFactory.create(id))
      .filter(Boolean);
    const abilitiesHtml = abilities.length > 0
      ? abilities.map((ab, idx) => `
        <div class="cd-ability" data-ability-idx="${idx}">
          <span class="cd-ability-icon">${iconImg(`ability_${ab.id}`, { size: 16 })}</span>
          <span class="cd-ability-name" data-ability-name="${idx}">${ab.name}</span>
        </div>
      `).join('')
      : '<div class="cd-ability-empty">—</div>';
    // Тултип-слой: один на контейнер обилок, показывается по наведению на имя
    const tipsHtml = abilities.map((ab, idx) => `
      <div class="cd-ability-tip" data-ability-tip="${idx}">
        <b class="txt-gold">${ab.name}</b><br>
        ${colorizeAbilityText(ab.desc)}
      </div>
    `).join('');
    const cost = showCost != null
      ? `<div class="cd-cost-row">
           ${iconImg('res_tactic', { size: 12 })}<span>${showCost}</span>
         </div>`
      : '';
    return `
      <div class="card-detail size-${size} faction-${factionId}"
           data-image-key="${card.key}" data-card-type="${card.type}">
        <div class="cd-art">
          <div class="cd-rarity-badge rarity-${rarity}">
            <div class="cd-rarity-tip">${rarityLabel}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="cd-name-box">
          <div class="cd-name">${card.name}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="cd-abilities-box">
          <div class="cd-count-row">
            ${iconImg('stat_str', { size: 14 })}<span>${card.str}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="cd-abilities-list">
            ${abilitiesHtml}
          </div>
          ${cost}
          ${abilities.length > 0 ? `
            <div class="cd-ability-tip-layer">
              ${tipsHtml}
            </div>
          ` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  static applyImages(container, imgLoader) {
    if (!container || !imgLoader) return;
    container.querySelectorAll('.card-detail').forEach((el) => {
      const key = el.getAttribute('data-image-key');
      const type = el.getAttribute('data-card-type') || 'warrior';
      const art = el.querySelector('.cd-art');
      if (art) imgLoader.applyBackground(art, key, type);
    });
    this.attachHover(container);
  }
  static attachHover(container) {
    if (!container) return;
    container.querySelectorAll('.card-detail').forEach((cardEl) => {
      const names = cardEl.querySelectorAll('.cd-ability-name');
      names.forEach((nameEl) => {
        const idx = nameEl.getAttribute('data-ability-name');
        const tip = cardEl.querySelector(`.cd-ability-tip[data-ability-tip="${idx}"]`);
        if (!tip) return;
        nameEl.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => tip.classList.add('visible'));
        nameEl.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => tip.classList.remove('visible'));
      });
    });
  }
}

📄 ui\components\CityPanel.js

import { CardImageLoader } from '../../infrastructure/three/visuals/CardImageLoader.js';
import { generalPortraitUrl } from '../../config.js';
import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
export class CityPanel {
  constructor(root, { bus }) {
    this.bus = bus;
    this.onBuySettlement = null;
    this.onAttackCity = null;
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'city-panel-overlay';
    this.el.innerHTML = `<div class="city-panel"></div>`;
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this._world = null;
    this._city = null;
    this._tile = null;
    this._imgLoader = new CardImageLoader();
    this.el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === this.el) { this.hide(); return; }
      const close = e.target.getAttribute?.('data-close');
      if (close !== null) { this.hide(); return; }
      const hireGen = e.target.getAttribute?.('data-hire-general');
      if (hireGen !== null) { this._tryHireGeneral(); return; }
      const buySett = e.target.getAttribute?.('data-buy-settlement');
      if (buySett !== null) {
        const cityId = this._city?.id;
        if (cityId) this.onBuySettlement?.(cityId);
        this.hide();
        return;
      }
      const atk = e.target.getAttribute?.('data-attack-city');
      if (atk !== null) {
        const city = this._city;
        if (!city) return;
        const defenderFactionId = city.settlement
          ? (city.neutralFaction || 'tauri')
          : (city.factionId || 'goths');
        this.onAttackCity?.(city.id, defenderFactionId);
        this.hide();
        return;
      }
    });
  }
  open({ tile, city, world }) {
    if (!city || !world || !tile) return;
    this._tile = tile;
    this._city = city;
    this._world = world;
    this.el.classList.add('visible');
    this._render();
  }
  hide() { this.el.classList.remove('visible'); }
  _tryHireGeneral() {
    if (!this._world || !this._tile) return;
    const result = this._world.recruit.recruitGeneral({
      factionId: this._world.playerFactionId,
      tileKey: this._tile.key,
    });
    if (!result.ok) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: result.reason });
      return;
    }
    this.bus.emit('resources:changed', { factionId: this._world.playerFactionId });
    this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `Нанят: ${result.general.name}` });
    // ─── Новое: сцена должна обновиться ───
    this.bus.emit('world:generalsChanged', { general: result.general });
    this._render();
  }
  _portraitStyle(general) {
    const idx = general.presetIndex ?? 0;
    const url = generalPortraitUrl(general.factionId, idx);
    if (!url) return '';
    return `background-image:url('${url}');background-size:cover;background-position:center top;`;
  }
  /** Найти защитника (нейтрального или отколовшегося). */
  _getDefender() {
    const world = this._world;
    const city = this._city;
    if (!world || !city) return null;
    // Для отколовшегося
    if (world._rebelDefenders?.has(city.id)) {
      return world._rebelDefenders.get(city.id);
    }
    // Для нейтральной деревни
    if (city.settlement && city.neutralFaction) {
      return world.getSettlementDefender(city);
    }
    return null;
  }
  /** Стиль портрета защитника. */
  _defenderPortraitStyle(def) {
    if (!def) return '';
    if (def.portraitUrl) {
      const url = def.portraitUrl.startsWith('/') ? def.portraitUrl : `/images/${def.portraitUrl}`;
      return `background-image:url('${url}');background-size:cover;background-position:center top;`;
    }
    // Fallback — общий портрет фракции
    const url = generalPortraitUrl(def.factionId, def.presetIndex ?? 0);
    if (!url) return '';
    return `background-image:url('${url}');background-size:cover;background-position:center top;`;
  }
  /** Секция обороны для своего города. */
  _renderDefenseSection() {
    const world = this._world;
    const city = this._city;
    if (!world || !city) return '';
    const d = world.cityDefense?.get(city.id);
    if (!d) return '';
    const pct = d.maxGarrison > 0 ? Math.round((d.garrison / d.maxGarrison) * 100) : 0;
    return `
      <div class="cp-section">
        <div class="cp-section-title">Оборона</div>
        <div class="cp-defense-bar">
          <div class="cp-defense-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></div>
          <span class="cp-defense-label">${d.garrison} / ${d.maxGarrison}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="cp-hint">Гарнизон восстанавливается +1 за ход</div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  /** Секция защитника-нейтрала/отколовшегося (для независимых городов). */
  _renderDefenderSection() {
    const def = this._getDefender();
    if (!def) return '';
    const portrait = this._defenderPortraitStyle(def);
    const factionLabel = def.factionId === 'cimmerians' ? 'Киммерийцы' : 'Тавры';
    const livesHtml = Array.from({ length: def.maxLives }, (_, i) =>
      `<span class="cp-life ${i >= def.lives ? 'lost' : ''}"></span>`
    ).join('');
    return `
      <div class="cp-section">
        <div class="cp-section-title">Защитник</div>
        <div class="cp-defender-row">
          <div class="cp-defender-portrait" style="${portrait}"></div>
          <div class="cp-defender-info">
            <div class="cp-defender-name">${def.name}</div>
            <div class="cp-defender-faction">${factionLabel}</div>
            <div class="cp-defender-lives">${livesHtml}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  _render() {
    const world = this._world;
    const city = this._city;
    if (!world || !city) return;
    const factionId = world.playerFactionId;
    if (!factionId) return;
    const income = world.economy.computeIncome(world, factionId);
    const generals = world.generals.filter((g) => g.factionId === factionId && g.alive);
    const citiesOfMine = world.cities.filter((c) => c.factionId === factionId);
    const myCities = citiesOfMine.filter((c) => c.isCity).length;
    const myVillages = citiesOfMine.filter((c) => !c.isCity).length;
    const isMine = city.factionId === factionId;
    const maxGenerals = Math.max(1, myCities + 1);
    const canHireGeneral = isMine && city.isCity && generals.length < maxGenerals;
    const cityGeneral = world.getGeneralAt(this._tile.hex);
    const typeLabel = city.isCity ? 'Город' : city.settlement ? 'Поселение' : 'Деревня';
    const ownerLabel = isMine
      ? 'Ваш'
      : city.settlement
        ? `Нейтральное (${factionLabel(city.neutralFaction)})`
        : city.factionId
          ? factionLabel(city.factionId)
          : 'Ничейное';
    let canAttackHere = false;
    let attackCost = null;
    if (!isMine && world.selectedGeneralId) {
      const g = world.generals.find((x) => x.id === world.selectedGeneralId);
      if (g && world.movement.canAttack(g, this._tile)) {
        canAttackHere = true;
        attackCost = world.movement.attackCostTo(g, this._tile);
      }
    }
    const generalsHtml = generals.map((g) => {
      const inCity = g.hex.key === this._tile.key;
      return `
        <div class="cp-general-row">
          <div class="cp-general-portrait" style="${this._portraitStyle(g)}"></div>
          <div class="cp-general-info">
            <div class="cp-general-name">${g.name}</div>
            <div class="cp-general-stats">
              ${iconImg('general_life', { size: 11 })} ${g.lives}/${g.maxLives}
              · ${iconImg('action_move', { size: 11 })} ${g.movePoints}/${g.maxMovePoints}
              · ${iconImg('general_capacity', { size: 11 })} ${g.deck.cards.length}
              ${inCity ? '· <b style="color:#3fb950">здесь</b>' : ''}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('') || '<div class="cp-empty">Нет полководцев</div>';
    const restrHtml = city.settlement
      ? `<div class="cp-restr">${iconImg('ui_help', { size: 11 })} В поселении: только <b>common</b> / <b>rare</b>, цена ×1.5</div>`
      : '';
    // Покупка деревни доступна, только если соседствует с нашей территорией
    const canBuyHere = !isMine && city.settlement && this._isAdjacentToFaction(factionId);
    const buySettlementHtml = canBuyHere
      ? `<button class="btn primary cp-buy-settlement" data-buy-settlement="1">
           🏛 Купить поселение · 40${iconImg('res_gold', { size: 12 })}
         </button>`
      : (!isMine && city.settlement
        ? `<div class="cp-hint">Покупка недоступна: деревня не граничит с вашей территорией</div>`
        : '');
    const attackHtml = canAttackHere
      ? `<button class="btn primary cp-attack-btn" data-attack-city="1">
           ⚔ Атаковать · ${attackCost}${iconImg('action_move', { size: 12 })}
         </button>`
      : (!isMine
        ? `<div class="cp-hint">Выберите полководца в радиусе, чтобы атаковать</div>`
        : '');
    const defenseSection = isMine ? this._renderDefenseSection() : '';
    const defenderSection = !isMine ? this._renderDefenderSection() : '';
    this.el.querySelector('.city-panel').innerHTML = `
      <h2>${city.name}</h2>
      <div class="cp-subtitle">
        ${typeLabel} · ${ownerLabel}
        ${cityGeneral ? ` · Полководец: ${cityGeneral.name}` : ''}
      </div>
      ${isMine ? `
      <div class="cp-section">
        <div class="cp-section-title">Империя</div>
        <div class="cp-stat-row"><span>Гексы</span><b>${world.map.landTiles().filter((t) => t.factionId === factionId).length}</b></div>
        <div class="cp-stat-row"><span>Города</span><b>${myCities}</b></div>
        <div class="cp-stat-row"><span>Деревни/поселения</span><b>${myVillages}</b></div>
        <div class="cp-stat-row"><span>Доход</span><b>
          ${iconImg('res_gold', { size: 11 })}${income.gold}
          ${iconImg('res_prov', { size: 11 })}${income.prov}
          ${iconImg('res_weapons', { size: 11 })}${income.weapons}
        </b></div>
        <div class="cp-stat-row"><span>Очки найма</span><b>${world.recruitPointsUsed(factionId)}/${world.economy.recruitPointsForFaction(world, factionId)}</b></div>
      </div>
      ` : ''}
      ${defenseSection}
      ${defenderSection}
      ${restrHtml}
      ${isMine ? `
      <div class="cp-section">
        <div class="cp-section-title">Полководцы (${generals.length}/${maxGenerals})</div>
        ${generalsHtml}
        ${canHireGeneral ? `
          <button class="btn primary cp-hire-btn" data-hire-general="1">
            Нанять полководца · 30${iconImg('res_gold', { size: 12 })}
          </button>
        ` : ''}
        ${isMine && city.isCity && generals.length >= maxGenerals
          ? `<div class="cp-hint">Максимум полководцев — постройте больше городов</div>`
          : ''}
      </div>
      ` : ''}
      ${attackHtml}
      ${buySettlementHtml}
      <button class="btn cp-close" data-close="1">Закрыть</button>
    `;
  }
  /** Есть ли у фракции гекс, соседний с городом. */
  _isAdjacentToFaction(factionId) {
    if (!this._world || !this._tile) return false;
    for (const n of this._tile.hex.neighbors()) {
      const nt = this._world.map.get(n.q, n.r);
      if (nt && nt.factionId === factionId) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function factionLabel(factionId) {
  return {
    scythians: 'Скифы', bosporus: 'Боспор', chersonesos: 'Херсонес',
    rome: 'Рим', goths: 'Готы', tauri: 'Тавры', cimmerians: 'Киммерийцы',
  }[factionId] || factionId || '';
}

export class ConfirmDialog {
    constructor(root, { title, text, confirmText, cancelText, onConfirm, onCancel }) {
      this.overlay = document.createElement('div');
      this.overlay.className = 'confirm-overlay';
      this.overlay.innerHTML = `
        <div class="confirm-dialog">
          <h2>${title}</h2>
          <p>${text}</p>
          <div class="confirm-actions">
            <button class="btn confirm-cancel">${cancelText || 'Отмена'}</button>
            <button class="btn primary confirm-ok">${confirmText || 'Да'}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      this.overlay.querySelector('.confirm-ok').addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.overlay.remove();
        onConfirm?.();
      });
      this.overlay.querySelector('.confirm-cancel').addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.overlay.remove();
        onCancel?.();
      });
      root.appendChild(this.overlay);
    }
    open() { /* уже добавлен в DOM */ }
  }

import { CardImageLoader } from '../../infrastructure/three/visuals/CardImageLoader.js';
export class DeckPanel {
  constructor(root) {
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'deck-panel';
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this._general = null;
    this._imgLoader = new CardImageLoader();
  }
  show(general) {
    if (!general) { this.hide(); return; }
    this._general = general;
    this.el.classList.add('visible');
    this._render();
  }
  hide() {
    this.el.classList.remove('visible');
    this._general = null;
  }
  refresh() {
    if (this._general) this._render();
  }
  _render() {
    const g = this._general;
    if (!g) return;
    const cards = g.deck.cards;
    const empty = cards.length === 0 ? `<div class="dp-empty">Колода пуста</div>` : '';
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="dp-title">Колода · ${g.name} · ${g.deck.totalStrength}/${g.deck.capacity}</div>
      ${empty}
      <div class="dp-grid"></div>
    `;
    const grid = this.el.querySelector('.dp-grid');
    if (!grid) return;
    for (const c of cards) {
      const url = this._imgLoader.getUrl(c.imageKey);
      const cardEl = document.createElement('div');
      cardEl.className = `dp-card type-${c.type}`;
      cardEl.title = c.name;
      cardEl.innerHTML = `
        <div class="dp-art">
          <div class="dp-count">${c.str}</div>
          <div class="dp-type-stripe"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="dp-name-bottom">${c.name}</div>
      `;
      const art = cardEl.querySelector('.dp-art');
      if (url && art) art.style.backgroundImage = `url("${url}")`;
      grid.appendChild(cardEl);
    }
  }
}

import { Theme } from '../../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
export class FactionsPanel {
  constructor(root, { world }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.overlay = document.createElement('div');
    this.overlay.className = 'factions-panel-overlay';
    this.overlay.innerHTML = `<div class="factions-panel"></div>`;
    root.appendChild(this.overlay);
    this.overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === this.overlay) this.hide();
      if (e.target.closest?.('[data-close]')) this.hide();
    });
  }
  open() {
    this.overlay.classList.add('visible');
    this._render();
  }
  hide() { this.overlay.classList.remove('visible'); }
  _render() {
    const rows = this.world.factions.map((f) => {
      const tiles = this.world.map.landTiles().filter((t) => t.factionId === f.id).length;
      const cities = this.world.cities.filter((c) => c.factionId === f.id && c.isCity).length;
      const villages = this.world.cities.filter((c) => c.factionId === f.id && !c.isCity).length;
      const generals = this.world.generals.filter((g) => g.factionId === f.id && g.alive);
      const totalLives = generals.reduce((s, g) => s + g.lives, 0);
      const totalCards = generals.reduce((s, g) => s + g.deck.cards.length, 0);
      const pool = this.world.resourcePools.get(f.id);
      const color = '#' + (Theme.faction[f.id] || 0x888888).toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
      return `
        <tr class="${f.isPlayer ? 'mine' : ''}">
          <td><span class="fp-dot" style="background:${color}"></span>${f.name}</td>
          <td>${tiles}</td>
          <td>${cities} / ${villages}</td>
          <td>${generals.length} (❤ ${totalLives})</td>
          <td>${totalCards}</td>
          <td>
            ${iconImg('res_gold',    { size: 11 })} ${pool.gold} ·
            ${iconImg('res_prov',    { size: 11 })} ${pool.prov} ·
            ${iconImg('res_weapons', { size: 11 })} ${pool.weapons}
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join('');
    this.overlay.querySelector('.factions-panel').innerHTML = `
      <h2>Фракции Тавриды</h2>
      <div class="fp-subtitle">Ход ${this.world.turn}</div>
      <table class="fp-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Фракция</th>
            <th>Гексы</th>
            <th>Города/Деревни</th>
            <th>Полководцы</th>
            <th>Карты</th>
            <th>Ресурсы</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
      </table>
      <button class="fp-close" data-close="1">Закрыть</button>
    `;
  }
}

import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
/**
 * Нижний футер с круглой кнопкой «Пропустить ход».
 * Позже сюда можно добавить ещё элементы (ресурсы, хоткеи, лог-статистику).
 */
export class FooterPanel {
  constructor(root, { onSkipTurn }) {
    this.root = root;
    this.onSkipTurn = onSkipTurn;
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'game-footer';
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <button class="skip-turn-btn" id="skip-turn-btn">
        ${iconImg('ui_hourglass', { size: 22 })}
        <span class="stt-tooltip">Пропустить ход</span>
      </button>
    `;
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this._btn = this.el.querySelector('#skip-turn-btn');
    this._btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (this._btn.disabled) return;
      this.onSkipTurn?.();
    });
  }
  setEnabled(enabled) {
    if (this._btn) this._btn.disabled = !enabled;
  }
}

import { Theme } from '../../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
import { generalPortraitUrl } from '../../config.js';
import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
export class GeneralPanel {
  constructor(root) {
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'general-panel';
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this._general = null;
    this._collapsed = false;
    this._flashTimer = null;
    document.addEventListener('general:no-move-points-ui', (e) => this._flashNoMovePoints(e.detail));
  }
  _flashNoMovePoints({ needed, have }) {
    const toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.className = 'gp-no-points';
    toast.textContent = `Нужно ${needed} очков, есть ${have}`;
    this.el.appendChild(toast);
    this.el.classList.add('gp-flash-error');
    clearTimeout(this._flashTimer);
    this._flashTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.el.classList.remove('gp-flash-error');
      toast.remove();
    }, 1600);
  }
  show(general) {
    if (!general) { this.hide(); return; }
    this._general = general;
    this.el.classList.add('visible');
    this._render();
  }
  hide() {
    this.el.classList.remove('visible');
    this._general = null;
  }
  refresh() { if (this._general) this._render(); }
  _toggle() {
    this._collapsed = !this._collapsed;
    this.el.classList.toggle('collapsed', this._collapsed);
    this._render();
  }
  _bar(pct, cls, text) {
    return `
      <div class="gp-bar">
        <div class="gp-fill ${cls}" style="width:${pct}%"></div>
        <span class="gp-bar-text">${text}</span>
      </div>`;
  }
  _render() {
    const g = this._general;
    if (!g) return;
    const factionName = Theme.factionName[g.factionId] || g.factionId;
    const presetIndex = g.presetIndex ?? 0;
    const url = generalPortraitUrl(g.factionId, presetIndex);
    const emblemStyle = url
      ? `background-image:url('${url}');background-size:cover;background-position:center top;`
      : '';
    const livesPct = Math.round((g.lives / g.maxLives) * 100);
    const movePct = Math.round((g.movePoints / g.maxMovePoints) * 100);
    const xpPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((g.xp / g.xpToNext) * 100));
    const perksHtml = g.perks.length > 0 && !this._collapsed
      ? `<div class="gp-perks">${g.perks.map((p) => this._perkIcon(p)).join(' ')}</div>`
      : '';
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <button class="gp-toggle" title="${this._collapsed ? 'Развернуть' : 'Свернуть'}">
        ${this._collapsed ? '▾' : '▴'}
      </button>
      <div class="gp-compact">
        <div class="gp-ava" style="${emblemStyle}"></div>
        <div class="gp-compact-bars">
          ${this._bar(livesPct, 'gp-fill-lives', `${g.lives}/${g.maxLives}`)}
          ${this._bar(movePct, 'gp-fill-move', `${g.movePoints}/${g.maxMovePoints}`)}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="gp-full">
        <div class="gp-header">
          <div class="gp-emblem" style="${emblemStyle}"></div>
          <div class="gp-head-text">
            <div class="gp-name">${g.name} <span class="gp-level">${g.level}</span></div>
            <div class="gp-faction">${factionName}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="gp-bars">
          ${this._bar(livesPct, 'gp-fill-lives', `Жизни ${g.lives}/${g.maxLives}`)}
          ${this._bar(movePct, 'gp-fill-move', `Ход ${g.movePoints}/${g.maxMovePoints}`)}
        </div>
        <div class="gp-xp">
          <div class="gp-xp-bar"><div class="gp-xp-fill" style="width:${xpPct}%"></div></div>
        </div>
        ${perksHtml}
      </div>
    `;
    this.el.querySelector('.gp-toggle').addEventListener('click', () => this._toggle());
  }
  _perkIcon(perkId) {
    const iconKey = perkId.includes('move') ? 'action_move'
                  : perkId.includes('lives') ? 'general_life'
                  : 'general_capacity';
    return `<span class="gp-perk" title="${perkId}">${iconImg(iconKey, { size: 14 })}</span>`;
  }
}

const TERMS = [
    { t: 'Гекс', d: 'Шестиугольная клетка карты. Основная единица территории.' },
    { t: 'Террейн', d: 'Тип местности: равнина, лес, горы, степь, город, руины. Влияет на стоимость движения и бонусы в бою.' },
    { t: 'AP (движение)', d: 'Очки движения полководца за ход. Лес — 2, горы — 3, равнина/степь — 1. Дороги снижают до 1.' },

        this.overlay.classList.add('visible');
        this._render();
    }
    hide() { this.overlay.classList.remove('visible'); }
    _render() {
        const items = TERMS.map((x) => `

          <div class="gl-term">${x.t}</div>

        <div class="gl-subtitle">Термины и механики игры</div>
        <div class="gl-list">${items}</div>
        <button class="gl-close" data-close="1">Закрыть</button>
      `;

📄 ui\components\MarketPanel.js

import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
const GOODS_LABEL = { weapons: 'Оружие', prov: 'Провизия' };
const GOODS_ICON = { weapons: 'res_weapons', prov: 'res_prov' };
export class MarketPanel {
  constructor(root, { bus, controller, world }) {
    this.bus = bus;
    this.controller = controller;
    this.world = world;
    this._tab = 'buy';   // 'buy' | 'mine'
    this.overlay = document.createElement('div');
    this.overlay.className = 'market-panel-overlay';
    this.overlay.innerHTML = `<div class="market-panel"></div>`;
    root.appendChild(this.overlay);
    this.overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === this.overlay) { this.hide(); return; }
      if (e.target.closest?.('[data-close]')) { this.hide(); return; }
      const tab = e.target.closest?.('[data-tab]')?.getAttribute('data-tab');
      if (tab) { this._tab = tab; this._render(); return; }
      const buy = e.target.closest?.('[data-buy]');
      if (buy) {
        this.controller.tryBuy({ offerId: buy.getAttribute('data-buy') });
        this._render();
      }
    });
    this.bus.on('market:changed', () => {
      if (this.overlay.classList.contains('visible')) this._render();
    });
  }
  open() {
    this.overlay.classList.add('visible');
    this._render();
  }
  hide() { this.overlay.classList.remove('visible'); }
  _render() {
    const world = this.world;
    const factionId = world.playerFactionId;
    if (!factionId) return;
    const pool = world.resourcePools.get(factionId);
    const myOffers = world.market.offersBy(factionId);
    const others = world.market.offersFromOthers(factionId);
    const tabsHtml = `
      <div class="mp-tabs">
        <button class="mp-tab ${this._tab === 'buy' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="buy">
          Купить (${others.length})
        </button>
        <button class="mp-tab ${this._tab === 'mine' ? 'active' : ''}" data-tab="mine">
          Мои лоты (${myOffers.length})
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
    let contentHtml = '';
    if (this._tab === 'buy') {
      if (others.length === 0) {
        contentHtml = '<div class="mp-empty">На рынке пусто. Излишки появятся после хода.</div>';
      } else {
        contentHtml = `<div class="mp-grid">${others.map((o) => this._offerHtml(o, 'buy')).join('')}</div>`;
      }
    } else {
      if (myOffers.length === 0) {
        contentHtml = '<div class="mp-empty">У вас нет лотов. Копите ресурсы — излишки уйдут на рынок автоматически.</div>';
      } else {
        contentHtml = `<div class="mp-grid">${myOffers.map((o) => this._offerHtml(o, 'mine')).join('')}</div>`;
      }
    }
    this.overlay.querySelector('.market-panel').innerHTML = `
      <h2>Рынок излишков</h2>
      <div class="mp-subtitle">
        ${iconImg('res_gold', { size: 12 })} ${pool.gold}
        · ${iconImg('res_prov', { size: 12 })} ${pool.prov}
        · ${iconImg('res_weapons', { size: 12 })} ${pool.weapons}
        <br>Лоты создаются автоматически при излишке ресурсов.
      </div>
      ${tabsHtml}
      <div class="mp-content">${contentHtml}</div>
      <button class="mp-close" data-close="1">Закрыть</button>
    `;
  }
  _offerHtml(offer, mode) {
    const label = GOODS_LABEL[offer.goods] || offer.goods;
    const icon = GOODS_ICON[offer.goods] || 'res_gold';
    const neutralBadge = offer.neutral
      ? `<div class="mp-neutral-badge" title="Нейтральный торговец">Н</div>`
      : '';
    const action = mode === 'buy'
      ? `<button class="mp-action buy" data-buy="${offer.id}">
           Купить · ${offer.price}${iconImg('res_gold', { size: 10 })}
         </button>`
      : `<div class="mp-action mine-label">${this._sellerName(offer.sellerFactionId)}</div>`;
    return `
      <div class="mp-card goods-${offer.goods} ${offer.neutral ? 'neutral-offer' : ''}">
        ${neutralBadge}
        <div class="mp-art mp-goods-art">
          <div class="mp-count">${offer.amount}${iconImg(icon, { size: 12, cls: 'icon-inline' })}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="mp-name">${label}</div>
        <div class="mp-seller">Цена: ${offer.price}${iconImg('res_gold', { size: 10 })}</div>
        <div class="mp-seller">${offer.neutral ? this._sellerName(offer.sellerFactionId) : ''}</div>
        ${action}
      </div>
    `;
  }
  _sellerName(factionId) {
    const f = this.world.factions.find((x) => x.id === factionId);
    return f ? f.name : factionId;
  }
}

📄 ui\components\Minimap.js

import { Theme } from '../../infrastructure/config/ThemeConfig.js';
export class Minimap {
  constructor(root, { world, cameraController, onCenter }) {
    this.world = world;
    this.cameraController = cameraController;
    this.onCenter = onCenter;
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'qj-minimap-inner';
    this.canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    this.canvas.width = 200;
    this.canvas.height = 200;
    this.canvas.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';    // ← гарантированно ловит клик
    this.canvas.style.cursor = 'crosshair';
    this.canvas.title = 'Клик — перейти к точке';
    this.el.style.position = 'relative';
    this.el.style.width = '100%';
    this.el.style.height = '100%';
    this.el.appendChild(this.canvas);
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
    this._scale = null;
    this._offset = null;
    this.canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => this._onClick(e));
    this.refresh();
  }
  refresh() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const W = this.canvas.width;
    const H = this.canvas.height;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    const tiles = this.world.map.all();
    if (tiles.length === 0) return;
    let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minZ = Infinity, maxZ = -Infinity;
    for (const t of tiles) {
      const { x, z } = t.hex.toWorld(1);
      if (x < minX) minX = x;
      if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
      if (z < minZ) minZ = z;
      if (z > maxZ) maxZ = z;
    }
    const pad = 10;
    const sx = (W - pad * 2) / (maxX - minX || 1);
    const sy = (H - pad * 2) / (maxZ - minZ || 1);
    const scale = Math.min(sx, sy);
    const offX = pad + ((W - pad * 2) - (maxX - minX) * scale) / 2;
    const offZ = pad + ((H - pad * 2) - (maxZ - minZ) * scale) / 2;
    this._scale = scale;
    this._offset = { offX, offZ, minX, minZ };
    for (const t of tiles) {
      if (!t.isLand) continue;
      const { x, z } = t.hex.toWorld(1);
      const cx = offX + (x - minX) * scale;
      const cy = offZ + (z - minZ) * scale;
      ctx.beginPath();
      const r = Math.max(2, scale * 0.55);
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      if (t.factionId) {
        const c = Theme.faction[t.factionId] || 0x888888;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#' + c.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
      } else {
        ctx.fillStyle = '#3a3028';
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = t.isVisible ? 1 : 0.4;
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    for (const city of this.world.cities) {
      if (!city.tileKey) continue;
      const tile = this.world.map.getByKey(city.tileKey);
      if (!tile) continue;
      const { x, z } = tile.hex.toWorld(1);
      const cx = offX + (x - minX) * scale;
      const cy = offZ + (z - minZ) * scale;
      const isMine = city.factionId === this.world.playerFactionId;
      ctx.fillStyle = isMine
        ? (city.isCity ? '#f5d57a' : '#d4a94c')
        : (city.isCity ? '#8a7a58' : '#5a4a30');
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, Math.max(2.5, scale * (isMine ? 0.5 : 0.35)), 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      if (isMine) {
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff3d0';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    for (const gen of this.world.generals) {
      if (!gen.alive) continue;
      const { x, z } = gen.hex.toWorld(1);
      const cx = offX + (x - minX) * scale;
      const cy = offZ + (z - minZ) * scale;
      const isMine = gen.factionId === this.world.playerFactionId;
      ctx.strokeStyle = isMine ? '#fff3d0' : '#fff';
      ctx.lineWidth = isMine ? 2 : 1.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, Math.max(3, scale * (isMine ? 0.7 : 0.5)), 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      const c = Theme.faction[gen.factionId] || 0xffffff;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#' + c.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, Math.max(2.5, scale * (isMine ? 0.6 : 0.42)), 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  _onClick(e) {
    if (!this._scale) return;
    const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    // Учитываем возможный CSS-scale
    const px = (e.clientX - rect.left) * (this.canvas.width / rect.width);
    const py = (e.clientY - rect.top)  * (this.canvas.height / rect.height);
    const { offX, offZ, minX, minZ } = this._offset;
    const worldX = (px - offX) / this._scale + minX;
    const worldZ = (py - offZ) / this._scale + minZ;
    let best = null;
    let bestD = Infinity;
    for (const t of this.world.map.all()) {
      if (!t.isLand) continue;
      const { x, z } = t.hex.toWorld(1);
      const d = (x - worldX) ** 2 + (z - worldZ) ** 2;
      if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = t; }
    }
    if (best) this.onCenter?.(best);
  }
}

📄 ui\components\NotificationCenter.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
const KIND_ICON = {
  info: 'ui_help',
  success: 'ui_check',
  danger: 'ui_skull',
  gold: 'res_gold',
  trade: 'res_weapons',
  battle: 'action_attack',
  turn: 'ui_hourglass',
};
export class NotificationCenter {
  constructor(root, { max = 20 } = {}) {
    this.max = max;
    this._items = [];
    this._nextId = 1;
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'notification-center';
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this._render();
  }
  push({ text, kind = 'info' }) {
    if (!text) return;
    this._items.unshift({
      id: this._nextId++,
      text, kind,
      time: Date.now(),
      unread: true,
    });
    if (this._items.length > this.max) this._items.length = this.max;
    this._render();
  }
  _render() {
    if (this._items.length === 0) {
      this.el.innerHTML = '';
      return;
    }
    this.el.innerHTML = this._items.map((it) => {
      const icon = KIND_ICON[it.kind] || 'ui_help';
      return `
        <div class="nc-icon kind-${it.kind} ${it.unread ? 'unread' : ''}" data-nc-id="${it.id}">
          <div class="nc-icon-glyph">${iconImg(icon, { size: 18 })}</div>
          <div class="nc-text">${it.text}</div>
          ${it.unread ? '<div class="nc-dot"></div>' : ''}
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
    this.el.querySelectorAll('.nc-icon').forEach((node) => {
      node.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const id = parseInt(node.getAttribute('data-nc-id'), 10);
        const item = this._items.find((x) => x.id === id);
        if (item) {
          item.unread = false;
          node.classList.remove('unread');
          node.querySelector('.nc-dot')?.remove();
        }
      });
    });
  }
}

📄 ui\components\QuickJump.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
import { Minimap } from './Minimap.js';
export class QuickJump {
  constructor(root, { world, cameraController, theme }) {
    this.root = root;
    this.world = world;
    this.cameraController = cameraController;
    this.theme = theme;
    this._generalIdx = 0;
    this._cityIdx = 0;
    this._minimap = null;
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'quick-jump';
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="qj-buttons">
        <button class="qj-btn" data-jump="general" title="К полководцу">
          ${iconImg('general_life', { size: 18 })}
        </button>
        <button class="qj-btn qj-btn-cycle" data-jump="next-general" title="Следующий полководец">
          ${iconImg('ui_arrow_right', { size: 14 })}
        </button>
        <button class="qj-btn" data-jump="city" title="К городу">
          ${iconImg('terrain_city', { size: 18 })}
        </button>
        <button class="qj-btn qj-btn-cycle" data-jump="next-city" title="Следующий город">
          ${iconImg('ui_arrow_right', { size: 14 })}
        </button>
      </div>
      <div class="qj-minimap" id="qj-minimap"></div>
    `;
    root.appendChild(this.el);
    this.el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const jump = e.target.closest?.('[data-jump]')?.getAttribute('data-jump');
      if (!jump) return;
      this._handle(jump);
    });
  }
  initMinimap({ world, cameraController, onCenter }) {
    if (this._minimap) return;
    const slot = this.el.querySelector('#qj-minimap');
    if (!slot) return;
    // Создаём миникарту
    this._minimap = new Minimap(slot, { world, cameraController, onCenter });
    // Переносим кнопки внутрь миникарты
    const buttons = this.el.querySelector('.qj-buttons');
    if (buttons && slot) {
      slot.appendChild(buttons);
    }
  }
  refreshMinimap() { this._minimap?.refresh(); }
  setWorld(world) {
    this.world = world;
    this._generalIdx = 0;
    this._cityIdx = 0;
    if (this._minimap) { this._minimap.world = world; this._minimap.refresh(); }
  }
  _myGenerals() {
    if (!this.world?.playerFactionId) return [];
    return this.world.generals.filter((g) => g.factionId === this.world.playerFactionId && g.alive);
  }
  _myCities() {
    if (!this.world?.playerFactionId) return [];
    return this.world.cities.filter((c) => c.factionId === this.world.playerFactionId && c.tileKey);
  }
  _centerOnHex(hex) {
    if (!hex) return;
    this.cameraController?.centerOn(hex, this.theme.hexSize);
  }
  _handle(action) {
    switch (action) {
      case 'general': {
        const gens = this._myGenerals();
        if (!gens.length) return;
        this._generalIdx = 0;
        this._centerOnHex(gens[0].hex);
        return;
      }
      case 'next-general': {
        const gens = this._myGenerals();
        if (!gens.length) return;
        this._generalIdx = (this._generalIdx + 1) % gens.length;
        this._centerOnHex(gens[this._generalIdx].hex);
        return;
      }
      case 'city': {
        const cities = this._myCities();
        if (!cities.length) return;
        this._cityIdx = 0;
        const tile = this.world.map.getByKey(cities[0].tileKey);
        if (tile) this._centerOnHex(tile.hex);
        return;
      }
      case 'next-city': {
        const cities = this._myCities();
        if (!cities.length) return;
        this._cityIdx = (this._cityIdx + 1) % cities.length;
        const tile = this.world.map.getByKey(cities[this._cityIdx].tileKey);
        if (tile) this._centerOnHex(tile.hex);
        return;
      }
      case 'prev-general': {
        const gens = this._myGenerals();
        if (!gens.length) return;
        this._generalIdx = (this._generalIdx - 1 + gens.length) % gens.length;
        this._centerOnHex(gens[this._generalIdx].hex);
        return;
      }
      case 'prev-city': {
        const cities = this._myCities();
        if (!cities.length) return;
        this._cityIdx = (this._cityIdx - 1 + cities.length) % cities.length;
        const tile = this.world.map.getByKey(cities[this._cityIdx].tileKey);
        if (tile) this._centerOnHex(tile.hex);
        return;
      }
    }
  }
}

📄 ui\components\RecruitModal.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { CardFactory } from '../../domain/card/CardFactory.js';
import { CardImageLoader } from '../../infrastructure/three/visuals/CardImageLoader.js';
import { iconImg } from '../../config/icons.js';
import { CardDetailView } from './CardDetailView.js';
export class RecruitModal {
  constructor(root, { bus }) {
    this.bus = bus;
    this.overlay = document.createElement('div');
    this.overlay.className = 'recruit-modal-overlay';
    root.appendChild(this.overlay);
    this.el = document.createElement('div');
    this.el.className = 'recruit-modal';
    this.overlay.appendChild(this.el);
    this._imgLoader = new CardImageLoader();
    this._world = null;
    this._general = null;
    this._factionId = null;
    this.overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === this.overlay) this.hide();
    });
    this.el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const close = e.target.getAttribute?.('data-close');
      if (close !== null) { this.hide(); return; }
      const key = e.target.closest?.('[data-recruit]')?.getAttribute('data-recruit');
      if (key) this._tryRecruit(key);
    });
  }
  open({ factionId, general, world }) {
    this._factionId = factionId;
    this._general = general;
    this._world = world;
    this.overlay.classList.add('visible');
    this._render();
  }
  hide() {
    this.overlay.classList.remove('visible');
    this._general = null;
  }
  _tryRecruit(cardKey) {
    if (!this._general || !this._world) return;
    const result = this._world.recruit.recruit({
      factionId: this._factionId,
      cardKey,
      generalId: this._general.id,
    });
    if (!result.ok) {
      this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: result.reason });
      return;
    }
    this.bus.emit('resources:changed', { factionId: this._factionId });
    this.bus.emit('deck:changed', { general: this._general });
    this.bus.emit('toast:show', { text: `${result.card.name} нанят` });
    this._render();
  }
  _render() {
    const world = this._world;
    const gen = this._general;
    const factionId = this._factionId;
    if (!world || !gen) return;
    const pool = world.getRecruitPool(factionId);
    const used = world.recruitPointsUsed(factionId);
    const total = world.economy.recruitPointsForFaction(world, factionId);
    const poolRes = world.resourcePools.get(factionId);
    const poolData = world.recruitPools?.get(factionId);
    const pointsLeft = poolData?.points ?? 0;
    const cards = pool.map(({ key }) => {
      const def = CardFactory.defForKey(key);
      const card = CardFactory.create(key);
      const cost = world.economy.recruitCost(card);
      const pts = world.economy.recruitPointsFor(card);
      const canAfford = poolRes.canAfford(cost);
      const canPoints = used + pts <= total;
      const disabled = !canAfford || !canPoints;
      const detailHtml = CardDetailView.html(card, { size: 'medium' });
      return `
        <div class="rm-card-wrap ${disabled ? 'disabled' : ''}"
             ${disabled ? '' : `data-recruit="${key}"`}>
          ${detailHtml}
          <div class="rm-card-cost">
            ${iconImg('res_weapons', { size: 11 })}${cost.weapons}
            ${iconImg('res_gold',    { size: 11 })}${cost.gold}
            · ${pts} очк.
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
    const empty = pool.length === 0
      ? '<div class="rm-empty">Пул пуст. Завершите ход — придут новые карты.</div>'
      : '';
    this.el.innerHTML = `
      <h2>Найм карт · ${gen.name}</h2>
      <div class="rm-subtitle">
        Очки пула: <b>${pointsLeft}</b> · Очки найма: ${used}/${total}
        · ${iconImg('res_gold')}${poolRes.gold}
        · ${iconImg('res_weapons')}${poolRes.weapons}
        · В пуле: ${pool.length}
      </div>
      ${empty}
      <div class="rm-grid">${cards}</div>
      <button class="rm-close" data-close="1">Закрыть</button>
    `;
    // ─── Картинки ───
    CardDetailView.applyImages(this.el, this._imgLoader);
  }
}
